(F's absolute level differs from 7.26's 5.90: other load on the machine at the time; the deltas are what is comparable.) GPU unchanged (3.1 - 3.3 ms in all arms, noise). The `-CpuProfile` after-run (prof2: F 6.61 / S 7.09 / A 7.13 ms interval) agrees: S - F +0.48 under the profiler (was +0.74).

**5. What is left** (profile of the after build, S - F +0.32 .. +0.48 ms; inclusive, overlapping): `setOwnedFor` 0.144 (its children: the material window fill + mutex, `scenetable::find`, the view block compare, the lighting window), `writeLighting` 0.125 (its register part also runs in F: below the sampler's threshold there), `FrameTap::beginView` 0.112 + `ownedPass` 0.082 + `passBegin` (the owned path: compaction stamps 28 us, per-draw signature / windows / observers ~100 us per view), `checkMaterial` 0.090 (the real once-per-slot polls: ~60 `refresh` per frame), table mutex pairs ~0.045. A adds trees + billboards: +0.12 .. 0.15 over S as before (A - S).
* **Not done, with the reason:** (a) the persistent draws' per-draw `ownedDrawSig` (buffer bindings via `Buffer::gpu()`, texture ready states, window bases, ambient test, pack word: ~1 us per owned draw) is still evaluated on every frame: making it dirty-driven needs change counters on `Buffer` bindings / texture ready states (many mutation sites) or a global resource epoch that streaming moves constantly; the check frames + rolling scrub (MISS) are its net today; a wrong counter would be a silent picture error between check frames, so it was not attempted blind. (b) the material / illumination polls: no engine-side change signal exists (only the switch-write and `UpdateIllumination` hooks, already used); the poll IS the read. (c) the table mutexes are engine-thread-only in practice but guard the 5 s report / flush paths: left. (d) per-op `recStamp` (guarded engine reads, ~0.45 us per op) is half of the compaction; it needs engine signals (F2c-5).
* **Open:** the static op records' read-log audit reported `1 read log disagreements` (class "other": a 4-byte word at an unrecorded engine address, 0x0 -> 0x100000006) once in each of three later runs (a walk, the verify-all walk and a spots run); the stampfix soak of 7.21 had 0 in 6 walks. The audit rebuilds the record, DIFFERING stays 0. **Control: the same spots run with fc7b370's DLL (before this stage) shows it in 2 of 3 runs** (`1 read log disagreements ... (other 1)`, DIFFERING 0): pre-existing, not caused by the verification cut (the audit runs every frame as before); its source is not identified (a first-sight engine word at an address no anchor covers, early in a spot / walk).

### 7.28 F2d: wider owned static scope (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-ad14201a3230beba6` on main d5c6fb7; no new setting)

Goal: after 7.27 the chain costs S - F = +0.32 ms; the fixed cost is mostly paid, so own more of the 61 % of the main view's static z / solid ops that were still composed every frame. **Result: hierarchy meshes and their node ops are owned (owned share 39.2 % -> 63.3 % of all ops looked at, 76 % of the ops that can be owned at all), correct (DIFFERING 0, MISS 0, GPU-assisted validation clean), but the CPU gain is inside the noise: S 5.96 -> 5.96 ms, A 6.03 -> 5.93 ms (-0.10). The net saving of an owned op is only ~2 us; the chain's remaining +0.3 ms is its fixed cost, not the unowned ops.** Instance lists and hardware batches were measured and NOT owned (below).

**1. Which classes are excluded, and what their composition costs.** `RVK_F2D_MEASURE=1` (new, diagnostics): the wall time from an op's entry to the next op's entry (composition + issue) summed per exclusion class (`native: f2d measure (5 s)`; arm T = records on, nothing owned, hierarchy excluded; savegame `Risen_Save_0005`, lod 2x, hold 60, ~160 fps, so 5 s = 800 frames):

| class (first reason) | ops / 5 s | per frame | composed us / op | CPU ms / frame | share of static compose CPU | ownable? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| eligible (owned since 7.21) | 60,000 | 75 | 4.3 | 0.33 | 35 % | yes (already) |
| hierarchy (ctx +0x34 > 0: landscape / harbour / monastery shells, their node ops) | 63,000 | 79 | 4.5 | 0.37 | 39 % | **66 %** (the rest = blended terrain layers, below) |
| instance list (node 0xffffff, one render instance per op) | 20,000 | 25 | 4.3 | 0.11 | 12 % | needs an object record per instance (not built) |
| hardware batch (render instances, >= 2, no lightmaps) | 10,000 | 12 | 10.2 | 0.13 | 14 % | needs c32 + 4 i instance rows (not built) |

Hierarchy was the heaviest class by far (7.12's census order stands). Of its 63 k ops / 5 s, **21.8 k are the landscape's blended terrain layers**: default blend mode with an opacity node and `TradeAlphaAsSolid` (`sc::isBlended` is false for them, `fillBlending` still draws src 5 / dst 6 with the depth write on), solid list, blended draws = order dependent: they stay composed (`blended / no z write draw`, the new floor: 17 % of all ops). The old `hierarchy` reason had hidden them; owning hierarchy first made every frame compose them with a read log and drop the record afterwards (first A/B run: no CPU gain at all, `no record 21,352` per 5 s): now they are asked for **before** the composition (`sc::blendsAtFadeOne`, a pure function equal to `fillBlending` at fade 1, test `static_blend_prediction_matches_the_states` over every mode x flag combination; a diagnostics line `owned records: ... op refused after its composition` names an op that still slips through: 0 seen).

**2. What was built.** `orec::hierarchyOwned()` (op_records.h; `RVK_F2D_NO_HIERARCHY=1` restores the old exclusion = the A/B arm): `recExclusion` and `ownedSelect` no longer refuse `ctx +0x34 > 0` / node ops. The one new dependency is the node's index range: `subMeshDraws` draws `[first, end)` of the node table at `sub +0x40` (0x28-byte nodes, `+0x20` = first index, the pointer at `+0` = the next node), so `recStamp` mixes node pointer + first + end into the geometry stamp of a node op (the whole-mesh z op and sub-mesh ops are already covered by the sub-mesh loop). Everything else rides the existing machinery unchanged: the OpRecord / DrawRec per (ctx, desc, list), the scene-table object slot (a hierarchy entity is an ordinary entity: `entity +0x60` matrix), the persistent DrawRecords, check-frame derive + compare (the compare's `Geometry` field covers first / count, so a stale node range would show as DIFFERING), `ownedMissCheck`, the illumination slot / loading-resource watch of the 7.21 addendum. No new draw path: `rvk_d3d9test` has no new case (the "owned persistent (F2c-4a)" case covers the replay of a record; a node op is the same record with another index range).

**3. Not built, and why** (an architectural question for the orchestrator):
* *Instance-list ops* (20 k ops / 5 s, 0.11 ms): the op has an entity (`ctx +0x10`) but its draw takes matrix / switch / illumination / flags from the resource's own 0x58-byte render-instance record (`res +0xb0`), so the object table (one record per scene-table slot, validated by the scene table's matrix signals) cannot serve it, `recStamp` reads the illumination from the entity (+0xf8) instead of the instance (+0x48), and the engine's `leanSortInstances` side effect would have to be reproduced on owned frames. It needs an object-record space keyed (resource, instance index) with the matrix in the stamp (88 bytes read per op per frame) and `recStamp` / `ownedDrawOp` / `setOwnedFor` / the RT observer rows taught the second source. Estimated gain at 2 us net per op: ~0.05 ms.
* *Hardware batches* (10 k ops / 5 s, 0.13 ms, the most expensive per op): the shader reads instance rows through `c32 + 4 i`, so a batch is one DrawRecord whose per-instance block (up to 50 x 4 float4) is rebuilt when any instance's matrix / illumination changes: a per-batch row image like owned trees' rows ring block, the batch split rule (`sc::planBatches` depends on the instances' light lists), and a stamp over every instance. Estimated gain at 10 us per composed op minus the owned cost: ~0.06 ms.
* *Blended terrain layers* (21.8 k ops / 5 s, ~0.13 ms): order-dependent blended draws; they would need an owned blended bucket drawn at the engine's sort position (F9 terrain layers of the PLAN).
With the measured gain per owned op (S1 vs S0: +0.00 for +31 k owned ops per 5 s = 39 per frame; A1 vs A0: -0.10) neither remaining class would move the chain by more than ~0.1 ms; the table / records layer's fixed cost (T - F +0.48) is where the chain is lost, not the unowned ops. Next lever: that fixed cost (7.27 "left"), not a wider scope.

**4. Owned share before / after** (`native: owned static (5 s)`, savegame hold, lod 2x, `ops looked at`):

| arm | ops looked at / 5 s | owned | share | left to the engine path |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S0 / A0 (old scope: `RVK_F2D_NO_HIERARCHY=1`) | 125.8 k | 49.3 k | **39.2 %** | hardware batch 8.3 k, instance list 16.7 k, hierarchy 52.0 k |
| S1 / A1 (this branch) | 127.4 k | 80.6 k | **63.3 %** | hardware batch 8.3 k, instance list 16.7 k, blended layers 21.8 k |

(share of the ops that can be owned, i.e. without the blended layers: 80.6 / (127.4 - 21.8) = 76 %; rvkcompare spots 64 - 67 % on the 5 s windows, the savegame walk 80 % on windows that carry no blended terrain.)

**5. Gates.** `rvk_tests` 296 / 0 (new `static_blend_prediction_matches_the_states`; the settings count stays 135: no new setting, env knobs `RVK_F2D_NO_HIERARCHY`, `RVK_F2D_MEASURE`). `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan and `--backend d3d12`: PASS, 0 failures, 0 validation errors. Game runs in the private copy `risen-test-vk-f2d`, instance 30, lock `rvk-f2d`, Full + the whole chain (the 7.27 soak settings incl. `owned_trees`, `owned_billboards`, `shadows.rt=near`, `rt.table_entries`, `debug.rt_table_compare`, `debug.split_verify`): `-Validation` + `VK_KHRONOS_VALIDATION_VALIDATE_GPU_BASED=GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED`, savegame hold, first with the hierarchy-only build and again with the final one: PASS, 0 VUID, 0 error lines, owned 80 % of the window's ops, `owned records` DIFFERING 0, `owned persistent` MISS 0 (scrub + check frames); 2 `rvk_rtwalk` walks of 200 s (per-draw / bulk alternating): PASS, 0 error lines, DIFFERING 0, MISS 0, owned path never turned off, no audit disagreement; rvkcompare pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks: PASS, owned records DIFFERING 0, MISS 0 in all 120 windows. **Fault injection still produces MISS**: `RVK_F2C4A_INJECT=2` with the 7.27 inject recipe (the nine table / owned settings, same spots): injected 14, scrub MISS 14, check frames MISS 24, `native: owned static turned off: 38 MISS`. (With the larger soak settings the same knob swallowed nothing: no dependency moved in the frozen spots; use the inject recipe.) **Open, not from this change:** rvkcompare runs with `native.owned_trees` log two `native: owned trees: DIFFERING ... (sampler)` errors (a SpeedTree z op and its solid op, `desc 0x4b0000` / `0x0`, the same context) in every spots run, **also with `RVK_F2D_NO_HIERARCHY=1`** (= main's behaviour for static meshes): an owned-trees template / sampler issue in these scenes that 7.27's walks (savegame, no spots run with trees owned) did not exercise. The trees stage reacts (composes late), but the DIFFERING count there is > 0 and needs its own look.

**6. CPU A/B** (`RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, `-PlayerDefaults`, lod 2x, savegame hold 60, shared copy + default lock, 2 interleaved rounds, mean of the steady 5 s `perf:` windows, engine + HAL CPU ms per frame; `0` = `RVK_F2D_NO_HIERARCHY=1` (the old scope, same DLL), `1` = this branch; arms of 7.26: F defaults, S = T + owned_static + owned_persistent + rt.table_entries, A = S + owned_trees + owned_billboards):

| arm | r1 / r2 | mean | fps | vs F | GPU |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| F | 5.61 / 5.68 | 5.64 | 172 | - | 3.10 |
| S0 | 5.96 / 5.96 | 5.96 | 163 | +0.31 | 3.22 |
| S1 | 6.03 / 5.90 | 5.96 | 162 | +0.32 | 3.28 |
| A0 | 5.95 / 6.11 | 6.03 | 160 | +0.39 | 3.24 |

S1 - S0 +0.00 ms, A1 - A0 -0.10 ms (noise of one arm: 0.03 - 0.15 between runs). GPU unchanged within noise. The owned path's own cost (compaction ~55 us + build ~100 us per view for ~100 owned ops, observers) is ~2 us per op against ~4.5 us of composition: a wider scope pays ~2 us per op, and the 39 extra ops per frame are ~0.08 ms before noise.

### 7.29 F2e-0: what an owned draw still costs, and where the engine changes its inputs (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a895065724e27cdef` on main e2df482; measurement + reverse engineering, no code change merged)

Question (orchestrator): after 7.27 / 7.28 an owned static op still costs ~2.5 us of CPU against ~4.5 us composed, the engine still collects the op lists every frame, so what would "per-frame CPU per owned draw -> 0" need? Facts only: (1) the per-op owned cost split, (2) for every validity input the engine code that changes it, (3) the engine's per-frame collection cost. Method: the savegame recipe of 7.26 (`Risen_Save_0005`, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, `-PlayerDefaults`, lod 2x, hold 60), arm S (T + `owned_static`, `owned_persistent`, `rt.table_entries`), shared copy + default lock for every timing run (the old copy path; a foreign `Risen.exe` of another agent's private copy was running at the start of the runs: GPU / CPU contention possible, the timers are relative anyway); the engine RE with headless Ghidra on Engine.dll (the MCP server has only Risen.exe open; RVAs below are Engine.dll RVAs, all decompiled this session). Logs, scripts, dumps: `rvk-work/scratch/f2e/` (`r1` timers + `RVK_F2C4_MEASURE`, `r4` `RVK_OWNED_TIMERS` only, `r5` extra step timers + counting hooks, `r2` / `r3` / `r7` `-CpuProfile` S / F / S, `r6` / `r8` walks with the hooks, `engine_dump*.txt`). Steady windows: 779 main views per 5 s window (~156 fps), 100.8 owned ops per main view on average (126 per owned view; 75 check + 75 warm-up frames per 779 compose), owned 63.3 % of 154 static ops looked at per view (7.28), 1.6 compaction calls per view (z + solid).

#### 1. Per-op owned cost (ns per owned op; r1 unless noted; the stage timers themselves cost ~25 ns per clock pair: 7.27 measured +0.05 .. 0.1 ms per frame, so the plain cost is ~0.4 us below the sums, i.e. **~1.8 us per owned op = ~0.18 ms per frame, 2.2 us with the timers**)

| step | ns / owned op | us / main view | what it reads / does | why it is needed today |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Compaction** (`FrameTap::compactOwned`, once per list, before the engine's sort) | **~700** | 70 | for **every op of the engine's list** (not only the owned ones): `readAt(ctx, 0)` (guarded vtable read) + `vtableInfo` hash lookup, then `ownedSelect`; copy of the raw array, rewrite of the engine's array + count, sort of the owned ops with the engine's comparator, `resolve` (vtable + 4 - 6 guarded reads + `meshState`) per owned op | the engine's `RequestRenderOps` has already appended the ops; they are taken out in place before `Execute` sorts / runs them |
| - `ownedSelect` (the per-op validity check) | 351 | 35 | see the next five rows | the op is drawn from its record only if every stamp holds |
| - - `map.find(Key{ctx, desc, list})` | ~62 | | hash of 3 words, bucket walk (the map is `unordered_map`; ~20 % more finds than owned ops: the unowned ops also look) | find the record |
| - - recStamp | ~172 | | ~35 guarded engine words per op: ctx `+0x28 / +0x10 / +0x18 +0x20`, entity `+0x28 / +0xf8`, illumination `+0x78` + list `+0 / +0x34`, mesh res `+0x38 / +0x58 / +0x60 / +0x88`, `+0x90 / +0x98` lightmaps (each: state `+0x38`, image data `+0x70`), per covered sub-mesh `+0x28 .. +0x34` ranges, material `+0x38 / +0x70`, shader `+0x50 / +0x58 / +0x68 / +0x78 / +0x79 / +0x220 / +0x2a8 / +0x2b8 / +0x2d8 / +0x330 / +0x420` + vtable, node table for hierarchy ops, `scenetable::find` (hash) + slot generation; two `mix` chains | the record is valid only while none of these moved (7.14 stamps) |
| - - firstDifference + recLiveWhy + draws loop | 27 + 24 + 39 | | 14 field compares; `illumtable().slotLive`; `watch` list validation (empty when resident); per draw `r.scene && cd->valid` | stamps equal; illumination slot not reused; pending loads completed; draw retained |
| - - pre-checks (tableLit, `ownable`, hierarchy env) | ~27 | | | |
| - loop over the whole list (all kinds), raw copy, rewrite, sort, resolve | ~345 | 35 | f2c4: per call select-phase 36.8 us (of which `ownedSelect` 22 us per call), rewrite 0.5, sort 3.5, resolve 3.0 | see above |
| `ownedPass` setup (view table slot, mirror, save / restore of the register file, `ownedBegin`, reserve) | 30 | 3 | per pass | persistent draws need the pass's env epoch + reserved ring |
| Material poll + structure stamp (`ownedMaterialsOk`) | 161 | 16 | per op: its `matSlots`; `MaterialTable::pollOwned` once per slot and frame (~60 slots), `structVersion` + hash per slot | the pool bytes / sampler images / ready states of its materials are only readable by polling (no engine event for the pool, section 2) |
| Per-op persistent loop (`ownedDrawOpPersistent`; **1240 incl. the rows below**) | **1240** | 125 | | |
| - object record (`scenetable::find` + `serveFast`: generation / version / flags / fade compare) | ~90 (r5) | 9 | scene-table hash lookup, shadow record compare | the draw's VS window 2 = the object record of the entity |
| - windows (`ownedAmbientLit` memo, `ownedBuildWindows`, shader / tag setup per draw) | 117 | 12 | per draw: view / material / object / lighting windows from the per-pass memos | the draw's 8 window bases |
| - `Device::ownedDrawSig` | 102 | 10 | per draw: replay allowance, per stream / index `Buffer::gpu()` binding, per sampled texture `isNull / pending_ / layout / bindlessSlot`, pack word, 4 window bases | the persistent slot is reused only while this hash and the pass env epoch are unchanged |
| - derive (rolling scrub 6.2 % of draws + first / changed derivations) | 86 | 9 | `ownedDerive` + compare | MISS net |
| - observers per draw (`ownedObserve`) + bulk observers per pass (`observeOwned`: RT scene) | 99 + 200 | 10 + 20 | RT registry `see()` per record, instance keys, table-entry stamps | the RT scene must see every record every frame (7.23) |
| - `ownedFlush` (ref list + indirect args per bucket) | 95 | 10 | ~33 k indirect packets / 5 s | the draws leave as indirect runs |
| - the rest (per-op `recs_->map.find` again, 3 `Tag` copies per draw, item / op index vectors, 1 late-composed op per frame, the timers) | ~450 | 45 | | loop overhead (about half is the stage timers) |
| Side effects (`ownedSideEffects`: touch the images / materials, BindLightmaps, stream 3) | 66 | 7 | 0.7 touches + 0.1 lightmap binds + 0.86 stream-3 switches per op (guarded engine calls) | what the engine's slot would do (LRU, lightmap binding) |
| **Total** | **2,190** | **221** | | composed: ~4,500 |

`-CpuProfile` (r7, S, interval 7.07 ms, inclusive ms / frame): `FrameTap::beginView` 0.100 (compaction) + `passBegin` / `ownedPass` 0.145 = **0.245 ms = 2.4 us per owned op** with the profiler (the profile cannot split below ~0.1 ms: its samples are 0.5 ms); `setOwnedFor` 0.140 + `writeLighting` 0.108 are the composed ops' (the 37 % of the ops not owned). So the timer split is the usable one; the profile agrees with the total. Observations on the numbers: (a) **the validity checks are ~30 % of the owned cost** (stamp 172 + diff / live / scene 90 + materials 161 + sig 102 + object 90 = ~620 ns), the other 70 % is hand-over: compaction loop + sort + resolve (345), observers (300), windows (117), flush (95), side effects (66), loop (450 with timers). (b) **One op per frame is composed late and never becomes owned**: `replay refusals: texture 603` per 5 s = 0.97 per owned view (a texture of that op is never in `SHADER_READ_ONLY` / is pending): its `composeLate` costs ~5 - 10 us per view (inside the loop row). (c) A *composed* op's 4.5 us has the same inputs; the owned path saves the composition, not the checks.

#### 2. Validity inputs and where the engine changes them (all RVAs Engine.dll, decompiled)

Frame structure (read from the code): `eCFrustumDatabase::Request` 0x66e8c0 -> `RequestRenderOps(db, mask)` 0x66cfe0: for **every element of the visible list** (`db +0x18` array, `+0x20` count; the list `ExtractFromFrustum` 0x66fa60 built this frame) `eCFrustumElementItem::CalculateFadeOut` 0x671220 (distance / view range -> fade) and, when fade > 0, `eCFrustumElementItem::Request` 0x671370: `eCIlluminated_PS::UpdateIllumination` 0x595300 if the element has an illumination PS (it returns at once unless the dirty byte `+0x188` is set), then per context `vt+0x30 UpdateLoD` and `vt+0x40 RequestRenderOps(db, fade, mask)`. `eCRenderContextMesh::RequestRenderOps` 0x5c75e0: QueueData / CacheResource (LRU) of the mesh resource and of each sub-mesh material, busy counter `res +0x24`, per sub-mesh `eCRenderOp2::Create` (0x67f680) into `db +0x14e8 / +0x1500 / +0x1518 / +0x1578` (z / solid / alpha / refracted lists), hierarchy meshes (`ctx +0x34 > 0`) cull their nodes against the camera per frame (`CullCamera` on the node boxes at the node table `sub +0x40`, 0x28-byte nodes), render-instance meshes (`res +0xa0`) `AddRenderInstance` 0x7602a0.

| input (stamp field / sig part) | what moves it in the engine | function (RVA) | single choke point? | tiny / COMDAT? | rate measured (savegame still / walking 20 s / loading burst, per 5 s unless noted) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ctx +0x10 / +0x18 / +0x34` (entity, mesh PS, hierarchy level), initial `+0x28` | context creation from the mesh PS properties | `eCRenderContextMesh::Create` 0x5c7360 (called from `eCMesh_PS::CreateRenderContext`); `Invalidate` 0x5c72e0 zeroes all four (tiny, 1 caller) | yes | `Invalidate` = 4 stores: do not detour | with cache-in: 17 / s walking |
| **LOD resource `ctx +0x28`** | recomputed every frame for multi-LOD meshes: squared camera distance vs the mesh PS thresholds (`PS +0x60 + 4 i`), picks the highest resident LOD, prefetches the wanted one | `eCRenderContextMesh::UpdateLoD` 0x5c74b0 (virtual slot `+0x30`, called from `eCFrustumElementItem::Request` per visible context with caps bit 0) | yes (this function writes it; a post-hook compares before / after: **tested**) | no (~50 instructions) | **called 134 k per 5 s = 173 per frame (the hook costs about what the poll costs)**; changed 0 still, **2.4 per s walking** (47 in 20 s; 449 / 480 per 5 s in the loading burst) |
| resource state `res +0x38` (0 failed, 1 unloaded, 2 loading, 3 resident) of mesh / material / image resources | cache `LoadData(res, file)` sets 3 after the class's `OnLoadData` (virtual `+0x148`) succeeded (0 on failure); `QueueData` mode 1 sets 2 (or 0: file missing), mode 0 loads at once; `FlushResource` sets 1 and calls the class's `OnUnloadData` (virtual `+0x160`); `LoadResources` initial population sets 1 | `eCResourceCache2::LoadData(res, file)` **0x684e90** (the `bCString` overload 0x685180 calls it), `QueueData` 0x6852d0, `FlushResource` 0x684340 (callers `FlushBytes`, `ReloadResource`, `FlushCaches`), `LoadResources` 0x686690 | **yes: every transition of every resource class passes these four (no other writer found in the callers of `eCResource2::LoadData` 0x6828f0)** | no (large / ~30 instructions) | **still 0 - 1 per 5 s, flushes 0; walking 3.5 per s (mesh 0.9, image 1.0, material 0.5); loading 1,800 per 5 s (321 mesh / 416 image / 178 material / 876 other); `FlushResource` 0 - 2** (the soft limit is not reached at the savegame) |
| image data `res +0x70`, mesh sub-mesh table `+0x58 / +0x60`, mesh data `+0x88`, material shader `+0x70` | set in the class `OnLoadData` (`eCImageResource2` 0x68b8f0 `+0x70` only on success; `eCMeshResource2` 0x75dfd0; `eCMaterialResource2` 0x68e480), cleared by `OnUnloadData` (0x68b840, 0x75dd80, 0x68e3f0) | both run inside the `LoadData` / `FlushResource` above | covered by the previous row | - | same |
| material shader pointer `mat +0x70` at run time | `eCMaterialResource2::SetShader` 0x68f620 (besides load / unload) | 0x68f620 | yes | no (~25 instructions) | **0** in every run (still, walking, loading burst; the load path itself does not go through this call: not seen) |
| shader object fields (blend mode `+0x50`, flags `+0x58 / +0x78 / +0x79 / +0x2a8 / +0x2b8 / +0x2d8 / +0x330 / +0x420`, class vtable, admin `+0x220`) | property values of the shader class: read at material load, edited by property setters / scripts | no single function (property notification machinery: `bCObjectBase::OnNotifyPropertyValueChanged...`) | **no** | - | 0 changes in all runs (7.12: material sampler images / ready 5 per 578 k) |
| lightmap images `res +0x90 / +0x98` | resolved by name from the mesh resource's `LightmapName` property (`<name>_1.dds`, `_2`): `OnLightmapChanged` 0x75f700 (from `OnNotifyPropertyValueChangedExitEx` at resource creation / property edit); `ClearLightmaps` 0x75f8e0; image states by the `LoadData` row; `BindLightmaps` 0x764220 per op in the slot (LRU touch + `SetLightmap`) | 0x75f700 | yes for the pointers (a property-time event: 1,666 per 5 s at **resource creation (startup)**, 0 afterwards); images via `LoadData` | `ClearLightmaps` = 2 stores, 1 caller: tiny | 0 |
| entity world matrix `entity +0x60` | `OnUpdatedWorldMatrix(now, before)` virtual: `eCDynamicEntity` 0x69e8a0 -> `eCGeometryEntity` 0x6a8d70 (-> the PS listeners) | both already hooked by `engine/scene_table` (the object table's signal) | yes | no | **the scene table counts ~31 signals per frame in the savegame (half of them unchanged matrices: dynamic entities), 3.4 per frame while walking on the beach** |
| entity cache-in / cache-out / delete (slot generation) | `eCGeometryEntity::OnCacheIn` 0x6a9720 (sets entity flag `0x20`, `eCFrustumElementItem::CacheIn`, then `OnEntityCachedIn`) / `OnCacheOut` 0x6a97b0 | `eCPrefetchAdmin::OnEntityCachedIn` 0x696120 / `OnEntityCachedOut` 0x696040 (called per frame from `CacheEntities` 0x6969c0, the cache-in sphere walk), `eCEntityAdmin::NotifyDeleteEntities` 0x695310 | yes (hooked, the scene table) | no | still 0; walking **17 cache-ins + 4 cache-outs per s** (4,938 / 996 per 5 s in the loading burst) |
| entity overlay flags `entity +0x28 & 0xe00000` (burned / frozen / highlighted) | inline bit writes through the reference returned by `eCEntity::GetEntityFlags` 0x852a0 (focus module of Game.dll `gCFocusModule2`, animation freeze, burn effects) | **none** (writers are inline in Game.dll / Engine.dll) | **no** | **0x852a0 is a COMDAT-folded getter shared with many exports: never detour** | 0 changes in all runs (7.12) |
| illumination PS pointer `entity +0xf8`, illumination record / light list `+0x78` | PS creation `eCFrustumElementItem::SetIlluminationPS` 0x6714b0 (1 store, 1 caller); `eCIlluminated_PS::UpdateIllumination` 0x595300 rewrites the record (static lights `FindWightedStaticLights`, up to 2 sets, sun bit) when the dirty byte `+0x188` is set, **and runs from `eCFrustumElementItem::Request` per visible element** | dirty set by `Invalidate` 0x5952d0 (4 stores, 1 caller, static-light changes), `eCIlluminated_PS::OnUpdatedWorldMatrix` 0x595d00 (**1 store: tiny, never detour**), `OnCacheIn` 0x595cf0 (**1 store: tiny**); the rewrite is observable at the **post-hook of 0x595300** (already hooked: `illumcensus`) | yes for the rewrite (0x595300), no for the dirtying (tiny virtuals) | see left | `Invalidate` 0 in all runs; record bytes changed 0 - 1 per 649 k (still) / **0.1 per view (monastery light turn)** |
| light set (sun bit, `list +0 / +0x34`) | `SetLightSet` 0x5972e0 (from `UpdateIllumination` via `QueryLightSet`), `eCLightSet::UpdateIllumination` 0x579c70 (bool changed; once per frame per set from `UpdateLightSets` 0x575130) | hooked (`lights` / `illumcensus`) | yes | no | 7.12: sun bit 2 changes per 4,931 |
| switch value `ctx +0x18 +0x20` | property of the mesh PS (scripts / animations) | no choke found | no | - | 0 |
| **fade** (op's `fadeBits`) | computed per element per frame from the camera distance and the entity view range `entity +0xcc` (`CalculateFadeOut` 0x671220: fade 1 inside 80 % of the range, linear to 0) | a per-frame engine decision, not a state | **no (not an event)** | - | `fade < 1` is excluded today (0 of the owned ops); the far-fade zone is the only place it varies |
| visibility / membership (the op list itself) | `ExtractFromFrustum` 0x66fa60 (`CullTree` 0x6711a0 per element: entity flag `0x10000`, box `entity +0xa0`, sphere `+0xb8`, view range `+0xcc`; `OnQueryNodes` 0x66d960 over the sector's beam tree) + per-op caps | per-frame engine decision | **no** | - | every frame |
| our side: texture ready (`pending_`, `layout`, `bindlessSlot`) and buffer binding (`Buffer::gpu`) in `ownedDrawSig`; `ownedEnvHash` (viewport, targets, jitter, depth remap, sampler rows, pack stamp, view table base); `variantEpoch`, `registryGen`, view stamp, material structure versions | our HAL / settings code | `d3d9::Texture` upload completion + `Image::transition`, `Buffer` / `MeshArena` binding changes, the settings setters, `viewStampOf` (admin fields per pass), `MaterialTable::pollOwned` | **yes in our own code** (one writer per field: events need no engine hook) except the material pool / structure (a poll: the pool is engine memory without a signal) | - | texture uploads 0 KiB per frame still; env epoch changes 0 per 5 s |

(`F2E count` / `F2E db` lines of the counting hooks: `resource LoadData / FlushResource / QueueData / SetShader / OnLightmapChanged / illumination Invalidate / UpdateLoD / Request` per 5 s; the walk was the `rvk_rtwalk` hero on a new game's beach, then standing: the hero moved for ~20 s of the 100 s; walking rates above are from those windows, the 7.12 census has the longer walks: 12 LOD changes per 1.14 M context-frames.)

#### 3. The engine's per-frame collection of the main view (what owning ops does not remove)

Counting hooks (`eCFrustumDatabase::Request` 0x66e8c0, `ExtractFromFrustum` 0x66fa60, QPC around the original, one main-view db): **savegame (harbour, 339 visible elements per frame): `ExtractFromFrustum` 220 us + `Request` 127 us = 0.35 ms per frame** (profile r7, inclusive: 0.26 + 0.17 incl. the hook's own cost); **new game beach (897 elements): 495 + 227 us = 0.72 ms per frame**, i.e. ~0.8 us per visible element (extract 0.55, request 0.25), independent of how many ops are owned. In `-CpuProfile` terms (r3, F arm): `OnQueryNodes` 0.24, `RequestRenderOps` 0.24 (main + shadow views), `Request` 0.21, `ExtractFromFrustum` 0.15, `CullCamera` 0.14, `eCBeamTree::Cull` self 0.13, op-list sorts (`fastsort`) 0.09 - 0.12, `ExtractShadowMap` 0.10: ~0.75 ms (7.26 / performance.md 33 said the same). `UpdateLoD` is called 173 times per frame at the savegame (134 k per 5 s; 190 - 270 k per 5 s at the beach), the illumination update is called per element but returns at once unless dirty (0 rewrites per 5 s).

**Could static entities be enumerated once at cache-in?** Mostly yes for what the culling needs: the scene table already holds a slot per cached-in entity (2,137 live at the savegame, 4,000 at the beach) and the world matrix signal; the culling inputs are entity-level (`+0xa0` box, `+0xb8` sphere, `+0xcc` view range, flag `0x10000`), refreshed by `OnUpdatedWorldMatrix` / `eCGeometryEntity::OnUpdateBoundary` 0x6a6d70 / `OnUpdatedWorldTreeBoundary` 0x6a8e20 (events; not hooked yet), the contexts and sub-mesh sets are fixed by the mesh PS and the resource's sub-mesh table (a `LoadData` event). What the per-frame engine path additionally decides, and a GPU cull would have to reproduce: the fade (distance), the LOD pick (thresholds at `PS +0x60`, with the "highest resident" fallback and the prefetch), the per-pass caps mask from the material (`uVar20` of 0x5c75e0: blend / refracted / z-pass bits from the shader class), the per-node camera cull of hierarchy meshes (node boxes), render-instance batching, and the **residency side effects of `RequestRenderOps`** (QueueData / CacheResource of the mesh and every material each frame, `res +0x24`): those need the visible set back on the CPU (F10: GPU cull + read-back, or a CPU visibility list refreshed from the GPU).

#### 4. View: which inputs can be event-driven safely, which cannot

* **Safe as events (single writer, observable, rare; every one stays covered by the check frames / rolling scrub as the net):** (1) every resource state / data pointer change (mesh, material, image, lightmap images): `eCResourceCache2::LoadData` 0x684e90 and `FlushResource` 0x684340 (+ `QueueData` 0x6852d0 for state 2 / 0) are the choke points; a version counter per resource or, better, an **inverted index (resource -> records that registered it)** invalidates exactly the dependent records; (2) entity cache-in / out / delete / matrix: already event-fed (scene table), the record's `objGen` / matrix stamp becomes a push; (3) illumination: the post-hook of `UpdateIllumination` 0x595300 (and the light-set hooks) when the bytes changed; (4) lightmap name change: `OnLightmapChanged` 0x75f700; (5) everything on **our side** (texture ready / `bindlessSlot`, buffer binding, env epoch pieces, variant epoch, registry generation, view stamp): one writer each in our code, so events are free of engine risk (the 7.27 objection "many mutation sites / a global epoch moves constantly" is answered by an inverted index per texture / buffer instead of one global epoch). Together these replace roughly the **620 ns of validity checks per op** (recStamp, diffs, materials, sig, object) with pushes at ~1 event per 5 s still / a few per second moving.
* **Cannot become events without owning more of the frame:** (a) the **LOD pick** (`UpdateLoD` is a per-frame distance decision; the post-hook is an event source but the engine calls it per context per frame, so it saves nothing until the engine stops iterating the element), (b) **fade** and **visibility / the op list itself** (per-frame decisions of `CalculateFadeOut`, `CullTree`, the beam tree, the node cull), (c) the **residency side effects** (they need the visible set every frame), (d) **entity overlay flags** (inline writers, the getter 0x852a0 is a folded export: poll once per entity, not per op, or RE the Game.dll focus writer), (e) the **shader object's property fields** and the **switch value** (no choke; a per-material / per-PS memo poll once per frame instead of per op: ~60 materials per frame against 1,500 ops), (f) the **material pool / structure** (engine memory, no signal: stays a per-slot poll), (g) the observers' per-frame view of every record (the RT registry ages out instances it does not see).
* **What that leaves per op even with perfect events:** the compaction loop (345) + observers (300) + flush (95) + side effects (66) ~ 0.8 us, i.e. event-driven validity alone takes the owned op from ~1.8 to ~1.2 us (of 4.5 composed). To approach zero the engine must stop creating and iterating the ops: a detour of `eCRenderContextMesh::RequestRenderOps` 0x5c75e0 for contexts with a valid owned record (replaying only its residency side effects: QueueData / CacheResource of the mesh and the sub-mesh materials, `res +0x24`, the render-instance append) removes the compaction loop, the sort and `map.find` per op (the key becomes a field of the context), and a persistent **visible list** (rebuilt from `ExtractFromFrustum`'s element list, 339 - 900 elements) hands the observers / flush one bulk record set instead of per-op calls; the engine's own collection (0.35 - 0.72 ms per frame, per element) only goes with GPU culling (F10).

Not done / limits: one scene for the per-op split (the savegame hold), the walking rates come from a short walk (the hero stopped after ~20 s), the profile could not split below ~0.1 ms (the step timers did), timer overhead inflates every row by ~25 ns per clock pair (the rows are an upper bound; their shares are what counts), the `UpdateLoD` / `Request` / `ExtractFromFrustum` hooks and the extra `ownedSelect` timers were temporary and are not merged (patch in `rvk-work/scratch/f2e/f2e0_hooks.patch`). No setting, no behaviour change.

### 7.30 F2e-1: event-driven validity of the op records (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a245b705bbf1276cc` on main 67dc4c5; no new setting; step A built, step B not built)

Order (orchestrator): make an owned static op cost close to nothing per frame: **A** event-driven validity (inverted index object -> records, engine events mark dirty, non-check frames skip the stamp), **B** detour `eCRenderContextMesh::RequestRenderOps` so owned ops are never created in the engine's lists. **Result: A is built, correct (EVENT MISS 0, MISS 0, DIFFERING 0, validation clean) and saves only ~0.01 ms per frame (select 343 -> 263 ns per owned op); arm S stays +0.31 ms over arm F. B was analysed and not built (below): its ceiling is ~0.05 ms and it does not reach half of the owned ops.** The gate "S below F" is not reachable by validity work: the owned path's whole per-op cost is 0.19 ms per frame, the S - F gap (+0.31) is made of other things (7.27 "left").

**1. What was built (step A).**
* `src/engine/dep_events.*`: post-hooks (bound by decorated name, `engine/symbols.h`; installed from `bootstrap.cpp` only while `native.owned_static` is on at start; `RVK_F2E_NO_EVENTS=1` (Win32 environment) leaves them out = the polled behaviour, the A/B arm) on `eCResourceCache2::LoadData` 0x684e90 (the string overload 0x685180 calls it), `QueueData` 0x6852d0 (**queued only when the resource's state word `+0x38` changed**: the engine calls it per op and frame for a resource that is not resident, 245 k calls per minute at the savegame, all no-ops), `FlushResource` 0x684340, `eCMeshResource2::OnLightmapChanged` 0x75f700, `eCMaterialResource2::SetShader` 0x68f620. All five are unique exports (none COMDAT-folded, none tiny). The hooks push the object into a lock-protected queue (they may run on a loader thread: the lock is taken per event, ~1,950 events at a new game's load, 0 - 2 per 5 s afterwards); `StaticOps::depDrain` drains it at the start of every main view (`ownedViewBegin`, before any stamp is read), deduplicated.
* `orec::DepIndex` (op_records.h): object -> records, engine thread only; a record registers at its build (`StaticOps::depRegister`, static_ops_deps.cpp): the LOD resource `ctx +0x28` (covers the sub-mesh table, the mesh data, the node table of hierarchy ops), every covered sub-mesh's **material** (state, its shader pointer), the **lightmap images** `res +0x90 / +0x98`; `~OpRecord` unregisters it. An event marks `OpRecord::dirty`. Unit test `dep_index_marks_dependents_and_forgets_destroyed_records`.
* `StaticOps::depFast` (the new first step of `ownedSelect`): a record that is not marked is valid on its **polled** inputs alone: `ctx +0x28` (the LOD pick), `ctx +0x10`, the entity's overlay flags `+0x28 & 0xe00000`, the illumination (the PS pointer + the 6 words of `recStamp`'s `light`), the op's fade, the switch value, the scene-table slot (a direct index: entity + generation, no hash), the variant epoch / registry generation, and the **covered shaders' property fields** as a fingerprint (`shaderFp`: class vtable, blend mode, flags, sort id, depth bias, admin; memoised per shader and frame; a change marks the record and leaves the op to the engine). A marked record (or `native.owned_static` without the hooks) runs the full `recStamp` + `firstDifference` as before and is clean again when it holds.
* **The nets.** (1) The check frames: `recEnter` compares the full stamp of every composed record; a difference in the event-class fields (geometry / material, lightmaps) of a record that no event marked and whose shaders did not change is **`EVENT MISS`** (error log + counter + the record marked; the polled-class fields differing is the normal rebuild path). (2) A rolling scrub: 1 in 16 owned ops of a non-check frame runs the full stamp too; any difference there is an EVENT MISS. (3) The read-log audit and `recLiveWhy` (illumination slot, loads that were pending) are unchanged. rvk.log `native: owned events (5 s)`: select fast / slow, scrub compares, shader polls / memo hits, QueueData calls that changed nothing, objects drained, records marked, `EVENT MISS ... (run N)`, the run's engine event counts, index size.

**2. What the measurements changed in the design (design points that proved wrong).**
* **The illumination cannot be an event input.** First build: `UpdateIllumination` 0x595300 (dirty), `SetLightSet` and `eCLightSet::UpdateIllumination`'s "changed" result as events on the PS / set. The first game run showed 15 EVENT MISS (illumination) from the set's list being rewritten without a PS event, and with the set registered **every record was marked every frame** (14,448 marks per 5 s): `eCLightSet::UpdateIllumination` reports "changed" ~every frame (28 per 5 s at ~9 fps, 0.66 per frame) and the light lists (`ptr(illum, 0)`) are shared by hundreds of records. A global "light epoch" had the same problem. The light stamp (6 words) is now **polled** per op; the PS / set hooks were removed again.
* **Events almost never fire for owned records.** Over four 200 s walks (savegame x 3, one new game: loading burst), one `rvkcompare` run and the holds: `records marked` 0 in steady state, `select slow` 0 (the marked path is exercised by the unit test and by the first build's runs only); resource events are 0 - 2 per 5 s still and the ~1,900 of a load happen before any record exists. So validity-by-events is correct but also nearly idle: **its saving is the polling it replaces (recStamp's ~35 reads -> ~12), not event traffic**.

**3. Cost** (savegame `Risen_Save_0005`, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, `-PlayerDefaults`, lod 2x, hold 60, **shared copy + default lock**, F = Full defaults, S = T + `native.owned_static`, `owned_persistent`, `rt.table_entries` (7.26); S0 = the same DLL with `RVK_F2E_NO_EVENTS=1` (the polled path of main 67dc4c5), S1 = events; 2 interleaved rounds, mean of the 11 steady 5 s `perf:` windows; logs / scripts `rvk-work/scratch/f2e1/`):

| arm | r1 / r2 CPU ms per frame | mean | fps | GPU ms |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F | 5.643 / 5.642 | **5.642** | 177 | 3.11 / 3.33 |
| S0 (polled) | 5.993 / 5.970 | **5.981** | 167 | 3.36 / 3.35 |
| S1 (events) | 5.965 / 5.943 | **5.954** | 168 | 3.36 / 3.36 |

S0 - F +0.34 ms, **S1 - F +0.31 ms**, S1 - S0 -0.03 ms (inside the run-to-run noise of 0.02 - 0.03 between runs). The A arm (trees + billboards) was not run: the gate is S against F. Per-op split with `RVK_OWNED_TIMERS=1` (owned records timers, 100.8 owned ops per main view, 154 ops looked at): `ownedSelect` per owned op 343 -> **263 ns** (26.5 us per main view instead of 34.6), compaction 631 -> **555 ns** (55.9 instead of 63.6 us), whole owned path 1,982 -> **1,931 ns** per owned op (timers on). What is left in the select: the `map.find` per op of the list (62 ns, also for the unowned ops), the pre-checks (27), the polled words (guarded reads ~10 ns each) and the 1-in-16 scrub.

**4. Step B: why it was not built.** `eCRenderContextMesh::RequestRenderOps` 0x5c75e0 (decompiled, `rvk-work/scratch/f2e/engine_dump.txt`) does, per context: `QueueData(res, 1)` / `CacheResource(res)` for the mesh and for **every** sub-mesh material, the busy counter `res +0x24` (++ ... --), then per sub-mesh the caps mask from the material shader's virtuals (`+0x198` / `+0x1a8`, together with the view's mask and fade) and the ops: `eCRenderOp2::Create` into the z (`db +0x1500`), solid (`+0x14e8`), alpha (`+0x1578`) and refracted (`+0x1518`) lists, after growing the array with the non-exported `FUN_1800ae210` / `FUN_1800ae1e0`; hierarchy contexts (`ctx +0x34 > 0`) instead **cull each node's box against the camera per frame** (`eCFrustumFilter::CullCamera`, three-way result and a size test) and create ops only for the visible nodes; render-instance meshes (`res +0xa0`) call `AddRenderInstance` instead. Consequences for the design in the order: (1) a "valid record set" does not exist for hierarchy contexts: their op set changes with the camera (the F2d hierarchy ops are 63 k of the 127 k ops per 5 s and 62 % of the owned ones) - only the non-hierarchy static contexts (the other ~38 % of the owned ops) could skip creation; (2) a context's ops live in four lists and a sub-mesh can have an owned z / solid op and an engine-drawn alpha / refracted op from the same call: skipping creation per context needs the function re-implemented per sub-mesh (the caps mask, the three list appends, the sort key `uVar21` = the quantised camera distance) or a per-sub-mesh bypass of `Create`, i.e. a native copy of the 300-line function; (3) the main db is not known at call time (shadow views call the same function with other dbs; the phase tracker learns the main view at Execute), so the detour would have to key on the db pointer of the previous frame; (4) the check frames would run the original and compare the ops it creates with the records (new compare code); (5) **the ceiling**: the compaction (555 ns per owned op) is what B removes for ~38 % of the ops = ~21 us per frame = **~0.02 ms**, plus the engine's own `Create` per op (not measured separately, ~0.1 us?) - against S - F +0.31 ms. The residency side effects (QueueData / CacheResource of the mesh and materials, the `+0x24` counter, `AddRenderInstance`) would have to be re-done per context per frame by us, which is what the engine's call costs anyway. Not built: a ~0.03 ms gain does not pay for a native copy of a function that decides what the world draws, with four engine lists, caps and culling behind it. The visible-list / F10 route (7.29 section 3: GPU culling with a read-back) is the one that removes the engine's collection (0.35 - 0.72 ms per frame) and the ops together.

**5. Where the S - F gap (+0.31 ms) is, from 7.27 / 7.29** (not touched here): `setOwnedFor` / `writeLighting` / `checkMaterial` of the **composed** ops (the 37 % that are not owned, 0.14 + 0.11 ms of the profile), the owned path's setup + persistent loop (materials 161 ns, `ownedDrawSig` 102, object serve 90, windows 117, observers 300, flush 95 per owned op), the tables' mutexes and flushes. The next lever is the persistent loop (the per-op material poll and signature: change counters on our own bindings / texture ready states, 7.27 "not done (a)") and the composed ops' table work, not the validity stamp.

**6. Gates.** `rvk_tests` 297 / 0 (new `dep_index_marks_dependents_and_forgets_destroyed_records`; settings count unchanged, 135: no new setting, env knob `RVK_F2E_NO_EVENTS`). `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan and `--backend d3d12`: PASS, 0 failures, 0 validation errors. Game runs in the private copy `risen-test-vk-f2e`, instance 31, lock `rvk-f2e`, Full + the 7.27 soak settings (the whole chain incl. `owned_trees`, `owned_billboards`, `shadows.rt=near`, `rt.table_entries`, `debug.rt_table_compare`, `debug.split_verify`, lod 2x), `RVK_F2C4_MEASURE=1`: `-Validation` + `VK_KHRONOS_VALIDATION_VALIDATE_GPU_BASED=GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED`, savegame hold: 0 error lines, 0 VUID, no device loss; 4 `rvk_rtwalk` walks of 200 s (3 from the savegame = a quick-load burst each, 1 new game = a full load; per-draw / bulk alternating): PASS, 0 error lines, `owned records` DIFFERING 0, `owned persistent` MISS 0 (scrub + check frames), **EVENT MISS 0**, owned path never turned off; `rvkcompare` pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks: PASS, EVENT MISS 0, MISS 0, DIFFERING 0, owned path never turned off (the two `owned trees: DIFFERING ... (sampler)` errors of 7.28's open item appear as before in every spots run: same context, unchanged by this stage). Not exercised: the marked-record path in a long run (events almost never hit a record, above): a fault-injection knob for dropped events would find nothing for the same reason; the mechanism is covered by the unit test and the check-frame / scrub nets.

### 7.31 F10-0: the main view's collection done natively, compare mode (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a7bc2b308bf3edcc4` on main c93946a; no setting, env `RVK_F10_COMPARE=1`; no takeover)

Built: `src/native/f10_math.h` (pure: beam-tree box / sphere cull, frustum-sphere + distance + camera cull, entity flag gate, `CalculateFadeOut`, `UpdateLoD` pick, hierarchy node walk, request mask; 8 tests `f10_*` in `tests/f10_tests.cpp`) and `src/native/f10_collect.*` (a persistent scene of one cache entry per scene-table slot, refreshed when `scenetable::stamp` changes + a rolling scrub of live reads; hooks on `eCFrustumDatabase::Request` 0x66e8c0, `ExtractFromFrustum` 0x66fa60 (timing) and the sector tree's walker 0x15c4a0 (captures the query's leaf mask / value per database); after each engine `Request` of a view with >= 16 elements the same view is collected natively and compared; logs `native: f10 collect compare (5 s)` / `class` / `diff` / `causes` / `cost` / `side effects`). `RVK_F10_EXP` bit 1 = an experiment (room path also applies CullNode: no change). Off = no hook, no memory.

**How the engine collects the main view (decompiled, Engine.dll RVAs; dumps in `rvk-work/scratch/f10/*dump*.txt`).** `Extract` 0x66f8d0 -> `ExtractFromFrustum` 0x66fa60: (1) in portal mode (`db[0x15a3]` = 1: **always on for the main view**) it finds the camera's portal room (`FindPortalRoom`) and walks `ExtractFromPortalRooms` 0x66db40 / `ExtractFromPortalRoom` 0x66daa0 through the portals with clipped beam trees (`eCBeamTree::AddPoly`): each room entity's box (vtable +0x260) against the room's beam, then `ExtractFromEntity(.., status 1)` = **no CullNode** (no view-range cull); (2) then it queries the **sector tree** (`FUN_18015c4a0`, nodes of 0x58 bytes: sphere, entity, flags `+0x18`, child `+0x40`, sibling `+0x30`, leaf bit `+0x50 & 4`) with `OnQueryNodes` 0x66d960: interior node = `CullCamera(sphere)` (1 = the whole subtree is extracted unculled via `FUN_18066da10`, 2 = descend), leaf = `node flags & mask == value` (**mask `0x7`, value `0x1` in portal mode** = `db+0x15b0 | 6`, `db+0x15ac`: leaves flagged 2 / 4 are the portal rooms' entities, reached only through the room walk), then `CullCamera(sphere)`, then `ExtractFromEntity`: flags `entity+0x28 & 0x8026 == 0x8026`, `& 8 == 0`, contexts at `+0xf0`, optional filter callback `db+0x15b8` (null in all runs), dedupe stamp `item+8`, `CullNode` 0x6710e0 unless status 1 (view range x `db+0x15b4`, `CullCamera(box, sphere, range)` 0x67e8e0). The frustum is a **beam tree** inside the filter (`db+0x118`: nodes `{int plane, inside*, next*}`, planes `{a b c d, selector}` at tree `+0x12d8`, box corner tables `+0x12e8 / +0x1348`; the sphere / box walks are ported literally), `CullCameraSphere` = the frustum's bounding sphere (`filter +0x44 / +0x48`), `CullCameraDistance` = the range sphere around the camera. `RequestRenderOps` 0x66cfe0 per element: `CalculateFadeOut` 0x671220 (fade 1 inside 0.9 of the range [DAT_1809f1674 = 0.9], linear to 0, `db[0x15a9]` = no range), `Request` 0x671370 (`item+0x10 & mask`; illumination update when mask bit 0x80; the visible stamp `item+0xc = db+8` when bit 0x40; per context `UpdateLoD` (mask bit 1) + `RequestRenderOps`).

**Compare results (steady state, per view).**

| scene (run) | elements / view (engine, native) | fade | LOD pick (multi-LOD ctx) | hierarchy nodes ((ctx, sub-mesh) pairs) | missing | extra |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| savegame hold (harbour, `hold9`, 554 views / 5 s) | 792 / 953 | 0 of 438,901 differ | 0 of 145,702 | 0 of 12,742 | 0.1 per view (stale flags) | ~160 per view, all portal-room entities (below) |
| walk A / B (quick-load, 120 s) | 732 / 885, 731 / 884 | 0 differ | 0 of 124,805 / 124,118 | 0 of 13,080 / 13,008 | 0.07 / 0.01 | ~153 per view |
| new-game beach walk | 896 / 946 | 502 of 486,211 differ (max delta 0.011) | 0 of 120,003 | 0 of 22,263 | ~1.0 (the sky entity, class 0x97b1f8) | ~50 per view |
| spots pub_interior, jungle, harbour_docks (11 windows) | 84 .. 1,049 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 .. 0.8 | 1 .. 8 per view (portal rooms) |

No view is difference-free because of the room path below. The tree-path elements (sector tree leaves with `flags & 7 == 1`, ~620 of 792 at the savegame) are **exact**: after the leaf gate was applied (its first version used the last query's mask, which belongs to another database: now per database) there is not one missing or extra element of the plain classes; fade, LOD and node sets are equal in every run (hundreds of thousands of elements, 0 differences). Residuals, named:
1. **Portal-room entities** (node flags bit 1 / 2; class vtable 0x9ae070 mostly): the native side takes the entities of the rooms the engine's own room walk reached (room visibility is **read from the engine's element list, not derived: stage-0 limit**) and tests their boxes against the view's final beam, which is a superset of the room-clipped beams the engine uses (temporaries on its stack) with no CullNode (the experiment with CullNode changed nothing, so the extras are not the range): ~150 extra per view outdoors, up to 8 indoors. Deriving it needs the portal walk (`eCBeamTree::AddPoly` polygon clipping, `eCPortal::GetCell`, 0x78-byte portals) ported: **the main blocker of F10-1**. Not compared: the room's entity list (`room +0x70 / +0x78`) against `GetPortalRoom(item)` membership (a possible cause of the over-inclusion).
2. **Stale cache**: 0 .. 55 per 5 s window (entity flags / bounds changed without a signal; the rolling scrub of 16 .. 32 slots per frame found 435 stale of 65,849 checks at the savegame = 0.7 %): flags have no event (`GetEntityFlags` is a folded getter), so a takeover needs a per-frame flag poll or the Game.dll writers.
3. Beach fade: 502 of 486 k differ by < 0.011 (not chased; likely a different sphere for some class, the engine's fade is evaluated after the fact with the same camera).
4. `extract filter callback` = 0 and `unknown to the scene table` = 0 in every run (the scene table covers the extracted set).
The sector tree's leaf flags are read from the engine's tree each compared view (diagnostic input `Cache::nodeFlags`); deriving the node flags (set at tree insertion) is part of F10-1.

**Cost (CPU, per main view; the compare overhead excluded).** Native collection = cull loop over all slots + fade / request / LOD + hierarchy nodes: **savegame 103 us** (cull 80 + fade / LOD 20.5 + nodes 2.5) + scene maintenance 18 us; **beach 160 us** (134 + 19 + 7.4) + 9 us; interiors 50 us. Engine: `ExtractFromFrustum` 225 us + `Request` 208 us = **433 us at the savegame** (F2e-0: 347 us with a lighter hook), beach 467 + 220 = **687 us**, spots 117 us. The native side is 4 - 6x cheaper, **but it creates no ops, does no QueueData / CacheResource, no illumination update, no visible stamp** (below), and its room entities are an engine read. Expected F10 gain once those are added back: about -0.25 .. -0.35 ms at the savegame, -0.45 at the beach; not demonstrated.

**M3 side-effect catalogue and baseline** (`SideEffectRec`, `diffSideEffects`, `digest`, rvk.log `native: f10 side effects`; all runs: **visible stamp = frame 100 %, resources touched 100 %, busy counter non-zero 0, illumination still dirty 0, LOD resource not resident 0**). What the engine does per requested element, and what the snapshot records after it:
- visible stamp `item +0xc` (= `entity +0xe4`) = `db +8` (when mask bit 0x40; the decals read it);
- the LOD resource `ctx +0x28` (`UpdateLoD`: the highest resident LOD; `PrefetchData(wanted)` when the wanted one is not resident);
- `QueueData(res, 1)` for a mesh / material in state 1 / 2, else `CacheResource(res)` = the LRU move-to-front (`res +0x10 / +0x18` links, cache `+0x18` head, `res +0x20` = cache clock `cache +0x28`): recorded as "last use == clock" for the LOD resource and each sub-mesh material;
- the busy counter `res +0x24` (++ ... -- inside the call: 0 between calls);
- `UpdateIllumination` for elements with an illumination PS when mask bit 0x80 (dirty byte `PS +0x188`: 0 afterwards);
- `AddRenderInstance` for render-instance meshes (`res +0xa0`) and `AddShadowCells` / pre-pass requests: not snapshotted.
The digest is order independent; a takeover must show `diffSideEffects(engine, native).zero()` over the same views. No baseline file is stored: the digest of each window's last view is in the log.

**What blocks F10-1:** (a) the portal-room walk (item 1: ~25 % of the elements outdoors); (b) the sector tree's leaf flags and the tree itself (node spheres of moving entities, flags set at insertion) or an equivalent native structure; (c) entity flag changes (no event); (d) the residency side effects above re-done natively and proven by the snapshot diff; (e) `RequestRenderOps` itself: the caps mask per sub-mesh from the material shader's virtuals (+0x198 / +0x1a8), `eCRenderOp2::Create` into four lists, render-instance batching, a native copy of a 300-line function (7.30 section 4): the collection alone does not remove the engine's cost while ops are still taken from its lists.

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 305 / 0 (8 new `f10_*`); `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan and `--backend d3d12`: PASS, 0 failures, 0 validation errors. Game runs (private copy `risen-test-vk-f10`, instance 33, lock `rvk-f10`, Full, lod 2x, `RVK_F10_COMPARE=1`; no GPU work in this stage, so no GPU-assisted validation run): savegame hold, 2 `rvk_rtwalk` walks of 120 s from the savegame (quick-load), 1 new-game beach walk, `rvkcompare` pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks: all completed, no `compare faulted`, no error lines.

### 7.31b F10-0b: the portal walk, the leaf flags and the stale entries done natively; the collection matches the engine exactly (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a5fe4ccd96e824571` on main 9ef756c; no new setting, env `RVK_F10_COMPARE=1`; compare only, no takeover)

Built: `src/native/f10_portal.h` (pure: `NBeam` = the engine's beam tree layout (root, 200 nodes of 0x18 bytes, plane array), `addPoly` = `eCBeamTree::AddPoly` 0x67afb0 + `clipPoly` = `FUN_18067c120` / `FUN_18067c510` + the classify `FUN_18067c900`, `addPortalPoly` = the wrapper 0x67cb90, `walkRooms` = `ExtractFromPortalRooms` 0x66db40 over a graph adapter, `NBeam::copyFrom` / `addPlaneAtRoot` = `CopyFrom` 0x67c9e0 / `AddPlane` 0x67d040, `sameNode`; 7 new tests `f10_portal_*` in `tests/f10_portal_tests.cpp`: corner tables, clip keeps the right side with one clip edge, the cone beyond a portal, clipping through a half-space beam, copy equality incl. the engine layout, a two-room walk, camera inside a portal's box) and in `src/native/f10_collect.cpp`: the graph adapter over the engine's rooms / portals (`EngineGraph`), `findPortalRoom` (0x672610 ported), `portalWalk` (the camera room's entry + the outdoor entry of every room), the leaf gate without the tree map, live entity flags, dynamic bounds. Constants read from the engine at run time: classify front +1 / behind -1 (DAT_1809f1698 / DAT_1809f1854), portal box margin 2 (DAT_1809f16f8), g_fEpsilon 1e-5.

**The engine's portal walk, decoded** (Engine.dll, `ExtractFromFrustum` 0x66fa60 with `db[0x15a3] == 1`; dumps `rvk-work/scratch/f10/beam_dump*.txt`, asm checked for every operand order):
1. `UpdateFrameDependencies` has built the **frustum beam** (`eCBeamTree::Init` 0x67bc40: 6 plane nodes chained through "next") in `db+0x118` before `ExtractFromFrustum` runs. The camera room = `FindPortalRoom(camera)` (rooms of the portal admin, bounding sphere then BSP leaf planes).
2. **Camera in a room R0**: the frustum beam is copied away, `db+0x118` is emptied, `ExtractFromEntity(R0's entity, 1)`, `ExtractFromPortalRooms(R0, frustumCopy, build = true, null)`; afterwards the near plane (`db+0xb8`) is added at the root (`AddPlane`). **Outdoors**: `db+0x118` stays the frustum.
3. Occluders (portal admin `+0x38`, count `+0x40`; `eCOccluder_PS::AddOccluder` 0x675270 = silhouette polygons of the occluder meshes, sorted, `AddPoly`'d into `db+0x118`): see open items.
4. **Every room** (portal admin `+0x20` / `+0x28`): `CullNode(room entity)` (flag 0x8000, view range x factor, box / sphere against the final beam); for each portal whose cell is not a room (leads outdoors): camera outside the portal's box (margin 2): `(v0 - cam) . normal >= 0` -> the portal polygon (vertices reversed, normal as is) is clipped through the final beam into a fresh tree (`AddPoly` with a destination tree), `ExtractFromPortalRooms(room, tree, false, null)`; camera inside the box: a copy of the final beam. This is how rooms seen from outside through their outdoor portals appear.
5. `ExtractFromPortalRooms(room, beam, build, from)`: `ExtractFromPortalRoom` (each entity of `room+0x70` / `+0x78` whose **box** is not culled by the beam: `ExtractFromEntity(e, 1)` = the gate + no `CullNode`), then per portal (0x78 bytes: 4 vertices, normal +0x30, box +0x3c / +0x48, cell cache +0x58): camera inside the portal's box -> the other room gets a copy of the beam (not back into `from`), a cell that is not a room with `build` -> the final beam becomes a copy of the beam; outside the box: skipped when `(v0 - cam) . normal > 0`, else the polygon (vertices as stored, normal negated) is clipped through the beam into a new tree: the outdoors (`build`) adds it to the **final beam** (`db+0x118`, chained through "next"), a room gets the new tree, its own entity is extracted and the walk recurses.
6. The sector tree query with the final beam (7.31: unchanged; mask 7 / value 1).
`AddPoly` (0x67afb0): walking the polygon through the source beam: at a node the polygon is clipped by the node's plane, the part **behind** goes to the `inside` child (only if it exists), the part **in front** to `next` (always); when a fragment reaches an empty slot it is added to the destination tree as a chain of its edge planes (plane through the camera: `(v_k - cam) x (v_k+1 - v_k)`, normalised when its length^2 > g_fEpsilon, selector bits 1 / 2 / 4 = the normal's sign) ending in the polygon's own plane. The clip functions classify with +-1 unit, new vertices at `v + (v' - v) * (d + d0) / (d0 - d1)`, clip edges get id -1.

**Compare results** (final build, `RVK_F10_COMPARE=1`, Full, lod 2x, private copy `risen-test-vk-f10`, instance 33, lock `rvk-f10`; counts over all windows of >= 50 views; "differ" = not equal to the engine's, per element):

| scene (run) | views | elements / view (engine = native) | missing | extra | fade | LOD pick | hierarchy nodes ((ctx, sub-mesh) pairs) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| savegame hold (harbour, `dhold1`) | 7,575 | 767 | 0 | 0 | 0 of 5,811,159 | 0 of 1,907,926 | 0 of 173,554 |
| rtwalk A (quick-load, 120 s) | 14,542 | 722 | 0 | 0 | 0 of 10,494,155 | 0 of 3,282,981 | 0 of 346,897 |
| rtwalk B (120 s) | 14,760 | 724 | 0 | 0 | 0 of 10,687,437 | 0 of 3,335,491 | 0 of 355,169 |
| new-game beach walk (120 s) | 14,160 | 830 | 0 | 0 | 0 of 11,752,728 | 0 of 2,857,746 | 0 of 547,720 |
| spots pub_interior, jungle, harbour_docks (camera in a room: 556 views) | 6,560 | 557 | 0 | 0 | 0 of 3,652,091 | 0 of 786,631 | 0 of 197,782 |
| spots monastery_hall, crystal_cave (camera in a room: 2,604 views) | 6,092 | 434 | 0 | 0 | 0 of 2,641,652 | 0 of 572,185 | 0 of 144,033 |

**63,689 main views, every one difference-free** (the requested-element sets are equal too; the M3 side-effect snapshot lines are unchanged: stamp 100 %, touched 100 %). The earlier builds of this stage had 0 .. 0.07 missing per view left, all in the first windows after a load or at the actors (class 0x9b7180: stale bounds, below); there are no transients left in the final build.

**The five residuals of 7.31**
1. **Portal-room entities: done.** The native walk reproduces the engine's room-clipped beams; the old +150 per view are gone. Room membership uses the room's own entity list (`room+0x70`), not `GetPortalRoom(item)`: they agree (0 differences). The walk's cell test is the cell's vtable == `eCPortalRoom_PS`'s (0x9ca870), `eCPortal::GetCell` 0x6718e0 is called when the cell cache (+0x58) is empty. Per view at the harbour: 332 rooms, 247 culled by `CullNode`, 18 entered from outside, 179 list entries tested, 47 accepted; indoors 0.6 .. 6 rooms entered from outside. **Final beam check**: the native final beam (frustum copy outdoors; the camera room's walk + near plane indoors) is structurally equal (planes by value, nodes by shape) to the engine's in **100 % of the views where no occluder survives `CullNode`** (spots: 1,832 of 1,832 and 1,507 of 1,507, camera in a room in 556 / 2,604 of those views, frustum copy == engine's outdoors 1,276 of 1,276) and in 96 % of the harbour views (7,270 of 7,575; 6 occluders survive per view and add nothing in most), but in 0 of 14,160 beach views (21 survive per view): **the occluder polygons are not ported** (open item); the compare uses the engine's final beam for the outdoor entries and the tree query, so the element sets are exact either way. The native camera room (`findPortalRoom`) is validated indirectly by the final beam equality of the camera-in-room views and by the element sets.
2. **Sector tree leaf flags: derived / cached, no per-view tree map.** Found: `eCFrustumElementItem::SetPortalRoom` 0x670a70 reaches the leaf in O(1) (scene admin = the cached global at Engine RVA 0xc624e0: array of 0x60-byte entries at `+0x148` indexed by `entity+0x118`, entry `+0x18` = node index, nodes of 0x58 bytes at `+0x3c0`, flags at node `+0x18`) and **sets bit 1 (value 2) while the item has a portal room, unless bit 2 (value 4) is set**: so bit 1 = `item(+0xd8)+0x28 != null && !bit 2`, derived per candidate. Bits 0 and 2 (values 1 and 4) are set at the entity's registration: not a function of the entity's flags (17 of 106 distinct flag words appear with more than one bit-0/bit-2 pair) nor of the room; they are **stable**: read once per entity at its first sight (about 4,000 reads per 5 s window at the savegame, 100 at the beach), re-checked by the rolling scrub (0 stale) and, in compare mode, against a live read at every candidate (**16 .. 18 M gate checks per 120 s run, 0 mismatches**). The tree path's gate is `(bit0 | derived bit 1 | bit 2) & 7 == 1`. The insertion code that sets bits 0 / 2 was not located (the scene admin's registration; candidates `eCEntityAdmin` / `eCGeometryEntity::OnCacheIn` 0x6a9720, which only sets entity flag 0x20 and calls `eCFrustumElementItem::CacheIn`): a takeover caches them at the scene table's refresh as done here and keeps the scrub.
3. **Stale cache: causes found, 0 differences.** Two different things: (a) the **entity flag word** has no event (writers found: `OnCacheIn` 0x6a9720 `|= 0x20`, `OnCacheOut` 0x6a97b0 `&= ~0x20`, `Enable` 0x6a96a0 bit 2, the view range / hidden / dynamic setters; inline writers elsewhere): the native gate now reads the **live flags and contexts of every culling survivor** (833 reads per view at the savegame, 913 at the beach: about 2 us, one cache miss each; **0 gate flips between the cache and the live word in every run** after (b)) and does not trust the cache for the gate; (b) the **bounds of dynamic entities** (entity flag 0x20000; ~3,950 of them per view at the savegame) change without any scene-table signal: skinned actors (context class 0x9b7180: bounds from the animation pose) and the sky (0x97b1f8) every frame; an entity first seen is re-read every view for 90 views, a dynamic entity found changing stays re-read for 120 views, a class where 12 entities were seen changing (only 0x9b7180) is re-read for all its dynamic entities every view (~290 reads per view), the others every 16th view. The room walk reads dynamic entities' boxes live (its list test comes before the main loop's refresh): that was the cause of the last differences (actors in rooms). Compare-mode constructs: a takeover needs the animation's bounds update as the signal (`eCFrustumElementItem` / the skinned context) or the per-view read of the ~300 actors.
4. **Beach fade: cause found and fixed**: the engine evaluates `CalculateFadeOut` with the entity's live sphere; the cached sphere of an actor was a frame (or more) old (the 502 differences < 0.011 were all class 0x9b7180). 0 of 11.8 M differ now.
5. **Missing sky entity at the beach**: the sky entity's flag word / bounds changed without a signal (its leaf passed the gate only in the engine's view of the live flags); the live flag read + the per-view bounds refresh of volatile entities fixed it: 0 missing in all runs.

**Cost** (CPU, per main view; compare overhead, the diagnostics inside the walk and the node-flag verification reads are excluded from the native numbers; final build, `dhold1` / `dwalk` / `dbeach1` / `dspots` runs, similar in the `e` re-run):

| scene | engine `ExtractFromFrustum` + `Request` | native portal walk + cull (live flags, dynamic bounds, leaf gate) + fade / request / LOD + hierarchy nodes | scene maintenance |
|---|---|---|---|
| savegame hold | 225 + 199 = **424** us | 46..51 + 138..151 + 17 + 3 = **208 .. 216** us | 14 us |
| rtwalk A / B | 213 + 192 = 405 / 226 + 199 = 425 | 52 + 137 + 16 + 2 = **207** / 52 + 141 + 17 + 3 = **212** | 14 |
| beach | 448 + 212 = **660** | 56 + 172 + 17 + 7 = **252** | 7 |
| spots (harbour_docks, jungle, pub_interior) | 331 + 183 = **514** | 46 + 149 + 14 + 5 = **215** | 11 |
| monastery_hall, crystal_cave | 323 + 168 = **491** | 47 + 144 + 12 + 6 = **209** | 10 |

The native collection is now 2.0 - 2.6x cheaper than the engine's (F10-0's 103 us missed the portal walk, the live flags and the dynamic bounds: the exact version costs about twice that). Portal walk alone 39 .. 56 us. Still not a gain by itself: no ops, no `QueueData` / `CacheResource`, no illumination update, no visible stamp (the M3 catalogue of 7.31 is unchanged).

**Open items for F10-1**: (a) the **occluders** (`eCOccluder_PS::AddOccluder` 0x675270: per occluder mesh the silhouette edge loops facing the camera -> polygons {vertex array, index range, plane, distance}, qsort by the distance field (+0x18), `AddPoly(db+0x118, ..., null destination, chain through "inside")` in reverse order; 39 occluders in the harbour world, 0 .. 21 survive `CullNode` per view): until ported the final beam is the engine's (outdoors the frustum copy equals it whenever no occluder survives); (b) `eCBeamTree::Init` (the frustum beam: 6 planes from the camera matrix) is captured from the engine at the entry of `ExtractFromFrustum` (a 15 KB copy, 0.4 us), not rebuilt; (c) the native cull still reads engine memory per view (live flags, dynamic bounds, room lists, portals): the persistent-table version of these reads is F10-1; (d) 7.31's (d) / (e): side effects and `RequestRenderOps`.

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 312 tests, 0 failures (305 + 7 new `f10_portal_*`); `rvk_d3d9test --validation` (Vulkan) and `--backend d3d12`: PASS, 0 failures, 0 validation errors (no GPU work in this stage, so no GPU-assisted validation run). Game runs as in the table (+ four earlier iterations of the same set: `a` .. `c` and `e`), all completed, no `compare faulted`, no error lines. Logs and scripts: `rvk-work/scratch/f10/` (`a*` first port, `b*` with live flags, `c*` / `d*` volatile classes, `e*` final; `summ.py`, `occ.py`, `soak2.ps1`, `batch2.ps1`).

### 7.32 F2c-4c: GPU compaction of the owned draws' indirect arguments + `DrawIndexedIndirectCount` (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-ad76e17abc1f20246` on main dbcc493; no new setting, env `RVK_F2C4C_CPU=1`, `RVK_F2C4C_CHECK=n`, overlay command `debug compact cpu|gpu`)

Stage F2c-4c of section 11 / 13 (the F5 prerequisite). Files: `shaders/gpuscene/owned_compact.comp.hlsl`, `src/gpuscene/owned_compact.{h,cpp}`, `Device::ownedFlush` (`d3d9/owned_persist.cpp`), the RHI (`DrawPacket::Op::DrawIndexedIndirectCount`, `BufferAccess::IndirectRead`, `GameDevice::packetHandle`; Vulkan `vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount` in `vk/game_record.cpp`, D3D12 `ExecuteIndirect` with a count buffer in `d3d12_game_impl.inc`), `DrawRecord::args` (`shader/abi.h`; the HLSL mirror keeps the name `reserved`: a rename would change the embedded ABI headers' hash and recompile every cached shader).

* **What it does.** 7.21's flush wrote, per item, a list entry (slot, view slot: 8 B) and an indirect argument record (20 B) into two ring blocks and queued ONE `DrawIndexedIndirect` per bucket. Now the flush writes the list and one 16-byte job per bucket (first entry, entries, first argument index, count index); at the present (after the record table's own upload, in the UPLOAD list, one memory barrier before, one `IndirectRead` barrier after) a compute pass runs one group per bucket: it reads the bucket's entries in chunks of 64, loads each entry's `DrawRecord` (`draw` address != 0 and `args[0]` = indexCount != 0, slot < the table's capacity: the same guards as the CPU's), compacts the valid ones IN ORDER (an inclusive scan in groupshared memory per chunk, no wave intrinsics) into `VkDrawIndexedIndirectCommand`s `{indexCount, 1, firstIndex, vertexOffset, firstInstance = the entry's position in the flush's list block}` and writes the bucket's count. The packet is a `DrawIndexedIndirectCount` over [bucket start, count word]: the arguments and the counts of one frame slot live in ONE device buffer (counts first: 4,096 x 4 B, then 16,384 x 20 B = 336 KiB per frame in flight; counts + arguments need UAV writes, the ring is an upload heap on D3D12). The record's `args` (indexCount, firstIndex, vertexOffset) are part of `ownedDrawSig`, so a draw whose packet moved rewrites its slot; the flush additionally compares every item's record with its draw (tripwire `record args differ from the draw`, refuses the flush). **What the persistent table's frame-ordering gives:** the dispatches are recorded in the upload list at the present, i.e. from the END-of-CPU-frame state of the records, before every draw of the frame (the lighting images' recipe, 7.9); the jobs of a frame are collected while it is composed (`OwnedCompact::queue`), their ring blocks (list, jobs) live until the frame completes.
* **Fallbacks.** The CPU-written arguments stay (the 7.21 path, byte for byte) when `DeviceCaps::drawIndirectCount` / `drawIndirectFirstInstance` is missing, while the pipeline is still being built, when a frame's capacity (16,384 records / 4,096 buckets) is spent, when a ring block cannot be had, with `RVK_F2C4C_CPU=1` and with the overlay command `debug compact cpu` (run-time A/B in ONE run; `gpu` = back). Nothing exists before the first flush asks (no buffers, no pipeline).
* **Checks.** Every 30th frame that has flushes (`RVK_F2C4C_CHECK=n`; default 30 since 85ca8f6, the first game runs below used 6; 0 = never, 1 = every frame; NOT `verifyFrame()`: on those frames the owned path composes instead of flushing, so they never carry a flush) the flush also keeps the CPU's expectation (the arguments the CPU path would have written: count per bucket + every record) and the present copies the GPU's counts + arguments into a read-back buffer, compared once the frame completed: `native: owned compaction (5 s): ... checks N (B buckets, R records compared) DIFFERING 0`.
* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 312 / 312 (settings count unchanged: no setting). `rvk_rhitest --validation` PASS on Vulkan and D3D12 (D3D12 with the debug layer + GPU-based validation), new case "owned draws compaction (F2c-4c)": the production shader on a record table with a record without a draw address, one with indexCount 0, a slot beyond the table, buckets of 150 / 1 / 64 / 65 entries (several chunks, a full chunk, the lone entry of a second chunk), non-zero count / argument bases and indices; the counts and every argument record equal the CPU's compaction, nothing is written outside the buckets' words, then `IndirectRead` + a copy out (D3D12 state tracking). `rvk_d3d9test --sync-validation` (new flag, also on `rvk_rhitest`; `DeviceDesc::syncValidation`) Vulkan + `--backend d3d12`: PASS 0 failures, 0 validation errors; the "owned persistent (F2c-4a)" case now runs ITS draws through the compaction (the pipeline is awaited first): eight persistent draws = one `DrawIndexedIndirectCount` call, 0 bytes differ from the normal draws, every guard / slot-reuse / two-views case unchanged, 7 check frames compared 56 records: DIFFERING 0. `rvk_rhitest --sync-validation` (Vulkan): the new case is clean; the two pre-existing errors of the basic test are swapchain acquire layout hints (`WRITE_AFTER_READ` of `vkCmdPipelineBarrier2` in the frame slot's commands before any of the new passes), not caused by this change. `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded`: 78 / 78 signed DXIL (the new shader is in the `gpuscene` row; push block 48 B).
* **Game runs** (Full, `-PlayerDefaults`, owned chain + `native.owned_persistent`, private copy `risen-test-vk-f5` instance 34, lock `rvk-f5`, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f5/`): **first run Vulkan `-Validation` + `VK_KHRONOS_VALIDATION_VALIDATE_GPU_BASED=GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED`**, rvkcompare 3 spots, 150 s: PASS, 0 VUID, 0 `[error]` / `[warning]` lines, no device loss; compaction 7 - 96 k items per 5 s window, 478 flushes per window, checks 35 - 40 per window (up to 16,160 records compared), **DIFFERING 0**, `owned persistent` MISS 0 / DIFFERING 0. D3D12 + debug layer (no GBV: `RVK_NO_GBV=1`): PASS, 0 errors, checks 33 - 40 per window, DIFFERING 0. D3D12 + GPU-based validation (`-Validation`; 0.1 fps with the RT scene): 12 messages, ALL from the pipeline `light.shadow trace` ("uninitialized descriptor heap index 1600" in Dispatch 2); the identical run with `RVK_F2C4C_CPU=1` gives the same messages (39 in its longer run): they are an old finding of the lamp-shadow trace pass, none comes from `owned draws compact`. (To do: that finding is not part of this stage.)
* **Picture A/B** (the 7.11 method, `debug compact cpu|gpu` every 300 frames inside one hold, a shot per 50 frames; `scratch/f5/abin_*`): pub_interior toggle pairs **0.000** mean absolute difference (max 0 - 1 level) against same-state pairs 0.002; harbour_docks toggle pairs 0.05 - 0.33 (max 24 - 65: the sea) against same-state 0.21 (0.03 - 0.42). No error line.
* **CPU** (per main view = per flush set of a pass, the `flush` piece of the `native: owned persistent (5 s)` line, measure timers ON, A / B / A / B inside ONE run through `debug compact cpu|gpu`, only 5 s windows wholly inside a state counted): **no CPU gain: +0.6 .. +1.5 us per view** (table below; the section 11 expectation of -10..-15 us is not reached: the CPU-written arguments were five stores per item into the ring, about 1 - 2 us for a 273-item view, and the compaction adds a fixed part per flush + per bucket (the reservation, the job block, the queue, one packet fill per bucket) and about 1 us per dispatch recorded at the present, which this piece does not contain).

| view | items / buckets per view | CPU-written arguments | GPU compaction | check every 30th frame | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| pub_interior hold | 15 / 12 | 3.97 us (42 windows) | 4.61 us (27 windows), +0.64 | off | first measurement with a check every 6th frame: 3.86 vs 5.01 (the expectation of a flush costs ~50 ns per item on those frames) |
| savegame hold (`Risen_Save_0005`, lod 2x) | 273 / 59 | 26.57 us (11 windows) | 27.65 us (4 windows), +1.08 | 26.60 vs 28.05, +1.45 | the args tripwire reading every 8th item off the check frames (f6ad525) did not move it (27.8 before) |

  Engine + HAL CPU per frame (`-Perf`, RisenTest.exe, vsync off, hold, 5 s windows, shared copy + lock, A / B / A / B in one run): pub_interior CPU args 1.70 / 1.68 / 1.59 / 1.60 vs GPU 1.68 / 1.64 / 1.60 / 1.60 ms; harbour_docks 3.30 / 3.26 / 3.12 vs 3.13 / 3.12 / 3.08 - 3.11 ms: differences between -0.17 and 0.0 ms that follow the drift of the hold, **not resolvable from the noise** (the hold replays most draws; the flush is a small part of the frame).
* **GPU** (`-PerfFrame 5200` + `RVK_PERF_FRAMES=24`, pub_interior, RX 7900 XTX): the upload stream (table copies + compaction) 0.0050 ms with the CPU arguments -> 0.0075 ms with the compaction (**+2.5 us**; 7 - 10 tiny dispatches per frame); the main submission 1.07 vs 1.11 ms (noise).
