**No stage FAILS.** The one number outside its noise (own_post, harbour_docks, one round) is an exposure outlier, not a repeatable difference; it is the only item I would re-run if own_post is promoted.

**Owned trees CPU / GPU, re-measured with the trees really drawn** (the 7.25 / 7.25b numbers were taken while most trees were not: 7.46). Recipe 7.26: savegame `Risen_Save_0005`, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, `-PlayerDefaults`, `lod.view / trees / meshes = 2`, `hold=60`, default shared copy + lock held per run (`with-test-lock.ps1 -Who ver-tally`), **4 rounds interleaved S A AS** (S = the 7.26 arm S: the table chain + `owned_static` + `owned_persistent` + `rt.table_entries`; A = S + `owned_trees` + `owned_billboards`; AS = A + `owned_shadows`: the cascades' trees owned too), the steady 5 s `perf:` windows of each run (world windows with GPU > 1 ms, the first dropped: 11 - 13 per run), the median per run, then the mean of the runs; logs `rvk-work/scratch/ver/tally/`, `ana2.py`, `drive2.ps1`; each arm's stage line asserted by eye in its log (A: `owned_trees=active(main 240 owned views ...)`, AS: `owned_shadows=active(...)` too). **The machine was at 97 % CPU from another agent's `rvk_shadertool` during all four rounds**, so the numbers are noisy (AS round 2: 15.85 ms; the others 8.05 - 10.09):

| arm | CPU ms / frame (runs) | GPU ms / frame (runs) | fps |
|---|---|---|---|
| S | 8.71 (9.39, 8.40, 8.33, 8.73) | 3.43 (3.07, 3.50, 3.59, 3.57) | 110 |
| A | 8.79 (8.52, 8.58, 8.91, 9.16) | 3.55 (3.50, 3.59, 3.74, 3.37) | 109 |
| AS | 10.65 (8.61, 15.85, 8.05, 10.09) | 3.69 (2.93, 3.77, 3.62, 4.41) | 97 |

**A - S = +0.08 ms CPU, +0.12 ms GPU: owned trees (main view, all trees drawn) save no CPU and cost ~0.1 ms of GPU at the savegame, inside the noise of this load (the runs of S and A overlap: S 8.33 .. 9.39, A 8.52 .. 9.16); the 7.25 claim "-0.2 ms CPU" does not hold (it was measured with 80 % of the trees missing).** AS (cascades' trees too) is not resolvable at this load (its best run 8.05 is below S's best 8.33, its worst 15.85). Verdict unchanged: **correct now, not a win; not for the player defaults** (that decision stays with the owner of the F-plan); to re-measure on a quiet machine before any claim.

**Open / known limits.** (1) The check compares the opaque scene at `AfterOpaque` (z / solid passes, decals, sky): the cascades' own pictures are covered only through the shadow mask they produce there, and the alpha / refracted passes (translucent effects, water) are not compared. (2) It needs a still scene; with FSR jitter or a moving camera the window is `UNRELIABLE`. (3) Colour noise of 0.15 - 0.2 % of the pixels between two composed frames of one still scene exists at every spot (the control sees it: box `[297,165..1599,899]`, max 0.42; not investigated: eye adaptation does not enter the HDR scene; looks like the sky's clouds / the sun's flare moving with real time). (4) D3D12 has the pass (RHI) and the compute test; no game run on D3D12 was made for it.

## 8. F1 / F2 design decision: windows onto persistent tables (2026-11-06, orchestrator)

**Decision.** F1 does not start with new shaders. Translated shaders and the ported ubershader read every float register through 4 register windows per
stage (`RVK_VSC(n)` / `RVK_PSC(n)`, `shaders/include/rvk_draw.hlsli`; window starts `abi.h kVsWindowFirst = {0, 12, 31, 232}`, `kPsWindowFirst =
{0, 5, 6, 14}`: `c[n]` = `rvkRawLoad(window base + n * 16)`). The window classes already match the tables:

| window | vertex stage | pixel stage |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | c0..c11 view / camera (per view) | c0..c4 camera, fog (per view) |
| 1 | c12..c30 vegetation / tree data | c5 mask reference / obstacle (per draw: DrawBlock `psC5`) |
| 2 | c31..c231 object rows, instance data, bones | c6..c13 material `g_ShaderData` (Material table) |
| 3 | c232.. material `g_vShaderData` (Material table) | c14..c37 lighting (Illumination + LightSet, composed) |

So a window can point at a **persistent table record laid out as a register image** (record address - first register x 16) instead of a per-draw
ring upload, and every existing shader reads identical values: picture identical by construction, verified with the existing `debug.split_verify`
(every window block a draw reads compared with the CPU register file) and the tables' own checks.

* **F1a (CPU path, persistent windows):** for the static ops N5 composes, the windows that a table covers point into the table: PS window 2 + VS window 3
  -> a Material register image (c6..c13, c232..c251), VS / PS window 0 -> a per-view block written once per view, VS window 2 -> a per-instance object
  block (the world rows / fade the composition writes, re-written on the matrix signal or a poll), PS window 3 -> a lighting register image per
  (illumination, pass parameters) composed from the Illumination / LightSet records (first on the CPU with `gpuscene::composeLighting`, then F1b on the
  GPU with its HLSL twin `rvkIllumCompose` in one compute pass per frame). Window bases may lie below the record (base = record - first x 16): Vulkan
  addresses are plain 64-bit arithmetic; D3D12 (heap slot, byte offset) needs offset >= first x 16, so register-image tables start with a pad of
  first x 16 bytes (VS window 3: 3,712 B; a 4 KiB pad covers every window). Gate: `split_verify` mismatches 0, picture identical, the composition
  of the covered registers skipped (CPU measured, gpu-scene.md 9.2 bounds the setter part at < 0.05 ms: the value of F1a is the proof of the register
  images, the CPU gain comes with F2).
* **F2 (GPU-driven):** one uniform shader change for every pipeline of an owned draw: a *scene variant* where `rvkVsWin(w)` / `rvkPsWin(w)` read the 8
  window bases (+ the DrawBlock address) from a per-instance **DrawRecord** selected by the instance-rate ref stream (section 4) instead of the push
  block. DrawRecords are written by the compaction compute from (instance slot, part, material slot, illumination slot, pass); indirect draws per
  pipeline bucket. Translated engine shaders stay valid: batches are still split by pipeline (the engine's permutations), but no longer by register
  values, and the CPU no longer touches the draw. The owned ubershader (graph-shared pipelines, dynamic light loop: 196 pipelines estimated, 7.3)
  is the later step that merges permutations.

## 9. F2c design: DrawRecords from retained op records, no per-frame composition (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator)

**Hook point (changes 3.2 / F2's "suppress at RequestRenderOps"):** the engine runs `RequestRenderOps` unchanged, appends included (all request-time
side effects stay bit-exact). At `viewBegin` (frame tap, before the sort) the owned ops are compacted out of the db's z / solid lists; an op without
a valid retained record simply stays and takes today's path (composition + F2b-1 scene draw). No late composition.

**OpRecord** keyed (ctx, desc, list, viewKey) = today's packet key minus the draw index, snapshotted from the last composition: draws[] (the
`CachedDraw` packet + matSlot, partSlot, illum info, the shader quad sun x lightmap-combi, scene pipeline), side-effect list (images, lightmap res,
stream 3), flags. Validity = stamp compares instead of `packetSameDesc`: Material version, MeshPart generation, object-table version, illum record
version + light-set epoch (sun bit per set per frame), `variantEpoch` (every setter of GI / G-buffer / pack / tint gates), target-format epoch,
`LightTable::registryGeneration()` (new) for `holdsAll`, a per-illum cached `ambientLit` bit (CPU compose of c15..c21 from the records), per-frame
polls: lightmap residency (res+0x90/+0x98), entity overlay flags (+0x28 & 0xe00000), illum pointer (+0xf8), texture-ready per material.
Owned in the first cut only: mesh ctx, static, main view z + solid, not instanced, fade 1, no overlay flags, no switch-element material, no video
image, no hierarchy node, **table lighting on** (one pass per sub-mesh; without it the pass count follows the per-frame dynamic light count).
Traps: alpha func / ref are not composed (device carry-over) and must be derived natively; observers (RT scene) need the draw: decide before F2c-3.

**Object table** (`GpuTable<ObjectRecord>` by scene-table slot, image c31..c35: c32.w fade, c33..c35 world rows from entity +0x60, base = rec - 31 x 16;
written in the scene-table drain on the matrix signal, fade from the visible list, `checkEntity` on check frames); VS window 1 (c12..c30) in the
view record (+ a `dbvp` c0..c3 variant per view for depth-bias materials). Instanced VS (c32 + 4i) stays on the old path.

**Side effects per visible record per frame** (from the lean path): image LRU touches per visible material, `BindLightmaps(res, 1)` per solid
resource, stream 3 for leftover engine draws, texture `lastUseFrame_`, draw stats. Check frames (`native.owned_check=N`, the lean frame kinds):
no compaction, the old path runs, the record-derived draws are built and compared (normalised fields), not submitted; any diff turns the group off.

| stage | content | gate | CPU (Full, est.) |
|---|---|---|---|
| F2c-0 | measure: owned-eligible share by reason, observer reads, invalidation rates, CTAB-live c12..c31 of the static VS, occurrence of hierarchy / instanced ops | numbers: done, 7.12 | - |
| F2c-1 | object table, VS window 1 / 2 served, `LightingImages::entryFor`, `LightTable::registryGeneration` (`native.owned_objects`) | split_verify 0, read-backs 0, checkEntity 0 | ~0 |
| F2c-2 | OpRecords + record-derived DrawRecords built and compared while composition runs; read-log signal audit (`native.owned_records`) | DIFFERING 0, audit disagreements 0 (done, 7.14: DIFFERING 0 in every run, 0 - 4 benign audit disagreements per run) | +0.05..0.1 (measured 0.37 - 0.58 ms with the compare, 0.15 - 0.25 ms the derivation) |
| F2c-3 | viewBegin compaction + `Device::replayOwned` at BeforeDepthPrepass / BeforeSolid, engine sort order, side effects, check frames | check frames 0, refcompare Full, long tour (placeholderBinds -> 0), both backends, validation | **-0.5..-0.7** |
| F2c-4 | persistent records, GPU compaction (ref.y = view slot), later cull (F5) | compaction = CPU list | -0.03..-0.05 CPU, GPU / low-end gain |

## 10. F4 design: SpeedTrees + tree billboards GPU-driven (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator)

Same recipe as F2c (section 9): a retained per-(render buffer rb, type) record + a per-view per-tree snapshot (LOD fields tree +0x48 / +0x50 /
+0x58 / +0x5c / +0x60 / +0x64 / +0x6c, read once per tree per view at `viewBegin`: they are overwritten per db); tree ops compacted out of the z /
solid lists; the CPU writes per frame the instance rows (64 B {c32..c35} per instance, in draw order, VS window 2 base = rows - 31 x 16), one
DrawRecord per instance (template copy + vsWin2 + psWin3 = the instance's lighting image `entryFor(illum, params)`) and one 20 B indirect record per
LOD bucket (indexCount = strip / leaf count, firstIndex = start, firstInstance = the bucket's first row); multi-draw runs through the F2a scene
variant / F2b-1 `sceneFlushRun`. **Per-instance DrawRecords remove the illumination cut (88 % of today's solid tree batch cuts), the light-list /
static-SH / own-lights cuts and the 50-instance limit** (under table lighting; the remaining per-instance permutation input is the sun bit).
Remaining cuts: pipeline (type x z / solid / shadow x sun x material graph / states), the tree resource's vertex / index buffers, topology.
The SpeedTree VS reads its rows as `g_vInstanceData(vInstance * 4 ...)` (TEXCOORD4 = stream 1, ramp 0..n-1 [I]): stage 1 replaces stream 1 by our
own identity ramp (instance-rate, length >= row capacity; `firstInstance` offsets it), later the owned VS sets vInstance from ref.y. Wind c14..c29
(+ c30 fog) in a tree view block (VS window 1), once per view. Billboards: per-(ctx, desc) retained constants images (c32..c36 + c37+j), the easiest
stage. Side effects at compaction: queue stamp reset (rb +0xe0 / +0xe4) + sort (`leanTreeSort`), MaskReference 80 (`leanMaskRef`), material /
resource touches, billboard lightmaps, RtScene observer calls; keep composition for trees while RtScene's animated-tree path (captures) is on
(until F8 supplies the rows). Check frames compare rows / strips / alphas / pipeline exactly; lighting only where the engine's batch had one
illumination (it draws a merged batch with its last instance's lighting; ours is exact per instance: an accepted Full picture change).

| stage | content | gate | CPU (est.) |
|---|---|---|---|
| F4-0 | measure (`RVK_F4_MEASURE=1`): composeTree phase timers, census (trees / ops / buckets / calls under the new rule, two-bucket trees, no-entity share, sun values, illum classes, hw vs sw instancing), invalidation rates of the tree LOD fields / matrix / illum / rb tables / wind, CTAB of the tree VS / PS (c0..c31, PS c5), stream-1 freq / size / content, RtScene coupling | numbers | 0 |
| F4-1 | billboards from retained records | DIFFERING 0 | -0.15..-0.25 |
| F4-2 | tree records + per-view plans, compared | DIFFERING 0, audit 0 | +0.05..+0.1 (built, 7.25: DIFFERING 0) |
| F4-3 | CPU-owned tree execution main view (compaction, rows, records, args, side effects, check frames) | check frames 0, refcompare Full, jungle spots, rtwalk sway, lod.trees=3, both backends, validation | -1.0..-1.4 (built, 7.25 / 7.25b: -0.22 ms CPU with RT off, ~-0.1 with RT on, GPU z + solid +0.03..0.05 ms, commands <= the engine's batches, RT scene served except animated trees; open: refcompare, rtwalk with wind, picture A/B) |
| F4-4 | tree shadow views (cascades, z) | shadow-compare | -0.15..-0.3 (built, 7.39: DIFFERING 0, spots GPU-AV clean, walks clean, CPU -0.09 ms, noise-limited) |
| F7-1 | vegetation instance streams retained (exact source compare, persistent GPU buffer, replayable draws) | check frames DIFFERING 0, picture A/B | -0.05..-0.1 expected, not resolvable (7.40: the vegetation is per-op cost, 58 one-draw ops per frame; conversion only 7 %) |
| F7-2 | owned vegetation draws (templates per bucket, check frames, pass with one indirect command per bucket, instance block) | check frames DIFFERING 0, picture A/B, validation | built (7.42): -0.15 ms per frame of the vegetation's 0.42 ms, frame -0.10 ms (noisy) |
| F4-5 | persistent per-tree records (UpdateLoD post-hook), GPU compaction | compaction = CPU list | -0.05..-0.15 + GPU |
| F4-6 | owned tree shaders (leaf PS wired: `uberSupportsFile`; vertex pulling; graph-shared pipelines) | equality gates | calls ~10-40 |

## 11. F2c-4 design: the CPU no longer touches individual owned draws (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator)

Starting point (7.16): the owned path costs ~3.4 us per draw vs ~1.7 us for the packet-cache replay it replaced; F2c-4 must remove the whole
owned path (281 us at the savegame, ~990 us at pub_interior) to pay. The section 9 F2c-4 row (-0.03..-0.05) is superseded.
* **Persistent DrawRecords** (DrawRecordTable 128 B x 64K, slot reuse deferred by frames-in-flight + 1) + a persistent **DrawBlockTable** (208 B
  slots). Per-frame inputs move out of the record: **ABI v2** (`TranslateOptions::sceneView`, own variant slots, default text unchanged):
  `ref.y` = a fixed ViewBlock2 slot (640 B: VS c0..c11, PS c0..c4, VS c12..c30, posFix incl. jitter + depthRemap, PS c5.xyz, flags; fixed slots
  0 main z, 1 main solid, 2-3 dbvp, 4-7 cascades, dynamic slots after); `rvkVsWin(0/1)` / `rvkPsWin(0)` computed from `pc.views + ref.y x
  stride` (positive offsets only); `rvkLoadDraw()` takes posFix / c5.xyz from the view, c5.w = the record's mask bits; VARY14 = (viewSlot << 24)
  | record. Lighting entries pinned by a per-frame `seen` mark + an entry generation in the record.
* **Bulk validity**: a dep table (global env epoch per pass; per entity: overlay flags, illum ptr, object slot / generation; per ctx: LOD
  resource, fade, switch; per resource: MeshPart generation, lightmaps; per material: structure version; per illumination / light set) polled
  once per dep per frame; records are invalidated through reverse lists; per draw a 1-byte valid read. Check frames run the full stamp + read
  log for every visible record (MISS counter, auto-off like DIFFERING) + a rolling scrub of 16 records per frame + a fault-injection debug knob.
* **Compaction** (4c): one compute dispatch per present in the upload list (as the lighting compose), job per (view, list), per (job, bucket)
  stable wave-prefix compaction of the visible id list into args / ref / count in a device-local per-frame arena; bucket = (scene pipeline, vi,
  dyn, ib, frame block) with stable ids + caps; one `DrawIndexedIndirectCount` per hit bucket (new DrawPacket op; zero-filled tail fallback
  without the cap). Order inside a bucket = engine order (stable); across buckets = bucket id (visible only for coincident EQUAL geometry:
  re-run the picture A/B).
* **Observers in bulk**: `DrawObserver::observeOwned(Device&, const OwnedFrame&)` once per frame (views with visible ids + `entered` / `left`
  deltas, per-record immutable views: query, tag, textures, object rows + version, view blocks; default = the old per-id loop). **This is also
  the F8 hand-over** (section 12): RtScene derives its table upserts / drops from entered / left.
* **Side effects batched** per resource / material / primitive per frame (first visible record does the touch), after checking that
  `RequestData(res, 1)` is idempotent per frame (disassemble first).

| stage | content | gate | CPU (savegame / pub_interior, est.) |
|---|---|---|---|
| F2c-4-0 | ABI v2 + the F2c-3 owned path on v2; measurements (main views per frame, posFix / c5 per frame, the `submit` split derive / packet / observers, desc vs LOD, RequestData idempotence, buckets, sort share of compaction) | rvk_tests, d3d9test v2 cases, parity_scene v2 == v1, corpus / DXIL v2 | ~F2c-3 |
| F2c-4a | persistent records + dep epochs + CPU-written args (one indirect per hit bucket, exact CPU count) | check frames DIFFERING 0 + MISS 0, A/B, validation, long tour | ~105 / ~400 us (from 281 / 990); **built (7.21): -20 % of the owned path, 231 -> 185 / 354 -> 282 us; the observers + stamps are what is left** |
| F2c-4b | bulk observers (`observeOwned`) + batched side effects | TLAS set equal on check frames, rtwalk, water floor | ~25-30 / ~95-120 us: -0.11 / -0.9 ms vs the pre-F2c replay; **built (7.23): owned path 189 -> 160 us (savegame) / 421 -> 346 us (pub_interior) with the RT scene on (-15 / -18 %), RT observe -0.07 / -0.12 ms, RT pass +0.05; the target is NOT reached (the pre-filter in the persistent loop gives most of it; compaction stamps, build and the 32-frame relearn are what is left)** |
| F2c-4c | GPU compaction + `DrawIndexedIndirectCount` (F5 prerequisite) | rhitest compaction case, read-back DIFFERING 0, sync validation, D3D12 GBV | -10..-15 us CPU; GPU 10-30 us (680M 50-100) **built (7.32): arguments made by a compute pass, `DrawIndexedIndirectCount`; the CPU gain is not there (7.32 has the per-view numbers), GPU +2.5 us** |

## 12. F8 design: the RT scene from the GPU scene tables (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator)

Findings: the tables hold no entity -> parts relation for never-drawn entities (parts are created on first sight; the link is the OpRecord), so
"off-screen" coverage = every entity ever sighted and still cached in, dropped by signals (cache-out, delete, bufferChanged), not by age;
never-sighted ones need an engine visual enumeration (F10, not now). The BLAS key is derivable from `PartCpu` + buffer generations (no strips:
SpeedTree is never a part), so table-built and observed instances share `blasByKey_` (BLAS builds, proxies, budget unchanged). Rows come from
`scenetable::slot(i).world` on the CPU (identity (slot, generation, part), moved = slot.version, no quantised keys). Materials at hits are per
material record except the uv map (evaluated on record change from the record's pools + a representative VS / PS; the table needs a CPU
`Texture*` per sampler slot + the last VS so RtScene keeps its layout / release checks).
* Interface: persistent `RtStaticEntry` (objSlot / gen, opDesc, part / gen, matSlot / structVersion, entity, viewMask, rank) maintained by deltas
  (from `OwnedFrame` entered / left once F2c-4b exists; before that learned from the first full observation); `DrawObserver::coversDraw` pre-filter
  in the observer loops (prepareDraw, replayDraw, replayOwned) skips query building for covered (entity, opDesc); `record()` takes a merged list
  (table entries + registry) through one shared `emitInstance`; no duplicates: covered draws skip observe, a `tableOwners_` guard in
  `selectForTlas`, the observed instance removed on migration. Stays observed: characters (F6), SpeedTrees (F4), instanced batches, grass, glass,
  water, blended layers, fade < 1, hierarchy meshes, everything in Wrapper.
* Risks: parts idle-free after 1800 frames (pin parts with live entries), more BLAS alive (check `rt: scene: lod bands`, cap by radius), uv-map
  animation (dirty only on uv-slice constants), stale addresses in persistent entries (rebuild the BLAS key per frame from buffer generations).
* Verification: `debug.rt_table_compare` on check frames: table set vs observed set (missing = DIFFERING, extra = off-screen, per-pair material
  fields), auto-off; `rt.debug=depth / instances` probes; lamp / RT shadow and water A/B; teleport soak with crash markers; GPU-safety steps.

| stage | content | gain (est.) |
|---|---|---|
| F8-0 | persistent entries learned from observation, rows from the scene table, signal aging, `coversDraw` pre-filter, merged selection, owner guard (no F2c-4 dependency) | -0.15..-0.25 ms CPU |
| F8-1 | materials from records (`RtMaterials::fromRecord`) | -0.02..-0.05 |
| F8-2 | discovery from `OwnedFrame` entered / left (needs F2c-4b) | -0.05..-0.1; **built (7.23): `RtScene::observeOwned` skips covered records by liveness + slot stamp; RT observe -0.07 / -0.12 ms measured (with the pre-filter); the material "ambiguity" of F8-0 did not occur (guard built, never fired): the residual 1 - 7 x 10^-5 material differences are engine-graph state changes bounded by the 32-frame relearn** |
| F8-3 | cascade coverage (after F3 shadow-view records) | the larger share of the 0.376 ms |
| F8-4 | optional GPU instance assembly | only if profiled |
Measure first: observer calls per view / pass / class, observe vs query-building ms, registry composition by class, the share of instances
whose entity has an ownable OpRecord, the cascade-only (off-screen) share.

## 13. F5 plan: measure first, then cluster culling (no cluster LOD) (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator + user)

User decision 2026-11-06: **cluster LOD (Nanite-style simplified cluster hierarchies) is not planned**. Risen's geometry is from 2009, and the
meshes it would help simplify badly: SpeedTree leaves and alpha-tested cards, skinned actors, meshes whose lightmap UVs and vertex colours carry
baked light. RT gets nothing from it on AMD (the caps report: no cluster acceleration structures on the RX 7900 XTX). Revisit only if F5-0 shows
that far, sub-pixel triangles cost real GPU time. **LOD selection itself** moves to the GPU inside the F5 cull pass (per instance: distance ->
the LOD's index range in the indirect args; never in the vertex shader, which cannot change a draw's vertex count). The engine's resource cache
must still see its touches: either every LOD of an in-view object stays resident, or the GPU's choices are read back a frame late and touched per
resource in the batched side effects of F2c-4b, as GD3D11 does for its texture streaming. Measure the memory of "all LODs resident" before
deciding the 680M default. Until F5 the engine's LOD choice stays: it is a dep of the F2c-4a records (7.19 measurement 4: 27 LOD-resource
changes in 2.5 M context-frame pairs).

**Model: GD3D11's GPU world** (`E:\git\github\GD3D11`, `Shaders/D3D12/WorldCull.hlsl`, `WorldObjects.h` `MeshCluster`,
`WorldConverter::ClusterWorldMeshTriangles`, `SpatialBVH.h`). The world mesh lies in XZ grid sections, and each mesh's triangles are split into
~128-triangle spatial clusters by a BVH. The index buffer is reordered so every cluster is a contiguous range (the shadow indices are derived
after clustering, so they stay valid). Per view a compute pass culls the sections, then the meshes, then the clusters (a groupshared visibility
bit mask of up to 2,048 clusters per mesh; beyond that the rest join the last run unculled). A **run of contiguous visible clusters becomes one
indirect command**, so hidden clusters drop out without multiplying draws.

| stage | content | gate | gain (est.) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **F5-0** measurement (no behaviour change) | at the player defaults (savegame, harbour_docks, jungle, pub_interior): triangles / vertices submitted per view kind (main z, main solid, each cascade) and per mesh-size class (pipeline-statistics queries where offered: input primitives, clipping primitives, fragment invocations; otherwise index counts on the CPU); the share of each large mesh's triangles outside the frustum and back-facing (offline from cluster bounds + normal cones built from the mesh arena's copies); a triangle screen-size histogram (a debug pass or offline from captured positions); `-PerfFrame` GPU time of the depth pre-pass + cascades vs the pixel-heavy passes; memory of "all LODs resident" vs today. A 680M run if the hardware is available, otherwise extrapolate from the 7900 XTX and say so. | numbers in 7.x | decides F5-1..3 |
| **F5-1** cluster build | static meshes above a size threshold (level meshes first; set the threshold from F5-0): ~128-triangle spatial clusters (`meshopt_buildMeshlets` or a BVH split as GD3D11), the **mesh arena's copy** of the index range reordered so each cluster is contiguous; z pre-pass, solid pass and cascades all use the same reordered indices, so the solid pass's depth EQUAL stays invariant; per cluster: bounds + normal cone in a GPU cluster table (the GpuTable pattern); cached on disk keyed by mesh content | per sub-mesh, the index multiset is equal to the engine's; picture A/B 0 beyond noise; rt / ocean floor / captures still see valid offsets | none by itself; **built (7.33, `native.clusters`): index COPIES in cluster order (the engine's copy stays), ~130 ranges / 6,800 clusters / 6.7 MiB per spot, 3.6 ms builds on a worker, picture identical** |
| **F5-2** cluster cull (after F2c-4c; **built for the main z / solid, 7.34: 0 - 0.09 ms on the 7900 XTX, cascades not built**) | per view (main + cascades), per visible owned record of a clustered mesh: frustum + back-face cone per cluster, runs of visible clusters merged (cap of runs per record, else the whole range), args written into the F2c-4c arena, `DrawIndexedIndirectCount`; per-instance GPU LOD selection in the same pass (see above) | GPU-safety steps; read-back verify on check frames (a cluster drawn by the engine but culled by us = a false negative, must be 0 for the conservative tests); bursts while turning (no pop-in); sync validation; D3D12 GBV | vertex + raster work of hidden parts of large meshes (680M first) |
| **F5-3** HiZ occlusion | same-frame two-phase HiZ from the main depth pre-pass (section 2's F5 row), per cluster and per small mesh | as F5-2 + pop-in bursts | depends on occlusion (old probe: 8-24 % hidden) |

Only opaque / z / shadow-caster draws are clustered. Alpha-blended draws keep their engine order and their whole range.

## 14. S plan: our own shaders for the owned frame, measured for a bandwidth-limited iGPU (2026-11-08, S-0: measurement + planning, no feature code)

User direction (2026-11-07/08): Full owns the whole frame AND runs our own, optimized shaders; the target is a Radeon 680M (RDNA2, 12 CU, ~100 GB/s shared); the user
tests on it only when everything is done. Owned draws exist for static meshes, cascades, trees, actors, vegetation and billboards, also under FSR / frame generation /
GI / AO (7.21 - 7.45); they still run the **translated** engine shaders (D3D9 bytecode -> HLSL, scene ABI v2 variants). Keep the visual identity (sun flare, exposure,
vivid colours; new lighting adds, never bland / PBR-realistic); Wrapper stays faithful; DXIL parity; no unread outputs.

Setup: private copy `risen-test-vk-s0` (instance 43, lock `rvk-s0`); GPU timing in the shared copy with `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, `-PerfFrame` with
`RVK_PERF_FRAMES=24` (24 frames averaged per pass), one run at a time. Everything on that the owned chain has: the `-All` list of 7.43 (`owned_*`, clusters, cull,
`rt.table_entries`, `lighting.clustered` + `full_lit`), player defaults, savegame hold (`rvk_perfsave`, lod 2x) and rvkcompare spots jungle / pub_interior /
harbour_docks, 1600x900. **Temporary counters** (not for main): branch `s0-census-temp` (2 commits on 7f4fcc8): `RVK_S0_CENSUS=1` (per owned flush: items, buckets,
distinct VS+PS modules per view kind x draw kind, every 300 frames), `RVK_S0_STATS=1` (`VK_PIPELINE_CREATE_CAPTURE_STATISTICS_BIT_KHR` on every graphics pipeline,
`VK_KHR_pipeline_executable_properties`: VGPRs / SGPRs / scratch per stage, with the module names). Scripts and raw logs: `rvk-work/scratch/s0/` (`run.ps1`,
`queue*.ps1`, `analyze_s0.py`, `vgpr_classes.py`, `perfpass.py`, `mdtable.py`, `merge_census.py`, `static_stats.py`, logs `c_*.log` census, `pf_*.log` GPU).

**Limits, stated once.** (1) The only GPU here is an RX 7900 XTX (RDNA3, 1,536 VGPRs per SIMD lane): VGPR counts come from AMD's RDNA3 compile of the SPIR-V (the driver
reports `sub 0`: wave size unknown); an RDNA2 compile (gfx1035 = the 680M) differs by roughly +-10 - 20 %, and RDNA2's register file is 1,024 per lane: the occupancy
column below is a MODEL (waves per SIMD = min(16, 1024 / VGPRs rounded up to 8), wave32; a wave64 pixel shader halves it). **Radeon GPU Analyzer is not installed
here**: S-2's first act is `rga -s vk-spv-offline -c gfx1035` on the dumped modules (real 680M register / ISA numbers, no hardware needed). (2) All 680M milliseconds are
**extrapolations** from the XTX (the model of 7.22 item 4, front end x5 - 7, pixel ALU x15, bandwidth-bound x10; this section uses x6.5 / x15 / x10); read them as +- 2x. (3) Run-to-run
noise of a pass is large: the same jungle hold gave a solid pass of 1.84 / 1.82 / 1.29 ms in three runs (content phase at the perf frame), so only differences > 30 % are
claimed from single runs.

Findings: (a) **the pipeline count is not the problem**: the z and cascade passes already run 2 - 5 pipelines, the solid pass 5 - 20; 87 - 137 distinct command keys per
scene are vertex layouts / index buffers of the engine's meshes, so a z pass issues 82 - 129 indirect commands for 4 pipelines (the command count = vertex input x index
buffer, not shaders). (b) The solid pass owns only ~34 - 80 static items: its other draws are trees, vegetation, instanced, blended / no-z-write (7.43 table).

(a faithful port is not cheaper by itself); (2) **at the player defaults the lit pixel shader is the GI / pack variant at 219 VGPRs (model: 4 waves per SIMD on RDNA2, spills
in 18 modules)**: the XTX is insensitive to it (jungle hold with `materials.derive_from_specular=false` = plain variants: solid pass 1.82 vs 1.84 ms, same scene state), the 680M
will not be; (3) z / cascade shaders are tiny (PS 7 - 15, VS 15 - 17 VGPRs, scene form VS 55 for skinned: bone fetch).

`perfpass.py`; ms; "post" = the engine's 1600x900 full-screen effect passes with <= 10 draws):

| scene | main z | solid + alpha | cascades (per frame, stride 2) | post (1600x900) | water.rt | water.ocean | rt.scene + light.cluster + light.shadow | submission total |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| savegame (frame 6000) | 0.28 | 0.72 + 0.15 | 0.18 | 0.21 | 1.00 | 0.27 | 0.16 + 0.11 + - | 3.33 |
| jungle (4200) | 0.40 | 1.84 | 0.54 | 0.25 | 1.03 | 0.28 | 0.16 + 0.13 + 0.08 | 5.03 |
| pub_interior (4200) | 0.41 | 1.17 | 0.13 | 0.19 | 0.92 | 0.33 | 0.16 + 0.14 + 0.13 | 3.81 |
| harbour_docks (4200) | 0.36 | 1.13 | 0.12 | 0.22 | 0.83 | 0.30 | 0.19 + 0.12 + 0.05 | 3.59 |

Experiments on the jungle hold (single runs, noise +-20 - 30 %): 800x480 (27 % of the pixels): solid 1.50 (-18 %), z 0.30; FSR `performance` (25 %): solid 1.18 (-36 %), z 0.30,
`upscale.fsr` 0.33 ms; `-NoChain` (translated, not owned): z 0.56, solid 1.50 (z -28 % with the chain: the 600 separate draws against ~80 indirect commands, one run). So on the XTX
the solid pass is **~25 - 45 % pixel, the rest geometry / draw**; the pub (33 + 20 draws, 1.17 ms) is pixel-bound: an interior's lit pixels (normal map + specular + 3 lights) are
the case for the pixel shader.

**14.1.6 Bandwidth** [I: arithmetic from formats at 1600x900 = 1.44 M pixels, not measured traffic; the targets are in `names:` lines]: scene HDR RGBA16F 8 B, depth D24S8 4 B,
depth map R32F 4 B (a colour export of the z pass: read by the shadow mask chain, SSAO, DoF, water, FSR prep), shadow mask A8R8G8B8, refraction copy RGBA16F, cascades 3 x 2048x2048
DF24 (16.8 MB each), terrain shadow map 1024^2 G32R32F, G-buffer normal A2B10G10R10 + motion (4 B each, written only when read: already packed). Per frame: z ~17 MB (depth + R32F
x ~1.5 overdraw), solid ~55 MB (colour 8 + depth 4 + mask 4 + ~15 B of texture traffic per pixel x 1.2), cascades ~50 - 70 MB when drawn (clear + write of 3 maps), **the post chain
~275 MB** (12 full-resolution RGBA16F passes, read + write each), RT / water compute extra. ~430 MB per frame = 26 GB/s at 60 fps (13 GB/s at 30): inside the 680M's ~50 GB/s share
with the CPU, so **the opaque passes are not bandwidth-bound; the post chain is** (275 MB / 50 GB/s ~ 5.5 ms; measured on the XTX 0.19 - 0.25 ms x ~10 - 19 = 2 - 4.7 ms: consistent).

### 14.2 Findings that change the plan

1. **`debug.ubershader` makes ownership impossible today** (`d3d9/draw.cpp:633` split constants off, `owned_persist.cpp:41` / `owned_submit.cpp:39` refuse, `scene_draws.cpp:121` "the
   ubershader has no scene form"): the ported HLSL ubershader and the owned path have never run together. The shader text is ready (`RVK_SCENE_RECORD` branches in `ge_default.frag/vert.hlsl`,
   `shader/uber.cpp:60` emits the define); what is missing is the wiring (variant slot + pipeline key + gates).
2. The owned path's cost is **not pipeline switches** (2 - 20 per pass) but (a) the **lit pixel shader's registers** (105 plain / 124 scene / 219 GI-pack-cluster) and (b) the **geometry
   volume** (2.1 - 2.4 M triangles per frame in z + solid + cascades, 74 % of the solid pass on the XTX) and (c) the post chain's bandwidth.
3. The engine already marks 89 % of the pixel ALU `_pp`: half precision is the shader's own intent, not an approximation we invent.
4. 196 translated pipelines (L1) need ~13 ms of driver compile each on first sight (~2.5 s of worker time, hitches on a new area): the merge ladder (112 / 70 / 57) is a loading /
   cache win, not a frame-time win; with dynamic branches a merged shader allocates the MAX registers of its branches, so merging is capped by classes, not by macro count.

### 14.3 Where a custom shader beats the translated one on the 680M, ranked

Model [I]: 680M ms = XTX geometry-part x 6.5 + XTX pixel-part x 15 (post passes x 10); the solid pass's pixel share 35 % (savegame, jungle; range 25 - 48 %), 70 % in the pub.
Baseline raster time on the 680M by that model (z + solid + cascades + post, no RT / water compute): savegame ~13 ms, jungle ~26 ms, pub ~20 ms (+- 2x). Gains are ms per frame at
1600x900, 3 scenes savegame / jungle / pub:

| rank | opportunity | why on the 680M (numbers) | expected gain (ms) | picture |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Lit pixel shader diet**: real `half` for the `_pp` ALU (RDNA2 packed 16-bit math: 2x rate and half the VGPRs for those values), one lit function with the cluster light loop instead of the 3-light unroll + separate GI / pack / cluster / G-buffer variant modules, dead `PassData` fields out, VGPR targets plain <= 96, lit + pack + cluster <= 128 (from 124 / 219) | pixel part of the solid pass = 4.6 / 9.7 / 12.3 ms; occupancy 4 -> 8 waves for the variant that is the default, 89 % of the ALU in half; 5 fetches per pixel stay (latency hiding is what the registers buy) | -1.2..-1.8 / -2.4..-3.9 / -3.1..-4.9 (25 - 40 % of the PS time) | identical within fp16 (tolerance gate) |
| 2 | **Post chain on own shaders**: fuse bloom / DoF / tone map passes, R11G11B10 for intermediates, half-res where the engine's passes are | ~275 MB / frame, the one bandwidth-bound part: 12 full-res passes -> ~6, -40..-50 % bytes | -0.8..-1.1 / -1.0..-1.3 / -0.8..-1.0 | filter-for-filter port first, then fusion (DoF / bloom identity risk) |
| 3 | **Trees / leaves / grass / billboards on own shaders**: 16-byte pulled vertices, wind in half, cheaper far-field PS, distance thinning (Full may change the picture slightly) | instanced geometry is ~60 - 70 % of the jungle's geometry (473 k of 949 k z triangles), ~50 % of the savegame's (268 k of 775 k) | -0.3..-0.5 / -0.8..-1.3 / 0 (15 - 25 % of the instanced geometry) | grass density / LOD pop must pass the nets / jungle A/B |
| 4 | **z / cascade depth-only**: opaque casters without a pixel shader (the R32F depth map written by one full-screen copy when a consumer reads it), cut-outs with the same texkill threshold, PS-less early-Z | z + cascades = 3.0 / 6.1 / 3.6 ms on the 680M (front end + ROP); PS launch + 4 B export per fragment dropped, foliage overdraw | -0.2..-0.5 / -0.5..-0.9 / -0.3..-0.5 | identical (same triangles, same threshold) |
| 5 | **Command collapse** (vertex pulling from `MeshArena` + one index buffer): indirect commands 163 -> ~25 (savegame), 250 -> ~30 (jungle); VS scene-record reads (+21 VGPR) become a leaner own VS | 1 - 3 us per small indirect draw on a one-shader-engine front end [I] | -0.14..-0.4 / -0.4..-0.7 / ~0 (plus ~0.1 CPU) | identical |
| 6 | Shadow mask filter (post_SSM / enhanced mask 3 draws: 0.08 ms on the XTX = ~1.2 ms) in half with a cheaper kernel at the Low preset | pass is ALU on a full-res mask | -0.3..-0.5 each | PCF look must stay (shadows.md) |
| 7 | Permutation merge (L3 / L4: 197 -> 57 - 70 pipelines), fewer interpolants (6.5 of 9 read; packed half normal / tangent) | GPU ~0 (registers = max of the branches); worker compile 2.5 s -> ~0.7 s, smaller caches, no hitches on a new area | ~0 per frame; loading / hitch win | identical |
| - | not S, noted: cascade budget (map size 1024 - 2048, stride, range; F3-3 cached casters), RT tier off on iGPU (water.rt alone is 0.8 - 1.0 ms XTX = 12 - 15 ms), render scale | cascades 50 - 70 MB / drawn frame; RT passes are ALU | settings / presets | - |
| - | rejected by the numbers: packed G-buffer writes (already A2B10G10R10 + RG16F, written only when read: < 0.05 ms), a custom z pre-pass vertex stream (z VS is 15 VGPRs; vertex bytes ~14 MB / frame) | | | |

Sum of 1 - 6 on the model: savegame -2.7..-4.4 ms of ~13, jungle -5.3..-8.3 of ~26, pub -4.5..-6.9 of ~20 (a third of the raster time; the pixel shader alone a quarter): the first
two items are about two thirds of it.

### 14.4 Stage plan S-1 .. S-9 (Full only; each stage behind a Debug setting, `native.own_shaders` as the umbrella, default off until S-9)

Rules for every stage: (1) the translated path stays as the per-draw fallback for any class an own shader does not cover and for devices without `shaderFloat16` / 16-bit ops
(optional capability with a parity fallback to float), so Wrapper and unsupported hardware are unchanged; (2) both backends: `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded | corpus
corpus --scene --uber` 0 failures, `rvk_rhitest` parity on Vulkan and D3D12 (DXC SM 6.6, `native 16-bit` needs SM 6.2 + the options-4 bit); (3) the owned checks stay at
0: `DIFFERING`, `MISS`, `split_verify`, GPU-assisted validation first run (GPU safety rule); (4) no unread outputs: a stage may not add a target / output nobody reads;
(5) picture gates in two tiers: **faithful** (EQUAL: `rvk_shadereq` 183 / 183 + leafs, bit-exact) for porting steps, **intentional** (tolerance + A/B pictures + the
visual-identity check: luma / hue table of `tools/water_stats.py`, sun flare, exposure, colours on pub_interior / harbour_docks / jungle / monastery_hall) for optimizations.

| stage | scope | gates | risk | expected gain |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **S-1a** own PS on every owned draw (faithful platform) | wire the HLSL ubershader (`ge_default.frag.hlsl`) as the scene-form pixel shader of the owned path: a variant slot beside `SceneVs` / the GI / G-buffer slots, pipeline key, `uberTaken` accepted by `sceneDecide`, the `!ubershader_` refusals of `draw.cpp:633` / `owned_persist.cpp:41` / `owned_submit.cpp:39` replaced by "uber has a scene form", `uber.cpp` already defines `RVK_SCENE_RECORD`; covers the PS of static meshes, skinned, instanced, trees, vegetation, billboards, cascades (all `PS_Default` / `PS_DefaultZPass`: 175 of 208 requests, 84 %; leafs PS = S-1b) | `rvk_shadereq` / `ubershader_check.py` in scene form (183 / 183 EQUAL, 16 trials), `dxil-check corpus --scene --uber`, rhitest parity (ubershader draw in scene form, both backends), check frames DIFFERING 0, `-Validation` + GPU-AV, in-game A/B `native.own_shaders` toggled (7.44's method: toggle pairs vs same-state pairs), **VGPR not above the translated scene form (124) and `-PerfFrame` solid not worse** | wiring of three gates; the uber is +19 VGPR over the translation today (S-2 pays it back) | ~0 (platform); share of owned PS on own shaders 100 % |
| **S-1b** own VS + leaf PS (done: 14.9) | `ge_default.vert.hlsl` (proven by `shader_equiv.py --vs`) as the scene VS for static / skinned / morph / SpeedTree / vegetation / billboard (macros ST_*, VEGETATION, BILLBOARD already in it), `ge_default_leafs` PS port (shader-permutations.md 11) | as S-1a + `shader_equiv.py --vs` 17 / 17 in scene form, tree checks of 7.39 / 7.40 / 7.42 (rows, counts, templates 0), nets spots | the tree flush's own templates / ramps (7.25) | ~0 |
| **S-2** lit PS diet (picture-faithful within fp16) | **first: install Radeon GPU Analyzer, dump the modules, `rga -c gfx1035` = real 680M VGPR / occupancy / ISA**; then (a) real `half` in the port (DXC `-enable-16bit-types`, the source's `half` / `_pp` set; specular `pow`, fog, depth math stay float), (b) split ZPass / Default / cut-out entries so no dead code is compiled, (c) PassData slimmed, texture fetches deduplicated where the graph samples one image twice (47 diffuse graphs contain repeats), (d) packed half interpolants | `rvk_shadereq` tolerance mode (<= 2 / 255 on 99.9 % of 8-bit outputs, < 1 % pixels at 2), pictures A/B refcompare Full vs Full (noise-level), banding check on dark gradients (pub_interior, monastery_hall), rhitest parity with the tolerance, **VGPR targets from RGA (plain <= 96)** | fp16 banding / specular sparkle; the driver's handling of 16-bit on RDNA2 must be measured, not assumed | rank 1: -25..-40 % of the PS time on the 680M |
| **S-3** one lit function | lighting in the own PS: ambient SH + sun (+ cascade term from the mask) + the cluster-table point lights (the `RVK_MV_CLUSTER` loop, `rvk_light_model.hlsli`) in half accumulators + lightmap + GI term + derived gloss (`kWordDerived`) computed once; the separate plain / gi / gbuf / cluster / scene modules (5 per shader today: 163 + 134 + 55 modules in the census) collapse into one per class | refcompare Full (own lighting) vs Full: spots equal to noise except the known by-design lamp-light difference (reference-compare.md 11.2); luma / hue table within 1 %; `gi-reference.ps1` quick re-measure; lamp shadows, RT shadows, material RT, FSR + G-buffer variants (7.44 gates) | the interaction of cluster lights, pack word, G-buffer MRT in one shader; VGPR = max of branches: flags only as scalar (per-draw uniform) branches | VGPR 219 -> <= 128 (occupancy 4 -> 8 on the model); with S-2 the rank-1 gain |
| **S-4** classes + command collapse (14.12: (b) built, (a) not) | (a) merge permutations to the L3 / L4 classes (z / cascade: static, skinned, morph x opaque / masked; lit: static / skinned / tree / leaf / vegetation x opaque / alpha) with record flags (light counts, normal map, specular, lightmap, cut-out as uniform branches), graph forms as a small recipe table; (b) vertex pulling from `MeshArena` (no vertex input state, one index buffer; D3D12 `SV_VertexID` base-vertex rule: `vertexOffset = 0`, absolute indices) | `bucket key clash` 0, rhitest `testIndirectPull`, pipelines per frame and compile time logged (`d3d9: pipelines built`), commands per frame (82 / 29 / 41 -> single digits per view), picture identical (same triangles), shadereq on every class | VGPR growth by merging: keep a class when max VGPR rises > 10 % | rank 5 (-0.14..-0.7 ms) + the loading win of rank 7 |
| **S-5** vegetation / trees / billboards (thinning built, the rest measured and not built: 14.14) | own SpeedTree / grass / billboard VS + PS: 16-byte pulled vertices, half wind, far-field grass PS, distance thinning tied to `lod.view`; intentional picture change (small) | nets (`net8_*`), jungle / swamp A/B + motion bursts (upscaling.md 12 flicker), `rvk_rare` billboards, F7's 7.40 / 7.42 checks | pop-in of thinned grass (burst test), FSR flicker | rank 3 (jungle -0.8..-1.3) |
| **S-6** depth-only z / cascades | PS-less opaque z in the cascades and the main pass; the R32F depth map by a copy only while a consumer reads it; cut-out variants keep the texkill threshold; cluster cull in the cascades if still off (F5 follow-up) | `shadow-compare.ps1` (engine vs enhanced cascades), the R32F consumers (FSR prep, SSAO / DoF, water, shadow mask chain) pixel-equal, `rvk_d3d9test` depth cases, no-stencil rule | the consumers of the depth map that read it mid-pass | rank 4 (-0.2..-0.9) |
| **S-7** post chain on own shaders | the engine's effect filters (native post, engine-frame/README.md 15, `native/effect_ops_post.cpp`) re-written filter-for-filter in HLSL (EQUAL first), then fused (bloom + DoF + tone map) with R11G11B10 intermediates | per filter EQUAL (the post units' compare), then refcompare post spots, exposure / adaptation identical, sun flare / streaks unchanged | DoF / bloom identity (visual identity is exactly here), FSR / frame-generation input order | rank 2 (-0.8..-1.3) |
| **S-8** (done: 14.11) shadow mask + iGPU preset | own mask filter in half, a "680M" quality preset (cascade size 1024 / 2048, stride, range, RT off, render scale) from the measured table | shadows.md A/B, `water-compare`-style spots, preset table in the overlay | settings audit (settings-audit.md) | rank 6 + the non-S rows |
| **S-9** default on + the 680M day | `native.own_shaders` default on in Full (translated fallback kept), RGA numbers of the final modules, the user's 680M test with the preset; update PLAN.md / CLAUDE.md | all gates above green on Vulkan + D3D12, the soak of 7.43 (all settings on) with the own shaders | the real hardware differs from the model | the sum of 1 - 7 |

Order: S-1a (platform, 0 gain, unblocks everything) -> S-2 + S-3 (the two thirds; start with RGA so the targets are real) -> S-1b -> S-4 -> S-5 / S-6 (independent) -> S-7 -> S-8 -> S-9.
S-1a is first because it turns the owned path onto our own pixel shader for **all owned draw kinds at once** (every material PS of static, skinned, instanced, trees, vegetation and
billboards is the same `PS_Default` text): up to ~4,500 of the 5,944 draws of a savegame frame (76 %: vegetation 2,510, trees 1,282, static 640, skinned 32, billboards 52) are owned, and the lit pixel shader is where the 680M's time is.

### 14.4b Roadmap after full ownership: a load-time Full data layout (user decision 2026-11-10, queued, not started)

End state (user): **Full makes no engine-composed calls and has no fallbacks**: every draw of the frame is owned (collected, skinned, culled, drawn by us with our own
shaders). Once that holds, the vertex data no longer has to serve the engine's draws, the composed forms or the input assembler, and Full can own its mesh layout:

1. **Load-time choice.** The render mode becomes a start-up choice for the data layout: Full builds its own mesh store when meshes load and keeps only what its own shaders read
   (no second copy of data nobody reads: the no-unread-outputs rule, performance.md 32). Today Wrapper <-> Full switch at the next frame boundary (`renderer.mode` `restartGroups = {0, 1, 1}`,
   `HalDevice::applyStaticOps`; the F10 A/B and the `-Commands 'N: set renderer.mode full'` test arms use it): a load-time layout makes Full a restart choice like Original,
   so in-run Wrapper / Full A/B has to move to two runs (or keep the switch in Debug builds only). Decide when this starts.
2. **Pack what is possible.** With no composed draw of the same geometry left, the bit-identical-position constraint (shader-permutations.md 12: the z-equal colour pass
   against another form) is gone, so positions may be quantized (cluster-relative 16-bit, the F5 clusters' boxes) as long as every pass of a geometry uses the same decode;
   normals / tangents as 16-bit octahedral pairs, UVs as half where the range allows, colours 8-bit. The RT scene's BLAS builds must then read the same decoded positions
   (or a float copy kept only while the RT tier is on).
3. **Split position / attribute streams** for the depth-only passes (main z + cascades fetch ~18 of ~26 MB per frame as positions alone, S-4 measurement 14.12).
   The exact float32 position-only stream (no picture change) may come earlier, after bone-history motion (14.13), if the 680M capture shows the z / cascade passes
   vertex-fetch bound.

Re-evaluate the whole item with the user's 680M RGP captures (Vulkan + D3D12) once the owned path covers every draw; until then attribute packing is parked (solid pass:
~4 - 5 MB / frame saving, ~0.05 ms on the model, plus a second mesh copy in shared RAM while composed draws still need the original).

### 14.5 Not measured / open

1. No RDNA2 compile and no 680M run exist: the occupancy column and every 680M ms are models (install RGA first; the user's hardware test is the final check). Wave size of the
   driver's pixel shaders (32 or 64) is unknown (`sub 0`): a wave64 compile halves every occupancy figure above.
2. The solid pass's pixel / geometry split is bracketed (25 - 48 %) by two noisy resolution experiments; a per-draw-kind GPU timing (veg / trees vs static) was not taken (the cap
   of the timestamps is the pass).
3. The trees / vegetation / billboard flushes were not given VS / PS module census lines (only the static + skinned `ownedFlush`); their pipelines appear in the pipeline
   statistics (412 PS modules) but are not attributed per flush.
4. `materials.derive_from_specular` off vs on shows no XTX difference (1.82 vs 1.84 ms): the 219-VGPR variant's real cost is a 680M question (RGA).
5. Fp16 on RDNA2 through DXC / SPIR-V (`RelaxedPrecision` vs native `float16_t`) and through DXIL min16float on D3D12 must be measured in S-2 (gain assumed 2x rate on packed ops).
6. The cascade budget, F3-3 and the RT tier presets are not S items but are larger than ranks 4 - 7 on the 680M (cascades 50 - 70 MB / drawn frame, `water.rt` 12 - 15 ms by the model).

### 14.6 S-1a result: the HLSL ubershader as the scene-form pixel shader of owned draws (2026-11-08, code commit 5526097 on main f296100; no new setting, settings count unchanged, defaults unchanged)

**Setting: `debug.ubershader` is reused** (Debug, default off), not a new `native.own_shaders`: the switch already means "material pixel shaders from the HLSL port, translated fallback for what it cannot build", the owned path needed only the refusals removed, and the umbrella `native.own_shaders` of 14.4 is a S-9 decision (one more setting would also touch `tests/settings_api_tests.cpp`'s count for no gain now). The setting now also works at run time (A/B F10, `-Commands 'N: set debug.ubershader true'`): `Device::resetUberVariants` drains the variant workers and forgets the variants of every material pixel shader (old modules live on in pipelines); retained packets / owned derivations notice through `CachedDraw::Variant::uberAsked`.

**What changed.** (1) `draw.cpp`: split constants no longer switch off for the ubershader (it reads c# through `RVK_PSC` windows like a translated shader; its reads are a subset of the translated shader's: the unused `RVK_LOAD_SHADER_DATA` loads are dead-code-eliminated, so the reflection's window extents suffice), `replayAllowed` no longer refuses it, a draw that took the ubershader module (or a variant) is retained (`captureOk_`), the plain module is requested only for draws no GI / pack / G-buffer variant is wanted for (the variants are ubershader modules by themselves: their providers already set `TranslateOptions::uber`; 148 needless plain modules less per run). (2) `owned_persist.cpp` / `owned_submit.cpp`: the `ubershader_` refusals are gone. (3) `scene_draws.cpp`: `uberTaken` no longer refuses the scene form; the plain scene slots (`ScenePs` / `ScenePs2`) are built from the ubershader (`variantWorker`: `uberBaseOptions` when the request is covered). `variantModule` falls back to the translated scene form when the ubershader build fails (`uberFallbacks_`). (4) Counters: rvk.log `native: owned shaders (5 s)` = per draw kind, owned items and scene-form decisions by pixel shader form: uber / other (not a material shader) / leaf (S-1b) / class skipped (`debug.ubershader_skip`) / request unknown / build failed.

**Gates.**
* Shader equivalence in scene form: `python tools/ubershader_check.py out/matsrc/*.jsonl [--scene | --scene2]` (new: `rvk_shadereq --scene / --scene2` builds one DrawRecord (+ ViewBlock2) from the random constants; the translated side = `rvk_shadertool export corpus game-shaders-scene --scene` / `game-shaders-scene2 --scene2`; `test/eq_varyings.vert` with `RVK_SCENE_RECORD` [+ `RVK_SCENE_VIEW`]): plain 259 EQUAL, scene v1 259 EQUAL, scene v2 259 EQUAL (63 NO-HLSL: not in this corpus export, 4 unsupported entries; the earlier 183 was a smaller capture). Variants (GI / G-buffer / cluster) are not compared by `shadereq` (they need a frame block and extra targets); they are covered by rhitest (hooks) + dxil-check + the in-game check frames and A/B.
* `rvk_rhitest` scene cases (new: z pass masked, G-buffer + GI hooks, v1 and v2) on Vulkan and D3D12 with `--validation` and `--sync-validation`: all `scene variant (...)` PASS, 0 differ. The one Vulkan `--sync-validation` finding (swapchain image WRITE_AFTER_READ vs `vkAcquireNextImageKHR` in the harness swapchain, 2 errors) is **pre-existing** (identical on main's binary).
* `rvk_shadertool dxil-check uber out/matsrc/*.jsonl` (337 requests x 8 variants incl. scene v1 / v2): 2,696 of 2,696 pass. `rvk_tests` 321 / 0.
* **VGPRs** (RDNA3 compile, pipeline statistics, 3 spots, `-All`, player defaults; same hash x variant, translated -> ubershader): lit PS_Default **scene v2 (the owned form) 117.6 -> 117.4**; scene v1 (composed scene draws) 129.7 -> 138.0 (+8, not on the owned path); z pass scene v2 17.4 -> 19.3, v1 13.8 -> 17.0 (tiny absolute values); **the GI / pack / cluster variants that the player defaults run on lit draws: scene v2 248.0 -> 194.7, scene v1 250.7 -> 192.2, plain 198.8 -> 140.9** (the 10 - 16 spilling variant modules per class no longer spill). The owned lit form is not above the translated one, the default lit form is 22 - 29 % smaller; the increases are the v1 plain form (+6 %) and the z pass (+2 VGPR). (The pipeline-statistics hook was a temporary patch from `s0-census-temp`, reverted, not committed.)
* Check frames: DIFFERING 0 / MISS 0 in every run below.

**Share of owned items on the ubershader** (rvk.log, last window of each run; `leaf` = `ge_default_leafs.fx`, S-1b): savegame hold (all owned settings, FSR performance + frame generation + GI + AO): static meshes 89,373 of 89,373 (100 %), skinned 2,833 / 2,833, vegetation 6,960 / 6,960, SpeedTrees 23,087 of 37,727 (61 %; the other 14,640 are leaf pixel shaders); walk: static 100 %, skinned 100 %, trees 23,868 / 38,028 (63 %), vegetation 100 %; turning burst: static 100 %, trees 23,439 / 34,143 (69 %); spots: static 100 %. Nothing else falls back: "other / skipped / unknown / failed" are 0 everywhere, `translated builds after an ubershader failure 0`. The owned share of ops is unchanged by the switch (pub_interior 63.8 %, jungle 78.6 %, harbour_docks 66.9 %, monastery_hall 69.8 %).

**Pipelines / buckets / commands before -> after** (pub_interior + jungle + harbour_docks, `-All`): buckets = indirect packets 64,200 -> 64,200, items 177,000 -> 177,000, scene indirect calls per window 6,480 -> 6,480: **identical** (a bucket's key = vertex input x dynamic state x index buffer x pipeline; the pipeline set did not change). Pixel-shader modules built over the run: 432 -> 579 before the "no plain module for variant draws" fix (148 extra), 350 / 400 in the final soaks (hold / 4 spots), 0 failures; 350 modules take 8.7 s on the three workers with a warm SPIR-V cache (cold DXC ~40 - 60 ms each, as for the translated ones). A run-time toggle resets ~120 pixel shaders and the owned share is back within one 5 s window.

**Picture A/B in one hold** (`debug.ubershader` toggled every 300 frames, 7 toggle pairs vs 33 same-state pairs, mean absolute difference per pixel, `cmp_ab.py`): pub_interior FSR performance 0.149 vs 0.817 (floor 0.127); jungle FSR performance 3.59 vs 4.01; harbour_docks FSR + GI + AO 0.461 vs 0.560; monastery_hall FSR + GI + AO 0.322 vs 1.057; pub_interior FSR + GI + AO 0.153 vs 0.933; jungle without FSR 1.59 vs 4.76 (wind): every toggle pair is inside the same-state range (noise = eye adaptation, wind, jitter), none bit-identical on either side.

**GPU per pass** (XTX, `-PerfFrame` 24-frame mean, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy; solid / main z, ms; single runs, noise +-20 - 30 %): savegame hold 0.790 -> 0.824 / 0.256 -> 0.243, total 3.652 -> 3.716; jungle solid 1.827 -> 1.749, total 5.921 -> 5.791; pub_interior solid 0.442 -> 0.435 (total 2.139 -> 2.291 is `light.shadow` 1.18 -> 1.30, not an owned pass); harbour_docks solid 0.472 -> 0.519 (+ mid 0.580 -> 0.603), total 3.201 -> 3.308. Not worse beyond noise on the XTX (the register saving is a 680M question). **CPU** (savegame hold, three interleaved rounds, shared copy, engine + HAL ms per frame): off 7.77 / 7.52 / 7.49, on 9.54 (one outlier window, sd 2.2) / 7.43 / 7.91: no difference beyond noise; the packet cache replays 97 % in both.

**Soaks** (private copy `risen-test-vk-s1`, instance 44, lock `rvk-s1`; all owned settings + `debug.ubershader` + FSR performance + frame generation + GI + AO): first run `-Validation` + GPU-assisted on pub_interior (0 errors); then GPU-assisted spots pub_interior + jungle PASS (only AMD's frame-generation `rw_output` format findings, 10 errors / 12 VUID lines as in 7.44); savegame hold PASS 0 errors; `rvk_rtwalk` walk 1 PASS 0; walk 2 (+ `shadows.rt=near`, `rt_skin_verify`) PASS 0; turning burst plain PASS (peak 5,146 live records), with `-Validation` PASS (only the `rw_output` findings); spots pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks / monastery_hall PASS; hero_idle + pub_idle PASS. No device loss, no stage turned itself off.

**Open.** (1) SpeedTree leaf pixel shaders (39 % of owned tree items) stay translated until S-1b (`uberSupports` excludes `ge_default_leafs.fx`; the port exists: `uberSupportsFile`). (2) Variants are checked by rhitest / dxil-check / in-game only, not by `shadereq`. (3) The lit v1 plain form is +8 VGPR, the z pass +2: S-2's diet should start from the v2 numbers. (4) No D3D12 game run was made (rhitest parity on D3D12 only). `rvk_d3d9test` PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan and D3D12, `--validation` and `--sync-validation`.

### 14.7 S-2 / S-3 result: real gfx1035 register numbers, half precision, lazy GI / cluster, the table wired into the ubershader (2026-11-08, code commits 6be9c60 + 5211284; no new setting, defaults unchanged: `debug.ubershader` stays Debug / off)

**Radeon GPU Analyzer.** RGA 2.14 (the Radeon Developer Tool Suite in `B:\A\Downloads`) lists RDNA3+ only; **RGA 2.9.1** (`rga-windows-x64-2.9.1.zip`, GitHub GPUOpen-Tools/radeon_gpu_analyzer releases; unpack into its own directory) is the last release with `gfx1035` (680M, RDNA2). `rga -s vk-spv-offline -c gfx1035 --frag <module>.spv --isa .. -a ..` compiles the renderer's own SPIR-V (the modules of the game's `bin\rvk\cache\spirv`) with AMD's offline compiler: wave32 ISA (`vcc_lo`), `USED_VGPRs`, SGPRs, spills, scratch, ISA bytes. It is the offline compiler, not the installed driver (RGA says so itself): the numbers are the compiler's, not the 680M driver's. New tools: `scripts/rga-survey.ps1` (a directory of modules -> CSV, parallel), `scripts/rga-quick.ps1` (sample modules from the working tree's shaders through `RVK_SHADER_DIR` + `rvk_shadertool dxil-check uber-spv`, then RGA), `tools/rga_summary.py` (per variant family: mean / p90 / max VGPRs, spill, scratch, RDNA2 waves per SIMD = min(16, 1024 / VGPRs rounded up to 8); `-b/-a` pairs). **No module of any form spills or uses scratch** (0 of 756 / 526).

**Numbers (gfx1035, in-game modules, mean VGPRs; waves per SIMD in brackets).** S-1a modules (756, `_u309f55b2`) vs the final modules (526, `_u35ecb4a0h`), paired by shader hash x family:

| form | n | S-1a | final | change |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| plain lit (no hooks) | 58 | 77.6 [11.6] | 77.1 | -0.5 % |
| scene v1 (composed scene draws) | 65 | 121.9 | 122.8 | +0.8 % |
| **scene v2 (the owned form), plain** | 39 | 112.0 [8.8] | 114.1 [8.8] | +1.9 % |
| plain, GI + cluster | 104 | 141.1 [6.8] | 150.3 [6.3] | +6.5 % |
| plain, G-buffer + GI + cluster | 120 | 171.9 [5.5] | 171.5 [5.5] | -0.3 % |
| **scene v2, GI + cluster** | 12 | 178.8 [5.3] | 157.2 [6.1] | -12.1 % |
| **scene v2, G-buffer + GI + cluster** | 37 | 191.4 [4.9] | 182.9 [5.1] | -4.5 % |

Maxima 218 -> 214 (scene v2, G-buffer). **The targets (plain <= 96, variants <= 128) are not met**: plain scene v2 is ~114 (max 157), the variants 157 - 183. Reading: (1) real half cut the plain / scene v2 forms by only 3 - 7 % in the offline sample (stride-6 sample, 55 requests: plain 88.9 -> 83.0, scene v2 101.5 -> 98.2): the AMD compiler keeps scalar half values in whole VGPRs and packs only vector ops (`v_pk_*_f16`), and the ISA grows by the conversions (+20 - 30 % bytes); (2) the S-1a variant numbers were **too low**: the ubershader never read the light table (below), so the cluster code was dead and dropped; the final modules do that work and land at the same registers; (3) `--livereg` of a G-buffer + cluster module shows 150 - 195 live VGPRs through the whole GI lookup (28-register sums of two cascades + probe loads) and the cluster selection, not one peak. Moving the GI lookup to the ambient term and back to the top gave -9 VGPRs offline in the proxy defines but **no gain with the game's real defines** (plain G-buffer 164 vs 171.5; scene v2 182.1 vs 182.9; GI scene v2 159.8 vs 157.2): the top placement (the original order) was kept; (4) storing `rvk_gi` as half4 changed nothing (identical ISA): tried, reverted. Not done, would cut registers: a probe lookup as weights-first + accumulate-once (peak 28 + 16 instead of 56 + the loads), the cluster result kept as indices instead of 12 float4, or the GI / cluster terms in a compute pass into an image the shader samples (a different architecture).

**Half precision (S-2).** `shaders/material/ge_default.frag.hlsl`: the lit core (tangent frame, normals, light terms, accumulators, ambient SH, finish, vertex colour, opacity) is `rvkh` = real `half` with `RVK_HALF16` (DXC `-enable-16bit-types`: SPIR-V `Float16`, DXIL native 16-bit ops), else `float` (bit-identical to before: 259 / 259 EQUAL). Kept float: world position / light distance (squares exceed half's range), depth, uv, the specular exponent path (`pow(dot(R, L), power x 128)` amplifies base error), the 0.45 / sqrt gamma, the fog, `Fresnel` (1e-5 floor). Texture-graph expressions (`XFX_PS_*`) are float views of the half fields, so the engine's expression text compiles unchanged. Host: `rhi::DeviceCaps::shaderFloat16` (Vulkan `shaderFloat16` + `shaderInt16`, D3D12 `Native16BitShaderOpsSupported`), `Device::uberHalf()` (capability and `RVK_UBER_HALF` not `0`; the float build is the fallback and the A/B), `dxc::Request::half16` (also defines `RVK_HALF16`, part of the cache key), module cache names carry an `h`. **DXC finding:** `col.rgb *= x` / `.rgb +=` on a `half4` changed the alpha channel of 40 MASKED + ALPHABLEND shaders in the 16-bit build (relative difference 1.0, found by the tolerance gate; the SPIR-V validated): the finish now computes on a `half3` temporary and builds the `float4` once.

**Tolerance gate (defined here).** `tools/ubershader_check.py --half --tol 0.004` (+ `--scene2`): per pixel `|a - b| / (1 + max(|a|, |b|)) <= 0.004` on the 2x2 RGBA32F outputs of the ubershader (16 bit) vs the translated game shader (float), 16 random constant / varying / texture trials. 0.004 x (1 + value) is <= 1/255 at 0 and <= 2/255 at white; the gamma (pow 0.45) is inside the shader, so the compared value is display-referred. Measured over 259 requests: max 0.0037, p95 0.0016, median 0.00018 (plain and scene v2 identical); all 259 pass. Float builds are still EQUAL at the default 1e-4: plain 259 / 259, scene v2 259 / 259 (scene v1 not re-run; the 63 NO-HLSL are outside the corpus export as in 14.6).

**S-3: what was done, what was not.** (a) `rvkMvPrologue` is split into `rvkMvBegin` (position, normal), `rvkMvAmbientNow` (GI probes + AO; loads the engine's ambient registers after the lookup: 32 VGPRs fewer live in it) and `rvkMvClusterNow`; the translated shaders still call the old one (same order); the ubershader runs the cluster selection right before the point-light loop. (b) **A bug S-1a left: the ubershader computed the Full-owned light table (`rvk_cl`, `RVK_MV_CLUSTER`) and never read it**: its `g_PointLight_*` macros read the engine's registers (the translated path replaces them through `constOverrides`). With `lighting.clustered` and `debug.ubershader`, the table's lamp lights were missing, and the 14.6 A/B ran without them. `ge_default.hlsli` now reads `rvk_cl` under `RVK_MV_CLUSTER` (check: uber (half) vs translated spots differ 0.56 / 0.46 / 0.89 mean abs per 255 at pub_interior / monastery_hall / harbour_docks, the size of run-to-run noise). (c) **One module per class (the collapse) was not built.** A merged module has the registers of its widest branch: the plain forms (77 - 114 VGPRs, 8.8 - 11.6 waves) would take the variants' 157 - 183 (5 - 6 waves) on every draw, also with GI / AO / G-buffer off at run time; the variants' outputs differ by pipeline (MRT count) anyway. Module counts of a soak: plain 174, GI 118, G-buffer 121 + 23, scene forms 70 + 64 + 52 + 50 + 47 + 37 (756; 526 in the later, smaller soak). The collapse would roughly halve the compiles and cost occupancy: not worth it until the variants fit <= 128.

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 321 / 0; `rvk_shadertool dxil-check uber` (337 requests x 8 variants) 2,696 / 2,696 for float and for `--half`; `dxil-check corpus corpus --gbuf / --gbuf-motion / --gi` no failures (the split prologue is shared with the translated variants; `--pbr` not re-run); `rvk_rhitest --validation` PASS on both backends (0 differ), `--sync-validation` only the 2 pre-existing swapchain-acquire errors of 14.6; `rvk_d3d9test --backend vulkan|d3d12 --validation` PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors; `rvk_vktest --passes` PASS. Soaks (private copy `risen-test-vk-s1`, instance 44, lock `rvk-s1`; all owned settings + `debug.ubershader` + FSR performance + frame generation + GI + AO; logs `rvk-work/scratch/s2/`): first `-Validation` + GPU-assisted on pub_interior + jungle PASS (only AMD frame generation's `rw_output` findings), savegame hold, walks 1 / 2 (+ `shadows.rt=near`, `rt_skin_verify`), turning burst with `-Validation`, the 4 spots, hero_idle + pub_idle: all PASS, `DIFFERING` / `MISS` 0, ubershader failures 0, no half module failed. One hero_idle / pub_idle run turned `native.owned_shadows` off by itself (3 shadow draws "vertex input / dynamic state" differing on a check frame: the draws' state, not a shader); an identical rerun was clean: a flake of the actor shadow check, not reproduced. No D3D12 game run (rhitest / d3d9test only, as in 14.6).

**Picture.** One run per mode of the 4 rvkcompare spots with the same settings (half / `RVK_UBER_HALF=0` / translated), mean abs difference per 255 of burst frames, same-state b0 vs b1 in brackets: pub_interior half vs float 0.74 (0.12), vs translated 0.56; harbour_docks 0.20 (0.21) / 0.89; monastery_hall 1.04 (0.35) / 0.46; jungle 12.3 / 15.1 (2.6), luma 52.6 vs 44.7 / 50.0: the SpeedTree / wind / streaming state differs between runs (the tree silhouettes of the three frames differ), the ground and lighting match: **not usable as a half A/B**. The same-run toggle method of 14.6 cannot flip half (a build property of the module). Visual identity (sun flare, exposure, colours) is untouched by construction: only the ubershader's lit arithmetic changes, only under the Debug `debug.ubershader`; no shot review beyond the jungle side by side.

**GPU, XTX (`-PerfFrame` + `RVK_PERF_FRAMES=24`, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy and lock per run, three runs per cell interleaved translated / uber float / uber half; solid pass = the 1600x900 scene pass, ms):** savegame hold (frame 6000): 0.963 (0.983 / 0.915 / 0.992), 0.890 (0.914 / 0.877 / 0.880), 0.897 (0.890 / 0.895 / 0.905); main z 0.266 / 0.270 / 0.248; with `vulkan.low_bandwidth=on` (one run): 0.908 / 0.881 / 1.017. jungle 1.394 / 1.195 / 1.430 (single runs 1.1 - 1.7: noise above the effect); pub_interior 0.836 / 0.841 / 0.972 (one run per cell hit a loading frame, 0.31). Reading: the ubershader is not slower than the translated pass (hold -7 %); half = float on the XTX within noise (no gain expected: RDNA3 runs these ops at the 32-bit rate). **680M extrapolation (14.2's model, pixel part x 15):** no measured basis for a gain: registers did not move (plain scene v2 114 -> 114 = 8.8 waves, variants 5 - 6 waves) and the half code adds ~20 % ISA bytes of conversions; 14.3's 25 - 40 % assumed packed half halving the registers, which the compiler did not do. What may remain on the real GPU is RDNA2's double-rate `v_pk_*_f16` on the vector ops (not visible in a register count, unmeasured without the machine). Treat S-2 as capability + correctness, gain unproven: the 680M day (S-9) decides whether `RVK_UBER_HALF` stays on.

**Open.** (1) variants <= 128 VGPRs (restructure above); (2) the collapse S-3(c); (3) a run-time half toggle for a same-run A/B or the 680M comparison; (4) leaf pixel shaders (S-1b) stay translated and float; (5) scene v1 half tolerance and `corpus --pbr` not re-run; (6) the actor-shadow check flake.

### 14.7b S-2b result: the ubershader variants at fewer registers on gfx1035 (2026-11-08, code 5825563 + 6fbc43f; no new setting, defaults unchanged)

**What changed (shaders only + one text in `enh/material_variant.cpp`).** (1) `rvkGiLookupLean` (rvk_gi.hlsli): the 8 probe weights of a cascade first (8 floats), the normalising / blend factors folded into them, then ONE accumulation into a single `sum[7]` (was: a[7] + b[7] + regs[7] live). Same cascade choice and blend; equal to float rounding. (2) The screen-probe factors and the AO factor are read BEFORE the probe lookup (`rvkMvSgiFactors`, `rvkMvAoFactor`) and applied after it: the image gathers and the 28-float sum no longer overlap. (3) The point-light registers are compact slot state (`rvk_clSrc` + `rvk_clK`, 6 words instead of 12 float4 = 48 VGPRs); `rvkClReg(slot, comp)` reads the engine register (uniform load) or the table record (one divergent load) where the light loop uses it. (4) **The point-light loop is `[loop]`**: the unrolled loop hoisted all three lights' loads and live ranges; this one change took the plain scene v2 shaders from 112 to 73 VGPRs on average. (5) The 16-bit ubershader packs the ambient as 16 half words (no gain by itself, kept: it is the 16-bit build's own storage). Not kept (measured on the proxy sample, mean VGPR gi_cl / gbuf_cl): the ambient at the lit core's ambient term 118 / 125 vs 111 / 116 at the entry; the cluster selection at the entry 116 / 123 vs 111 / 116 before the loop; engine positions re-read instead of the `ePos` array -1.5 but **it hangs the GPU-assisted validation layer** (see below), so `ePos` stays; re-running the lookup in the debug view's epilogue +9 on the G-buffer variant.

**Numbers (RGA 2.9.1, gfx1035, in-game modules of the soak: 413 modules of the final text against the 526 of 14.7, paired by shader hash x family; mean VGPRs, waves per SIMD, modules above 128):**

| form | n | 14.7 | now | waves | > 128 before / after | max |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| plain scene v1 | 53 | 125.7 | 79.3 | 7.9 -> 12.0 | 36 / 0 | 165 -> 128 |
| **plain scene v2 (the owned form)** | 33 | 115.0 | **73.4** | 8.7 -> 12.3 | 20 / 0 | 157 -> 126 |
| GI + cluster | 88 | 151.6 | 124.2 | 6.2 -> 7.7 | 78 / 22 | 193 -> 162 |
| GI + cluster, scene v1 | 3 | 163.7 | 134.0 | | 3 / 2 | |
| G-buffer + GI + cluster | 111 | 172.5 | 126.9 | 5.5 -> 7.6 | 105 / 43 | 212 -> 162 |
| G-buffer + GI + cluster, scene v1 / v2 | 36 / 32 | 184.2 / 182.4 | 142.8 / 142.4 | 5.1 -> 6.6 | 36 / 32 and 32 / 27 | 213 / 214 -> 166 |
| G-buffer + motion + GI + cluster | 17 | 171.5 | 121.8 | 5.3 -> 7.9 | 17 / 2 | 189 -> 138 |
| base (unlit) | 28 | 51.9 | 33.9 | 13.3 -> 15.6 | 1 / 0 | |

**Target met for** the plain forms (mean 73 / 79, none above 128), GI + cluster on average (124) and the G-buffer + motion variants. **Not met:** the scene v1 / v2 forms of the G-buffer + GI + cluster variant (mean 142, 27 of 32 above 128, max 166). What remains is the shader's own state plus the scene form's record loads through a per-lane address (the draw index is a flat varying, so the compiler cannot make the record loads scalar: scene v2 plain is +35 VGPRs over the non-scene plain form); `WaveReadLaneFirst` on it is unsafe (a wave may hold two draws' pixels). **Not built:** (b) the GI / cluster diffuse as a compute pass into an image (a different architecture: 7 float4 per pixel, or SH rebuilt from G-buffer normals, FSR jitter and render scale to keep equal, the engine's per-object light weighting cannot be split from the material); with plain at 73 and GI + cluster at 124 the pixel shader is no longer as register-bound as before. **S-3 collapse (one module per class): not done**, the variants are not all <= 128 (the plain forms would take the variants' registers: 73 against 124 - 142 on every draw).

**Gates passed.** `rvk_tests` 321 / 0; `dxil-check uber` float and `--half` 2,696 / 2,696; `dxil-check corpus corpus --gbuf / --gbuf-motion / --gi / --pbr / --capture / --tint` 0 failures; `ubershader_check --half --tol 0.004` plain and `--scene2` 259 EQUAL each, the float build 259 EQUAL each; `rvk_rhitest --validation` PASS on Vulkan and D3D12 (`--sync-validation`: only the 2 swapchain-acquire hazards of every run), `rvk_d3d9test --backend vulkan|d3d12 --validation` PASS 0 / 0, `rvk_vktest --passes` PASS; **new rhitest cases "ubershader draw (light table, three slots / physical / slots view)"** (tools/rhitest/parity.cpp: a table of four lamps of different colours, one kept engine light + two refilled slots, both styles, the slots debug view; Vulkan = D3D12 to 0). Game runs (private copy `risen-test-vk-s1`, instance 44, FSR performance + frame generation + GI + AO + `debug.ubershader` + all owned settings; `DIFFERING` / `MISS` 0, no ubershader failure): first run Vulkan `-Validation` + GPU-assisted on pub_interior + jungle + harbour_docks + monastery_hall PASS (nothing beyond AMD frame generation's `rw_output`), savegame hold, walk, turning burst with `-Validation`, hero_idle + pub_idle PASS. **A GPU-assisted finding:** a variant that re-read the engine's light registers inside the selection loop (instead of the `ePos` array) made the GPU-assisted validation layer hang at the jungle spot (no log line for 500 s; the standard validation layer was fine, and so was main's build in 86 s): the layer's shader instrumentation; the array was put back.

**Two defects of main found while testing (NOT from S-2b: both reproduce with main's own shaders / with this commit reverted):**

* **D3D12, pub_interior, `debug.ubershader` + `lighting.clustered`: saturated wrong wall colours (purple lower wall, yellow / green upper walls), flickering, green NPCs** (the user's observation of the D3D12 soak run). Vulkan and the translated shaders on D3D12 are right. Reproduces with this commit's shaders, with main's (5211284) shaders, with 6be9c60's shaders, with S-7 reverted, in the 16-bit and the float build, with GI / AO off, with the packed ambient off, with loops unrolled, with the table never read (engine registers only) and with a materialised register array (the old structure): it is not the compact slots. It goes away with `lighting.clustered=false` and mostly with `lighting.light_shadows=off` (a purple tint remains): the D3D12 lamp-shadow path (`light.shadow` pass image / ids read by `rvkMvLightShadow`, the baked-shade term `rvk_clBake`; compare gpu-scene.md 9.12, the earlier push block defect) is the suspect. The table selection is right on D3D12 (`lighting.debug=slots` pictures equal Vulkan's). Why the earlier "within noise" A/B missed it: it ran on Vulkan, where the picture is right, and its baseline DLL was an older S-2 build, not main. The parity harness case above does not show it either (it has no lamp-shadow image): a D3D12 lamp-shadow case is still missing.
* **Vegetation flicker (Vulkan, jungle, all owned settings, no FSR / frame generation needed):** consecutive frames (time frozen, burst 8, interval 1) alternate between a full canopy and black tree silhouettes without leaves (mean abs difference between neighbours 17 - 31 of 255, green share 0.13 <-> 0.26). Stable (0.00 - 0.03) with `native.owned_trees=false` and with `lighting.clustered=false` + `lighting.full_lit=false`; the same flicker with this commit reverted: `native.owned_trees` (F4-2 / F4-3) together with the lighting table in Full. **Fixed in 7.46** (the owned trees laid more than 50 instances out as one run: rows beyond c231 read zeros; chunks of 50 now); not the ubershader, the light table or the lamp shadows.

**Open after the interruption (reboot, then the two defects):** GPU timing of the new modules against the translated pass (three runs), the picture A/B against main's own build, the D3D12 lamp-shadow case + fix, the owned-trees flicker fix.

**14.7b addendum (2026-11-08, S-fix): the zero reference of the light table, the damaged DXIL cache, two test defects, and what stays open.**

* **Defect A (rhitest after the S-2b merge) = three causes.** (1) In this environment the D3DX assembler (`d3dx9_39.dll`) rejects `kUberVsAsm`'s `mad o2, v1.yxyx, c1, c2` (error X5752, two constant registers in one instruction): every ubershader / scene case failed at "shaders" before it ran; the VS now moves the second constant through `r0` (same values). (2) "light table, slots view" is flat by design (the debug view paints one colour per class: two refilled slots = green everywhere): the case now checks the centre colour = green instead of "pixel changes along the rows".
