(3) **D3D12 GPU-based validation, 3 x "RawBuffer expected, Texture2D in descriptor, heap index 0" in pipeline 'parity uber' (one per light-table case): `rvkClReg` (rvk_material_variant.hlsli) read the table record through `rvk_clLb` (zero until `rvkMvCluster` found a valid table) at `(src - 1u) * RVK_LIGHT_BYTES` (src = 0 wraps) *below* its `src == 0` return; the compiler turns the branch into a select and hoists the load, so every lane of an engine-register slot read a raw buffer through the zero pair = heap slot 0 (a texture) on D3D12.** Fix: `rvk_clLb` starts as the pixel window 3 (a real buffer), the index is clamped (`max(src, 1u) - 1u`), the refilled slot's record index is clamped (`min(pick, 0xFFFF)`), and `rvkMvLightShadow` fetches `max(slot, 1)`. After it all three messages are gone (`rvk_rhitest --validation`: 0 GBV messages on D3D12, PASS on both; `--sync-validation`: only the 2 known swapchain-acquire hazards of Vulkan). The mechanism (hoisted load) is inferred from the compiled behaviour, the effect (messages gone) measured. On a driver the hoisted load reads a texture's memory as buffer words; whether that changed any pixel was not shown (the select discards it).
* **New D3D12 / Vulkan parity cases "light table, lamp shadows" and "... physical"** (`tools/rhitest/parity.cpp`, `LightTableSpec::shadows`): an RGBA16F lamp-shadow image (entries `id + q / 64`, lamp 1 shadowed on the left half, lamp 2 fading down the rows, lamp 3 lit, lamp 4 shadowed on the right half), four coloured lamps with shadow ids, `params[lights].z` = 0.6 (the baked-light term runs), a material without tangent space (the first lamp cases' heavy request showed the lamps at 0.5 of 49 mean: the picture did not depend on them; with the simple request the mean moves 13.7 -> 14.8 / 11.8 -> 17.4 with the shadow visibility). Both backends equal (worst difference 0), 0 validation messages.
* **Damaged DXIL cache files (found while chasing defect B; D3D12 only).** 14 of the 5,067 files in the test copy's `bin\rvk\cache\dxil` were **all zero** (an interrupted run / the reboot: NTFS had committed the rename before the data). `Device::dxilModule` used any non-empty file as DXIL; the debug layer said `CreateVertexShader: Encoded Vertex Shader size doesn't match specified size` and `CreateGraphicsPipelineState ... failed (0x80004005)` for e.g. the skinned `fea9beaa... cap6` vertex capture and several `gbuf_m-cl` pixel shaders: those draws are skipped for the rest of the session (= meshes that are missing / unlit in a D3D12 run). Fix: a cache file is used only when it starts with `DXBC` and the container's own size field (offset 24) equals the file size, else it is deleted and recompiled (`d3d9: DXIL cache file ... is damaged`). The SPIR-V and HLSL caches had no damaged file. This is a plausible part of the user's "some meshes unlit", **not** shown to be the wall colours.
* **Defect B (saturated flickering wall colours) did NOT reproduce here.** With main's own `rvk_material_variant.hlsli` (a clean copy through `RVK_SHADER_DIR`; an earlier first copy was mangled and fell back to the translated shaders: its pictures do not count), the user's settings (Full, `debug.ubershader`, all owned settings, `lighting.clustered` + `full_lit`, lamp shadows nearby, FSR performance, GI, AO) on D3D12 at pub_interior (freeze_wait 4,800, frames 4,000 - 5,001) and monastery_hall / torch_hero: **the walls are right with and without the fix** (`scratch/s1/base2_d12/frame_4500.png` vs `fix2_d12/frame_4500.png`, `fix_d12_*`). So the zero-reference fix is *not shown* to cure the colour bug (the S-2b agent's wrong pictures `g_f32_*`, `k_base_*` came from its own builds / GPU state, not reproducible on main + this machine today). Not excluded: the damaged cache above, an uninitialised read of the hoisted-load kind, or a state of the earlier session. If the user still sees it: delete `bin\rvk\cache\dxil` and run with `-Validation` (GBV) to see the first bad read.
* **Open, found by the bisect (D3D12 only): the skinned actors are black / torn with `debug.ubershader` + `native.owned_actors` (F6-1).** torch_hero, the hero: lit on Vulkan, lit with the translated shaders on D3D12, **black with a detached boot** on D3D12 + ubershader (with or without `lighting.clustered`, with or without lamp shadows), **lit again with `native.owned_actors=false`** (`scratch/s1/hero_montage.png`: fix_vk, tr_d12, fix3_d12, nocl_d12, va_d12 (owned_actors off), vb_d12, vc_d12); the rug and one stool of pub_interior are dark / missing in the same runs. Not touched here (owned actors = the persistent bone table `gpuscene::bonetable()` read by the scene variant on D3D12): next task.

### 14.8 S-7 result: the post chain on our own shaders - the catalogue, and the luminance chain as two dispatches (2026-11-08; code eb011ed + the fixes after it; setting `native.own_post` = off | on | compare, Debug, Full, default off; settings count 143)

**The catalogue overturns the S-0 premise.** The post chain is **not** bandwidth-bound and is not "12 full-resolution RGBA16F passes, ~275 MB". Frame 2500 of the savegame hold (`-LogFrame`, 1600x900, the engine's default DoF graph, 19 effect draws,
every target logged) and the shaders' tap lists (`rvk_fxtool dump --listing` of the corpus's post_hdr / post_bloom / post_dof / post_streaks variants):

| # | unit (filter : technique) | target | reads | bytes (read + write) | GPU, XTX (ms) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Down64x64 : LogLuminance | 64x64 R32F | scene RGBA16F, 4 point taps per texel (16 K taps) | 0.13 MB + 16 KB | 0.005 |
| 2 - 6 | Down32x32 .. Down2x2 : DownSample | 32 .. 2 R32F | previous level, 4 point taps | < 20 KB each | 0.003 - 0.004 each |
| 7, 8 | AdaptiveLiminance, AvgLuminance : Copy | 1x1 R32F (the persistent adapted luminance) | 2x2 level, the previous value | ~0 | 0.004 each |
| 9 | BrightPass (BrightPassAlphaMask) | 309x210 A8R8G8B8 | scene 4 taps / texel, depth map, avg luminance | 2.4 + 0.26 MB | 0.010 |
| 10 - 12 | GlowH, Streak (64 radial taps), GlowVAddBloom2 | 309x210 A8R8G8B8 | bloom images (cache-resident) | ~0.5 MB each | 0.006 / 0.012 / 0.006 |
| 13 | ToneMap | **1600x900 A8R8G8B8** (not FP16) | scene | 11.5 + 5.8 MB | 0.013 |
| 14 | DoFDown | 400x225 A8R8G8B8 | ToneMap (4 taps = all of it) + depth map (16 taps = all of it) | 11.5 + 0.36 MB | 0.011 |
| 15 - 18 | BlurH, BlurV, CalcNearCoc, SmallBlur | 400x225 A8R8G8B8 | the 400x225 images | ~0.7 MB each | 0.005 each |
| 19 | DoF (screen) | back buffer X8R8G8B8 | ToneMap 5 taps, depth map, blur images, bloom | 12 + 5.8 MB | 0.030 |

Total **~48 MB** per frame (6x less than 14.1.6's 275 MB: every intermediate is 8 bit or a small R32F; only ToneMap / DoFDown / DoF touch full-resolution images) and **0.15 - 0.17 ms** of GPU time on the XTX (19 passes; `postsum.py`
over `-PerfFrame` logs): the three full-resolution passes are 0.054 ms (36 %), the luminance chain 0.030 - 0.046 (20 - 27 %), the bloom chain 0.033 (22 %), the DoF middle 0.019 (12 %), pass barriers the rest. On the XTX the chain is
**per-pass overhead, not bandwidth** (3 - 6 us for a 16 KB pass); on the 680M the 48 MB are ~0.5 - 0.8 ms of its 50 - 100 GB/s plus the same per-pass overhead x ~3 - 5 [I, not measured]. Consequences for the plan: "R11G11B10 / half-res
intermediates" gains nothing (they are already 8 bit); folding ToneMap into DoFDown + DoF saves ~13 % of the bytes (the scene is re-read as RGBA16F instead of the 8-bit ToneMap image) and a quantisation step; the real saving is **fewer passes**
(19 -> ~7 possible: luminance 8 -> 1 (built), bloom 4 -> 2, ToneMap folded in, DoF middle 4 -> 1), and S-7's -0.8..-1.3 ms of 14.3 is an overestimate: ~-0.2 .. -0.4 ms on the 680M by the pass-count model.

**What is built (group 1: the luminance chain, 8 passes -> 2 dispatches).** `native.own_post` (Full; `on` needs the post chain taken over, `compare` needs it composed): the eight luminance units of the scene graph are recorded as `EffectOps`
composes them (the composed draws are the source of every parameter: the quad's uv at the sample points, the taps' offsets, the adaptation constants c1.x lerp factor and c0.xy AvgLumClamp, the textures, the targets), held (not drawn), and
when the eighth is composed `native/own_post.cpp` records two compute dispatches (`Device::runPassNow`, new: an ad-hoc pass mid-frame, the body of `inject` for one pass): `post_lum_tile.comp.hlsl` (64 groups of 8x8: the 64x64 level straight
from the scene with the engine's tap order, then 2x2 boxes down to one texel of the 8x8 level) and `post_lum.comp.hlsl` (one group: levels 4, 5, the geometric mean, the lerp with the previous value, the clamp) which writes the Adaptive
unit's 1x1 target and the persistent AvgLuminance image (`Texture::setSmallStorage`: R32F render targets <= 64x64 get storage usage while the setting is on; Vulkan's storage view of the swizzled (r, 1, 1, 1) R32F image is its identity view,
fixed in `rhi/vulkan`). The previous value rides to dispatch B in row 8 of the tile image (the output is also the source: no SRV / UAV aliasing, found by D3D12 GPU-based validation). The tile stages need the engine's DownSample taps to be aligned 2x2
blocks (checked per run). A group that breaks off (a unit fails composition, another unit arrives) or a kernel that cannot run (pipeline not built, tap geometry not as expected, textures without storage, FSR frames where the post chain sees
the display-size image but no substitute is set: ~1 - 3 % of the frames) **flushes the held units as draws** at that point, in order: the picture is then the engine's. Findings that cost a run each: the renderer shifts every D3D9 draw by the half
pixel (`posFix` = (+1/W, -1/H) in clip space), so D3D9 pixel p samples the quad at NDC 2p/W - 1, not at the pixel centre (the first kernel with centre sampling was 0.15 % off); with FSR the post chain's scene texture is the upscaler's output (taps 1/1600
while the engine image is 1066x600): the kernel samples the substitute's slot.

**Gates.**
* `rvk_tests` 321 / 0. `dxil-check embedded`: post_lum.comp and post_lum_tile.comp PASS. `rvk_rhitest` case "post luminance chain by compute" (new, `tools/rhitest/parity_post.inl`: an RGBA16F scene through the bindless heap, the parameter block by reference,
  two dispatches, two storage outputs; CPU port of the eight draws; the backends cross-checked): PASS on Vulkan and D3D12 with `--validation` (rel difference to the CPU 0, Vulkan vs D3D12 0) and `--sync-validation` (only the two swapchain-acquire
  WRITE_AFTER_READ hazards every Vulkan `--sync-validation` run of this tool prints, also with no case selected).
* **Picture equality, in the game, numerically** (`native.own_post=compare`: the kernel next to the engine's units, before the Copy overwrites the previous value; read back and compared with the Adaptive unit's target): relative difference
  **max 3.9e-5, mean 1.7e-6** over 4,600 frames (pub_interior + jungle; 0.1 % of the frames above 1e-5). Tolerance: the luminance divides ToneMap's exposure, 4e-5 relative = 0.004 % brightness, a hundredth of one 8-bit step; the rest is fp32
  summation order and the engine's `_pp` taps.
* ON vs OFF pictures (`water-compare -Mode full`, frozen spots; control = off vs off, `tools/sbs_diff.py`): mean |d| ON 0.72 / 1.36 / 1.55 / 2.85 / 3.90 / 0.08 (pub_interior, monastery_hall, harbour_docks, jungle, beach_sun, sea_horizon) vs control
  0.47 / 1.48 / 0.20 / 3.68 / 2.48 / 0.51; luma differences inside the control's. `shot-reviewer` (crops, radial glow profiles of beach_sun / sea_horizon, a 12-cell grid of harbour_docks): no exposure, bloom, glare or colour change beyond the control
  (harbour_docks' +2.4 % luma = one splash particle on one side); the hard sun disc is not in frame in beach_sun, the flare is judged through the horizon glow (sea_horizon identical: 0.08).
* Game runs, private copy `risen-test-vk-s7` (instance 45, lock `rvk-s7`), Full + `native.own_post=on` + `native.scene_ops_compare` + `native.lean_check=10` (the held units' compositions compared with the engine's draws on check frames): first run
  `-Validation` + GPU-assisted (pub_interior): **0 messages**; FSR quality + `-SyncValidation` (harbour_docks, jungle): only AMD's own `rw_spd_mip3` format warning; FSR quality + frame generation (savegame hold): PASS; **D3D12** (`-Api d3d12 -Validation`,
  `RVK_NO_GBV=1`): only the DSV-format warning the baseline prints; with GPU-based validation the aliasing described above was found and fixed (3,708 reports, 0 after); turning burst (`rvk_rtwalk` burst, savegame, `-Validation`): 0 messages, check
  frames `after 0 / 43,337 differing, store 0 / 43,337, fields 0 / 87,034`, composition mismatches 0; the walk ran before the last fixes (storage-view VUID only, fixed since). Defaults unchanged (off), `Debug` setting.

**GPU per pass** (XTX, `-PerfFrame` 24-frame means, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy, `-All`; three runs each, `postsum.py`; the post range = first luminance pass .. DoF screen pass): savegame hold off 0.170 / 0.181 (a third log without a post range)
-> on 0.151 / 0.130 / 0.202 (one spiked frame, min 0.102); harbour_docks off 0.140 / 0.115 / 0.208 (spike) -> on 0.113 / 0.110 / 0.110; the luminance part: eight draws 0.030 - 0.046 ms -> `post.lum` (both dispatches) **0.010 ms**; passes in the range 19 -> 12.
Net **-0.02 .. -0.03 ms of 0.15 - 0.17** (about -15 %, of ~3.5 ms per frame < 1 %): inside the run-to-run noise of a single run, consistent across the six. A first version with ONE workgroup was 0.035 ms (slower than the draws: 16 K dependent point fetches on one CU),
hence the tile dispatch. Bytes per frame ~48 -> ~47.7 MB (the luminance units are 0.3 MB): unchanged. 680M extrapolation (pass-count model, per-pass overhead 3 - 5x the XTX's, kernel time x ~3): -7 passes x ~15 - 25 us - ~30 us = **-0.07 .. -0.15 ms**
[I, unmeasured].

**Not built (S-7 remains open).** Bloom (BrightPass + GlowH + Streak + GlowV: 4 -> 2 with the streak's 64 radial taps), the ToneMap fold into DoFDown / DoF (needs inline tonemapping of 4 + 5 taps and a quantisation decision: the engine's ToneMap image is 8 bit), the DoF middle
(BlurH / BlurV / NearCoc / SmallBlur -> 1 group per tile with halos), and the HDRI-only graph's other filters; each is a port from the translated shaders (listings: `rvk_fxtool dump --listing`) + a compare mode like this one. By the table above the remaining
gain on the XTX is ~0.05 - 0.08 ms and on the 680M ~0.2 - 0.3 ms: worth doing for the iGPU target, not a large share of the frame; S-8 (shadow mask, iGPU preset) and the S-2 / S-3 pixel shader work are larger.

### 14.8b S-7b result: the bloom chain and the DoF middle as compute kernels (2026-11-08; code c5d1170 + the alias fix after it; `native.own_post` unchanged: off | on | compare, Debug, Full, default off)

**Built (three groups on S-7a's mechanism).** `EffectOps` holds / flushes any of three groups of the scene graph's units (`OwnGroup`: luminance 8 units, bloom 4, DoF middle 4; a unit out of order, a refused kernel or a failed parameter check draws the held units as the engine's draws, in order). Every parameter is read from the composed draws (quad uv, the vertex shaders' tap offsets, the pixel constants); the tap pattern of each unit is verified before a run (`native: own post: the bloom chain stays the engine's: ...` names the first failed check).
* **Bloom, 4 passes -> 3 dispatches** (`post_bright.comp` BrightPassAlphaMask: four point taps of the scene x ToneMapFactor / avgLum, white-point and threshold curve, depth tap -> alpha; `post_glow.comp` GlowH and Streak from the bright image with the linear / clamp sampler; `post_bloom.comp` GlowV + streak, blended onto the **persistent bloom image** by read-modify-write, SRCALPHA / INVSRCALPHA on all four channels). The engine's BrightPass / GlowH / Streak targets are never written (intermediates are transient RGBA8). The glow weights are the shaders' `def` constants; **the engine's vertex shader outputs a zero offset for the 7th tap pair** (v7 = 0: a centre tap counted twice with weight 0.036): ported as it is.
* **DoF middle, 4 passes -> 1 dispatch** (`post_dofmid.comp`): an 8x8 tile computes BlurH over a 17 x 11 halo, BlurV and NearCoc over 10 x 10, SmallBlur from those, in groupshared memory, and writes BlurV (the "large" blur) and SmallBlur (the "medium") that the final DoF pass samples. **Every tap of these four shaders is a texel corner in both axes** (the units add no half texel: the quad's uv at pixel p is p / size, so a "horizontal" tap is also half a texel above the pixel): each bilinear tap is the exact mean of a 2 x 2 block; the kernel does that arithmetic with clamped indices and rounds each stage to 8 bits like the engine's A8R8G8B8 images. (The first version assumed one axis and was off by up to 39 steps: the rhitest CPU port, written as the engine's draws, caught it.)
* **Aliasing found by D3D12 GPU-based validation**: the engine's image pool shares textures between filters whose lifetimes do not overlap, and the SmallBlur / BlurV target **is** the DoFDown image in every frame (`native: own post (5 s): DoF middle: N runs read a copy of the DoFDown image`); the kernel read what it wrote (SRV / UAV alias, also a data race on Vulkan). The input is copied to a transient first.
* **Storage targets**: while `native.own_post=on` render targets of A8R8G8B8 with 65 .. 1024 px sides are created as **R8G8B8A8_UNORM storage images** (BGRA8 is no guaranteed storage format; the colours are the same, the byte order is not visible to shaders). The bloom blend needs typed UAV loads of RGBA8 (`storageImageExtendedFormats`; refused without).
* New diagnostics: `native: own post (5 s)` lines per group and per compared image (`bright`, `glow`, `streak`, `bloom`, `large blur`, `small blur`: max step in 8-bit units, mean relative difference, share of pixels more than one step off); `RVK_OWNPOST_DUMP=1` logs the differing texels; `RVK_DOF_TEST=1` forces a strong depth of field (focal range 2,500 / 6,000 .. 14,000 units: the composed draws then differ from the engine's, test pictures only); `water-compare.ps1 -Environment`; rvkcompare spot `sun_disc` (explicit; the evening sun just at the top edge of the frame).

**Gates.**
* `rvk_tests` 321 / 0; `dxil-check embedded` 84 / 84 (the four new kernels PASS); `rvk_rhitest` cases "post bloom chain by compute" (max 1 step against the CPU chain on both backends, Vulkan vs D3D12 worst difference 1) and "post DoF middle by compute" (37 x 29 image, not a multiple of the tile: max 1 step, backends within 1): PASS with `--validation` and `--sync-validation` (only the two known swapchain-acquire hazards); `rvk_d3d9test --validation` 0 errors.
* **Compare mode** (`native.own_post=compare`, 2,300+ frames per run, pub_interior, GPU-assisted validation: 0 messages): luminance max rel 4e-5; DoF middle: BlurV max 2 steps, SmallBlur max 3 steps (0.16 % of the pixels more than one step: ties of the 8-bit rounding); bloom image max 18 - 24 steps in 0.08 - 0.16 % of the pixels; bright pass max 255 in about 6 texels per frame: **a scene texel flip at the rows where the 309 x 210 grid meets a 1600 x 900 texel boundary exactly (every 7th row)**: the engine's draw lands on either side by the rounding luck of the rasteriser's interpolation, a kernel cannot copy that (it picks the upper texel every frame); streak max 17 - 21 steps in 0.004 % of the pixels (64 bilinear taps).
* **ON vs OFF pictures**, `water-compare -Mode full`, mean |d| ON / control (off vs off): pub_interior 0.49 / 1.31, monastery_hall 1.25 / 0.89, harbour_docks 0.24 / 0.20 (repeat after the alias fix 0.25), jungle 3.38 / 3.08, beach_sun 2.23 / 2.30, sea_horizon 0.05 / 0.04, sun_disc 0.05 - 0.08: inside the control's noise (a run after the fix showed harbour_docks 6.9: one splash-particle burst on one side only, gone in the repeat). Strong DoF (`RVK_DOF_TEST`): 0.22 / 0.83 / 1.59 (harbour_docks / pub_interior / jungle). FSR quality: 0.22 / 0.73 / 2.81 / 0.22 (harbour_docks 2.7 in one run = a splash); D3D12: 0.21 / 1.50 / 2.60 / 0.03. `shot-reviewer` (numbers on all 22 pictures, 3 viewed by eye, no crops): no change in bloom, glare, god rays, exposure, colour or DoF blur; the sun disc itself is only just in the frame of `sun_disc` (halo / streaks judged by the numbers).
* Game runs (private copy `risen-test-vk-s7`, instance 45, lock `rvk-s7`): first On run `-Validation` + GPU-assisted: 0 messages; `-SyncValidation` (pub_interior, jungle): 0; FSR quality + frame generation (harbour_docks): AMD's own `rw_output` format errors only; the groups are refused in FSR frames before the first upscaled one (`tap offsets ... are not 1 / 1066x600`; bloom runs 1,498, refused 134); D3D12 `-Validation` (GBV): the baseline DSV-format warning only after the alias fix (840 `Incompatible texture barrier layout` before it); walk (`rvk_rtwalk` burst, `-Validation`): 0 messages.

**GPU per pass** (XTX, private copy, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, harbour_docks hold, `-PerfFrame 4200` x 24 frames, three runs each, range = first luminance pass .. DoF screen pass incl. barriers): off 0.217 / 0.190 / 0.180 ms (19 passes) -> on 0.207 / 0.148 / 0.132 ms (6 passes: `post.lum` 0.014, `post.bloom` 0.036, ToneMap, DoFDown, `post.dofmid` 0.020, DoF screen). Mean **-0.034 ms (-17 %)** including S-7a's luminance part. The bloom kernels (0.036 ms, three dispatches, 64 streak taps per pixel) are no faster than the engine's four passes (0.033); the gain is the DoF middle and the pass boundaries. Measured in the private copy (another agent held the shared lock for over an hour), so other agents' GPU load can add noise.

**What stays on the engine shaders (translated), of the 19 units**: **ToneMap**, **DoFDown** and **DoF (screen)**. ToneMap's 8-bit image is read by DoFDown and by the final pass (5 taps), so folding it needs the final pass as a kernel, and **the final pass draws into the back buffer / display image, which is no storage target** (swapchain storage usage, a flip-model UAV on D3D12, the FSR / frame-generation paths that read it); DoFDown reads 16 depth + 4 colour taps of the tone-mapped scene (a fold would tone-map 64 texels per output pixel). Also left: the overlay effects (OverlayMovieStripsAbs, OverlayHit: screen draws blended onto the finished frame), the HDRI-only graph's HDRI screen pass (same reason), Indicator / Copy filters and the GUI graph (effect_ops_gui). 16 of the 19 units now run on our kernels (8 luminance, 4 bloom, 4 DoF middle).


### 14.9 S-1b result: the HLSL vertex shaders and the SpeedTree leaf pixel shaders on the owned path (2026-11-08, code commit b357003; no new setting, settings count unchanged, defaults unchanged: `debug.ubershader` stays Debug / off)

**What was built.** (1) `debug.ubershader` now also builds the scene vertex shaders (`SceneVs` / `SceneVs2` / `SceneVsLod`) of `ge_default_phong.fx` / `ge_default_leafs.fx` `VS_Default` / `VS_DefaultZPass` requests from `shaders/material/ge_default.vert.hlsl` (`shader::uberVertexHlsl`; one port covers static meshes, skinned + morph, instanced, SpeedTree branch / frond / leaf mesh / leaf cards / billboards, vegetation (grass) and the screen billboards: the `XFX_*` macros of the request select the code). The pipeline's vertex input state is built for the engine's shader, so the port declares **exactly the inputs the bytecode declares** (`RVK_VS_DECL`: per declared input `RVK_LOC_<attr>` = the dcl register, `RVK_SEM_<attr>` = `ATTR<register>`, the name the D3D12 input layout uses; the old fixed order stays for the offline tools). All varyings are float4 and in ascending location order (D3D12 links by register and component mask: the first version, float3 tangent / binormal and the scene index declared after the pad 15, failed `CreateGraphicsPipelineState` with signature errors; caught by the new rhitest cases). The billboard fade variant (`lod.trees != 1`, the player default) is `#define RVK_LOD_FADE <frame parameter>` in the port (`Device::lodModule`'s input override for BlendWeight1.xy as text). (2) The leaf pixel shaders: `uberCovers` / `uberModule` use `uberSupportsFile` / `src.file` (the RVK_LEAFS port of shader-permutations.md 11 was already proven). (3) The translated shader is the per-draw fallback (a failed build keeps the translated scene form, counted in `translated builds after an ubershader failure`); `debug.ubershader_skip` bit 256 keeps the vertex shaders translated (A/B; the setting range is 0..511). rvk.log `native: owned shaders (5 s)` has a `[<kind> vertex: owned uber N translated N failed N; decided ...]` entry per draw kind (`Variant::uberVs`, `SceneDrawStats::uberVsOwned / uberVsDecided`).

**Equivalence tools.** `tools/vs_equiv.py out/matsrc/*.jsonl --corpus game-shaders` (new; revives the GLSL-era `--vs-test` mode of `rvk_shadereq`): for every captured vertex request the translated corpus export and the port built with the run time's own text (macros + the dcl inputs parsed from the export's listing) run behind a wrapper that draws one fixed triangle and exports the clip position + every varying the translated shader writes; the same random vertices (per-semantic slots) and random constants go to both, the 4x4-pixel interpolated varyings are compared (`|a - b| <= 1e-4 (1 + max)`, 8 trials, validation on). **52 of 52 supported vertex shaders EQUAL** (55 captured; 3 are particle / skydome vertex shaders, not ge_default) covering rigid, instanced, masked, z pass, shadow map pass, skinned, skinned + morph, billboard, vegetation, SpeedTree branch / leaf mesh / leaf cards / billboards (worst relative difference 1.8e-7). Negative control: swapping the binormal's cross product makes **29** of them DIFFER. Not covered by it: the fade variant `RVK_LOD_FADE` (a multiplier; the corpus has no export of the overridden shader) and the scene forms of the vertex shader beyond the rhitest cases below. Pixel shaders (`ubershader_check.py`, leaves included): plain **259 EQUAL**, scene v2 **259 EQUAL** (63 NO-HLSL, 4 unsupported entries as in 14.6). `rvk_shadertool dxil-check uber` now also builds the vertex forms (offline order, declared inputs, + scene, + scene view: 102 requests x 4): **3,104 of 3,104** SPIR-V + DXIL OK (337 x 8 pixel + 102 x 4 vertex); `dxil-check embedded` 84 / 84; `rvk_tests` 321 / 0. `rvk_rhitest` (new cases "scene variant (corpus: rigid mesh / skinned mesh, uber vertex shader[, ABI v2])": a real corpus vertex shader's translation as the plain reference, the port as the scene vertex shader, same pixel shader and records): Vulkan and D3D12 PASS, 0 differ, `--validation` 0 errors; `--sync-validation` only the 2 pre-existing swapchain-acquire errors of 14.6. `rvk_d3d9test --backend vulkan | d3d12 --validation`: 0 failures / 0 validation errors.

**Registers (gfx1035, RGA 2.9.1 offline, in-game SPIR-V modules, scene ABI v2 plain form).** Vertex shaders, 27 shaders paired by hash: translated **74.1 -> port 71.6 VGPRs** (-3.3 %, max 105 -> 102), SGPRs 44.5 -> 42.4, ISA bytes -5.3 %, 0 spills / scratch (all 46 port modules). Leaf pixel shaders (and the few other pixel shaders S-1a left translated that now have an ubershader module; 40 paired `scene_ps2` modules): **113.0 -> 117.6 VGPRs** (+4 %, max 153 -> 167), 0 spills. Occupancy is set by the pixel shader (the plain scene v2 form ~114 = 8.8 waves; the vertex shaders at ~72 are not the limit), so the leaf change costs a little and the vertex change gains a little; no change that is visible on the register model.

**Share of owned items on our shaders** (rvk.log, all 5 s windows of each run summed; the owned scene draws of `native.owned_persistent` / `owned_trees`; billboards go through their own flush and are not in this counter): **static meshes, skinned, SpeedTrees, vegetation: pixel shader 100 % on the ubershader and vertex shader 100 % on the port in every run** (S-1a: SpeedTrees 61 - 69 % PS, because of the leaf shaders); other / skipped / unknown / failed = 0, `translated builds after an ubershader failure 0`, no module failed. Totals of one run for scale: walk 1: static 4,429,243, SpeedTree 1,929,536, vegetation 354,074, skinned 150,112 items; savegame hold: 1,029,721 / 433,619 / 80,244 / 33,874; Direct3D12 spots: 936,661 / 536,404 / 67,612 / 9,156. (The decided counters show the billboard vertex shaders as `uber` too since the fade variant exists; in the first soak, before RVK_LOD_FADE, they showed `failed 31`.)

**GPU per pass** (XTX, `-PerfFrame` 24-frame means, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy + lock per run, no FSR / frame generation; translated / pixel shaders only (S-1a state, skip bit 256) / S-1b (+ vertex shaders); solid pass = the 1600x900 pass with the most draws, ms; runs that hit a loading frame (total < 1 ms) or another map state are dropped): savegame hold 0.717 (0.689, 0.730, 0.731) / 0.737 (one valid run) / **0.696** (0.700, 0.712, 0.676); jungle 1.243 (1.282, 1.238, 1.209) / 1.476 (1.330, 1.622, 0.827 = noisy) / **1.233** (1.143, 1.365, 1.192); totals hold 3.579 / - / 3.516, jungle 5.469 / 5.301 / 5.644 (single runs 5.3 - 6.6: noise above the effect). pub_interior runs mostly hit the loading frames (valid single runs 0.741 / 0.933 / 0.799 solid). Reading: **not worse than the translated shaders beyond noise** on the XTX; the vertex part is a small share of the solid pass here, the 680M question stays open (the vertex VGPR cut is 3 %).

**Picture A/B in one hold** (`debug.ubershader` toggled every 300 frames = translated <-> pixel + vertex ports, FSR performance, all owned settings, 7 toggle pairs vs 33 same-state pairs, mean abs difference per pixel of 255, `cmp_ab.py`): pub_interior toggle 0.144 vs same-state 0.211 (floor 0.137); jungle toggle 6.06 vs same-state 7.55 (wind / streaming noise): every toggle pair is inside the same-state range, none bit-identical on either side, 0 rvk.log errors.

**Soaks** (private copy `risen-test-vk-s1b`, instance 47, lock `rvk-s1b`; all owned settings + `debug.ubershader` + FSR performance + GI + AO): first `-Validation` + GPU-assisted run (pub_interior; later pub_interior + jungle) PASS, only AMD frame generation's `rw_output` findings (frame generation was on in those two); savegame hold PASS; walk 1 PASS; walk 2 (+ `shadows.rt=near`, `rt_skin_verify`) PASS; turning burst with `-Validation` PASS; spots pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks / monastery_hall PASS; hero_idle + pub_idle PASS; **one D3D12 spots run (pub_interior + jungle) PASS**. `DIFFERING` / `MISS` 0 in every run, no stage turned itself off in the final runs, no device loss. **Frame generation hang (not S-1b):** with `framegen.mode=fsr3` the burst, spots, hero and burst-with-validation runs were killed by the log-silence watchdog ("STALL: no new log line for 90 s") a minute or two after the savegame load; the same burst with the vertex shaders translated (`debug.ubershader_skip=256`) and with every shader translated (`debug.ubershader=false`) stalled the same way, and the burst without frame generation passed; those four runs were therefore repeated without `framegen.mode` (that is the soak list above, FSR performance kept). The earlier soaks of 14.6 / 14.7 ran this combination clean, so something around AMD's frame generation swapchain changed (the walk / hold runs with it still passed); not investigated here. One spots run in the frame-generation series turned `native.owned_shadows` off on 12 draws DIFFERING in "vertex input / dynamic state" (the same class as the 14.7 flake); the rerun without frame generation was clean.

**Open.** (1) The fade variant has no `shadereq` proof (observed only: billboards 100 % on the port, soaks and A/B clean). (2) The scene form of the vertex shader is proven by the two corpus cases of rhitest (rigid, skinned) on both backends and by the in-game check frames, not by `vs_equiv.py` (plain form only). (3) Leaf pixel shaders +4 % VGPRs: S-2's diet should include them. (4) The frame generation stall above: **fixed (FG-1, frame-generation.md 13)**, AMD's named swapchain events shared with the other agents' games. (5) 680M day: S-9.

**14.9 addendum (2026-11-08, fix 9fe8985): the port's UBYTE4 bone indices on D3D12.** On D3D12 the skinned actors were black with a detached boot with `debug.ubershader` + `native.owned_actors`: the HLSL vertex shader read `BLENDINDICES` as `int4(aIndices0)`, but D3D12 reads a D3D9 UBYTE4 attribute as `R8G8B8A8_UNORM` (no scaled formats in DXGI; the translator scales by 255 under push flags bit 17 + register), so every bone index was 0. Fixed in `ge_default.vert.hlsl` (`rvkDrawFlags()`); the corpus parity cases of 14.9 (Open 2) now feed UBYTE4 bytes and include the skinned + morph request (`vs30_5d3d2ccda5a8b178`): PASS on both backends and ABI forms, 8 failures with the old shader text. Lesson: a parity test that feeds float4 vertex data misses attribute-encoding differences between backends; feed the engine's own formats. The ABI probe covers blocks only. Open: the pub_interior rug / stool darkening on D3D12 (dx12-game-port.md defects of 2026-11-08: lamp shadows' baked-light darkening, not the ubershader).

### 14.10 S-6 result: depth-only z passes (2026-11-10; one Debug setting `native.depth_z` = off | on | compare, Full, default off; settings count 146)

**Census (what the z passes run).** The main z pre-pass binds the R32F "depth map" as its only colour target and the scene's hardware depth (D32 float, reversed under `debug.reverse_z`); `PS_DefaultZPass` writes `viewPos.z` (the VS divided it by the engine far) to it, plus `texkill` of alpha - c5.w for cut-outs (`XFX_MASKED`). So the map is the hardware depth's twin: the same triangles, `view z / far`, cleared to 1 (the sky), the `rvk_kill` / cut-out flags of the draw block unchanged. The sun cascades render into a NULL colour target + a DF24 depth map: their pixel shader already had no attachment to write. Cold-path counts (draws seen by `prepareDraw`, retained / owned replays are not counted: they replay the pipeline decided at capture), savegame + jungle: of the colour-less / z draws about 2/3 had no texkill and lost the pixel shader (first windows: 3,798 left out / 1,709 kept; 279 / 780 later in the same area; the kept ones are the foliage cut-outs). Interiors have no cascades, only the main z pass. GPU time of the passes (XTX, 1600x900, `-PerfFrame` 24-frame means, three runs per arm, shared copy, another agent's game ran on the GPU part of the time): see below.

**What was built.** (1) `Device::DepthZ` (`d3d9/draw.cpp`): a draw into a scope without colour attachment whose engine pixel shader has no texkill, no depth write and the alpha test state off is pipelined **without a fragment stage** (`PipelineDesc::fs = None`: Vulkan `build()` with one stage, libraries fall back to a whole build, D3D12 PSO without PS; the scene / owned forms (`sceneDecide`) skip the PS variants for such draws). Cut-outs keep the translated / ubershader z pixel shader: with no colour attachment its colour output is dead and the driver's compiler removes it, so what runs is the sample + compare + kill; no separate "minimal alpha PS" module was built (not verified in ISA: RGA was not run on it). (2) `native.depth_z=on`: the main z scope of a Main view whose RT0 is the DepthMap-role texture drops that attachment (needs reverse-z, a D32 float depth buffer at least as large, level 0): colour count 0; `enh::DepthZ` derives the map ONCE at AfterDepthPrepass (order -200, before every consumer: `rt.scene` -100, G-buffer, lights, RT shadows, GI, water, the engine's own mask chain which runs after the hook) with one full-screen triangle into the engine's own image (`shaders/depth/depth_z.frag.hlsl`: `rvkHwDepthToViewZ` x 1/far, 1 for the sky). The first view only builds the pipeline; the attachment is dropped from the next scope on, and only a scope that dropped it is derived (`takeDepthMapDropped`). (3) `compare`: the engine's z pass is unchanged, a compute reduction (`depth_z_compare.comp.hlsl`) counts pixels over relative tolerances. Stages line: `native: stages (5 s): ... depth_z=active(...)`, `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1` knows `native.depth_z`.

**Consumer equivalence (compare mode, GPU, pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks, including loading frames; GPU-assisted validation clean).** Sky classification differs in 0 pixels in every window. Relative error of the derived map vs the engine's: over 1e-6 in 80 - 98 % of the geometry pixels (float rounding), over 1e-5 in 28 - 87 %, over 1e-4 in 0 - 11 %, over 1e-3 in <= 0.003 %, over 4e-3 (the picture check's depth tolerance) in 0; the maximum is 1.7e-3 (distant geometry, where `1 - k z / w` cancels: the hardware depth is n / z, absolute error ~6e-8), 2.6e-4 - 4.1e-4 below a map value of 0.02, 4.7e-5 in the settled interior. Not bit-equal, tolerance-equal. All consumers read the map through the same image, so they see these values: GI / screen probes / material RT / water / RT shadows / lights / the engine's SSM, SSAO, DoF chain / `rt.debug` / the picture check; FSR prep already reads the hardware depth. Jitter: the z geometry carries the camera jitter in both, so the derived value is the jittered raster's; the render scale: the map and the scope have the scene target's size (the scope keeps the dropped RT's size). The scene A/B (below) is the end-to-end check.
* `rvk_rhitest` "depth map derived from the hardware depth (S-6)", both backends, `--validation`: two triangles at known view depths drawn as the engine's z pass (R32F colour + depth) and colour-less (depth only, no outputs); both depths turned into the map by the derive pass: worst relative error 9.3e-7 against the z pass's own output (0 pixels over 2e-5), sky exactly 1, depth of the two draws bit-equal, D3D12 vs Vulkan 0 of 6,144 pixels over 1e-4. `rvk_d3d9test` "depth-only pass without a pixel shader (S-6)": a colour-less pass writes the depth (a farther quad is hidden), exactly one pixel shader left out. `rvk_tests` 328 / 0, `dxil-check embedded` PASS incl. the two new shaders, ABI block `DepthZPush` in `abi_blocks.cpp`.

**Picture checks.** `native.picture_check` (owned vs composed frames, depth_z on, GPU-assisted validation): pub_interior ok (depth 0 differing pixels, 0.016 % colour), harbour_docks ok windows; the jungle spot reports FAILED windows with depth_z OFF too (3 of 21 windows baseline, 5 of 34 with it on: 0.47 - 0.89 % of the pixels, baseline depth max 3.19, with depth_z 0.09): a pre-existing wind / tree difference between owned and composed frames, not caused by this stage (depth_z on shows fewer differing depth pixels: 190 vs 1,252). Full A/B `water-compare -Mode full` arms jungle / pub_interior / harbour_docks (`Assert-ArmStages` accepted both arms: depth_z active only in the enhanced one): mean abs difference per pixel 2.85 / 0.44 / 0.27, same-state noise floor (an extra off-vs-off run) 2.43 / 0.97 / 0.58: inside the noise.

**GPU time (XTX, 24-frame means, 3 runs: off / on).** Hold (savegame): main z 0.379 / 0.336 / 0.366 -> 0.320 / 0.295 / 0.289 ms (-0.06), derive pass 0.013 - 0.046 ms, cascades 0.23 - 0.28 both, total 3.92 / 3.70 / 3.69 -> 3.59 / 3.68 / 3.66; jungle: main z 0.674 / 0.666 / 0.608 -> 0.602 / 0.621 / 0.609 (-0.04), derive 0.013 - 0.039, cascades 0.42 / 0.35 / 0.34 vs 0.68 / 0.36 / 0.36 (cascade stride noise), total 6.02 / 5.68 / 5.34 -> 6.11 / 5.66 / 5.57; pub_interior total 1.93 / 2.20 / 2.12 -> 1.93 / 1.89 / 2.05, derive 0.008. **On the XTX the net effect is inside the noise (-0.03 ms at best): the saving (one R32F export per fragment, the pixel-shader launch of 2/3 of the draws) is about what the derive pass costs.** 680M extrapolation with the 14.2 model [I]: the saved part is pixel / ROP work (x 10 - 15 as the post passes), the derive pass a full-screen 1600x900 pass (x 10): hold / jungle about -0.2 .. -0.5 ms, interiors ~0 .. +0.1 ms (no cascades, small z pass, the derive pass is pure cost); bytes: the R32F write of the z pass (4 B x ~1.5 overdraw x 1.44 M = ~8.6 MB per frame) becomes one 5.8 MB derive write + a depth read. The lower end of 14.3's -0.2 .. -0.9 is what the model supports; it needs the user's 680M run.

**Soaks** (private copy `risen-test-vk-s6`, instance 53, lock `rvk-s6`; all owned settings + `debug.ubershader` + depth_z on): GPU-assisted first runs pub_interior (compare, on) and three spots: 0 validation messages; D3D12 spots PASS (depth map derived every view, 0 errors); savegame hold `-Validation` PASS 0 messages; `rvk_rtwalk` walk PASS (491 s); turning burst `-Validation` PASS 0 messages (31,682 maps derived, 702 attachment-less z scopes per 5 s window); pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks with FSR performance + GI + AO + `-Validation` PASS (only the format-mismatch warnings of AMD's FSR shaders, accepted as in 7.44). One walk run ended early ("the game exited by itself", a closed window, no error in the log) and passed when repeated.

**Found on the way (not this stage).** With `debug.ubershader` on, jungle pictures show black foliage patches (near-black pixels 0.04 - 0.22 % of the picture vs 0.0003 % with the translated shaders, 0 in Wrapper / without the owned chain; seen with depth_z off, so independent of it): a defect of the ubershader path, Debug / off by default (resolved 2026-11-10: not the pixel shader but the vertex port's clip position, which failed the depth-equal test against the translated pass; shader-permutations.md 12). **Open.** (1) Cut-out z draws still use the translated pixel shader (no ISA check). (2) 680M numbers are a model. (3) `kept` / `left out` counters do not see replayed draws.

### 14.11 S-8 result: a cheaper sun-shadow mask filter and the Integrated GPU preset (2026-11-10; code 4de6626; one hidden setting `system.igpu_preset_done`, settings count 145; discrete-GPU defaults unchanged)

**A. Mask shader** (`shaders/shadows/shadow_mask.frag.hlsl`; same picture, fewer fetches / registers; shadows.md 4). (1) The pixel's depth and its four neighbours (the receiver plane's one-sided differences) come from **2 gathers instead of 5 point fetches**. (2) For `shadows.filter` medium / high (3x3 / 4x4 bilinear taps) the grid is one weighted pass over the texels it touches: the sum of the taps' bilinear weights is separable (wx(x) * wy(y)), so every texel is fetched once by 2x2 gathers: **3x3 = 4 gathers instead of 9, 4x4 = 4 (spacing <= 0.5, the far cascades) or 9 instead of 16**; the Low filter (2x2) stays the exact per-tap form (4 gathers either way). Algebra checked in numpy (`scratch/s8/algebra.py`): identical to the per-tap form to 1e-15 at zero receiver slope; with a slope the receiver plane is taken at the texel instead of at its tap (1 % mean, up to 0.3 in adversarial synthetic noise: a hair of penumbra shift on slanted receivers). (3) Only the cascades the pixel can reach get their coordinates + receiver plane computed (was 3 x 3 mat4 products per pixel), the VSM terrain fetch is skipped where the cascade term is 0, and where the RT shadow covers the pixel completely (`shadows.rt`, weight 1) the cascades and the terrain term are skipped. **RGA gfx1035 (RGA 2.9.1 offline): 77 -> 67 VGPRs, 58 -> 46 SGPRs, 0 spills / scratch** (ISA 5.1 -> 13.3 KB: the 4x4 / 3x3 weight code is unrolled; the executed path is shorter). Dynamic fetches per pixel, one cascade: Low 9 -> 6, Medium 14 -> 6, High 21 -> 11 (about twice that inside the 20 % blend band before a split).

**GPU, XTX (private copy `risen-test-vk-s8`, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `shadow_inland_a` hold, `-PerfFrame 3055` + `RVK_PERF_FRAMES=24`, three runs each, shared lock per run; the mask pass = the pass before the Solid pass, `scratch/s8/perfsum.py`)**:

| resolution | filter | old (ms, mean of 3) | new |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1600x900 | low | 0.053 | 0.055 |
| 1600x900 | medium | 0.051 | 0.054 |
| 2560x1440 | low | 0.095 | 0.095 |
| 2560x1440 | medium | 0.094 | 0.096 |

**No GPU change measurable on the XTX** (the pass is 0.05 / 0.095 ms, all the filters cost the same: it is not fetch-bound there; run-to-run spread +-0.004 ms). The fetch count falls by a third (Low) to 55 % (Medium), so on the 680M, where the model puts this pass at ~0.7 - 1.5 ms (x15), the saving is est. **-0.1..-0.25 ms (Low), -0.3..-0.5 ms (Medium)**: a model, not a measurement. The "half-res mask + depth-aware upsample under Low Bandwidth" and a tile-classification early-out were not built: the mask target is the engine's (its Blur reads it), a half-size intermediate would add a pass + an upsample, and without a measurable pass cost on the available hardware there is nothing to judge them by.

**Picture.** Six spots (shadow_inland_a / b / far, beach_sun, harbour_docks, jungle) x filters low / medium / high, old vs new shader (`RVK_SHADER_DIR` = the old text, no rebuild), plus same-shader control runs: a frozen rvkcompare hold is NOT pixel-stable between runs (wind, water, eye adaptation: the same shader twice differs by mean 2.5 - 10 / 255). On pixels that two repeats agree on (<= 2 / 255), old vs new at medium differs by > 4 / 255 on 0.3 - 1.8 % (> 12: <= 0.34 %, mean < 1), the same order as the unchanged Low path's run-to-run difference; the shot-reviewer agent (heat maps, 3x crops of the shell pile and sand of shadow_inland_b, the logs' shadow edge) found **no change in penumbra softness, no acne, no peter-panning, no leaks, no banding** for all three filters (the outliers it named were water splashes). Gates: `rvk_tests` 328 / 0, `dxil-check embedded` PASS (shadow_mask.frag signed), `rvk_rhitest` PASS (both backends), `rvk_d3d9test` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS, first game run `-Validation` + `VK_KHRONOS_VALIDATION_VALIDATE_GPU_BASED=GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED` (shadow_inland_a, filter medium): 0 messages; the spots above on Vulkan, the fake-integrated run also on D3D12 (no errors).

**B. The Integrated preset** (`settings::graphicsPresets()` entry 5 "igpu" / "Integrated"; `applyIntegratedDefaults`; overlay.md 2.9). On the first run on an integrated GPU (`caps::Report::integratedGpu`, the criterion of low bandwidth auto) every setting that is **still at its default** (not set by settings.toml / config.toml / the player, not locked) takes the preset's value, once (hidden `system.igpu_preset_done`, saved with settings.toml); rvk.log `settings: integrated GPU: Integrated preset chosen by default, N values set` + one `settings: integrated preset: key: old -> new` line each. The player can change everything afterwards (the overlay shows the "Integrated" segment on integrated GPUs, or while the values match it; a hand change = Custom). A discrete GPU never calls it (the run `plain_vk` has no such line; the defaults are still "High": tests `settings_integrated_defaults` + `settings_presets_and_tabs`). Test knob: `-Environment 'RVK_FAKE_INTEGRATED=1'` (the device counts as integrated: the preset AND low bandwidth auto) with `-PlayerDefaults` (the test baseline's explicit values would keep their own); verified on Vulkan + D3D12 (19 values set; a second run with `-KeepSettings`: "already applied", values kept).

Estimated 680M cost per choice (**a model**: 14.3's rule 680M ms = XTX x 6.5 geometry / x 15 pixel and compute / x 10 post, XTX numbers from the research docs; none of it measured on a 680M):

| choice (default -> preset) | what it is on the XTX | est. 680M gain, ms / frame |
|---|---|---|
| `rt.reflections` high -> off (water RT) | 0.8 - 1.0 ms + part of the TLAS (0.12 - 0.7) | 12 - 15 (the largest item) |
| `rt.preset` members low (rt.lod_scale 0.3, budget 384 MB, `shadows.rt_*` low, `gi.quality` / `gi.screen` low, `materials.rt_reflections` low / range 60 m, `lighting.light_shadow_quality` low) | only matter while shadows.rt / gi / traced materials / lamp shadows are on (off or cheap by default) | 0 by default, caps the cost if the player turns them on; lamp shadows stay `nearby` at quality low (est. 1 - 2) |
| `gi.enabled` stays false, `materials.quality` stays medium (no traced reflections: 1.4 - 2.8 ms XTX) | | 0 vs the default (21 - 42 ms avoided if a player picks High) |
| `water.ocean` fft -> gerstner, `water.ocean_quality` high -> low, `water.quality` high -> low | FFT cascades + foam compute, a few 0.1 ms | 2 - 4 |
| `shadows.map_size` 2048 -> 1024, `shadows.range` auto (100 m) -> 60 m | cascades are 3.0 / 6.1 / 3.6 ms on the 680M (z + ROP, 50 - 70 MB per drawn frame) | 0.5 - 1.5 |
| `lod.trees` / `lod.meshes` / `lod.view` 2x -> 1.5x | geometry volume -10..-15 % | 0.3 - 1 |
| `upscale.mode` off -> quality (1.5 x; forces the G-buffer; the one entry that switches something on) | the pixel part of the solid pass 4.6 / 9.7 / 12.3 ms (savegame / jungle / pub) / 2.25, minus the FSR pass (~3 ms x10) and the G-buffer's bandwidth | about 0 in the savegame, -3 in the jungle / pub: pays only where the scene is pixel-bound; Balanced or Performance are one click away |
| `shadows.filter` low stays; the cheaper mask (A) | | 0.1 - 0.25 |
| `vulkan.low_bandwidth` auto = on (unchanged) | | see low-bandwidth.md |

Summed, the model gives roughly 15 - 25 ms on a 680M outdoors (mostly the water RT); the user's 680M test is the real check. Not in the preset: render mode (Full), frame generation, `shadows.rt`, the clustered lights.

### 14.12 S-4 result: vertex pulling collapses the owned buckets; the permutation merge is not built (2026-11-10, branch `worktree-agent-a7033459935f162d1` on main ba4c917, code 9a1b22b + c4022e5; one Debug setting `native.vertex_pull`, Full, default off, needs `native.owned_persistent`; settings count 147)

**What was built (part b).** With `native.vertex_pull` the persistent owned draws of `ownedFlush` (static meshes, skinned actors, the cascades' z ops; not the trees' / grass' own flushes, which keep their instance streams) are drawn by a **pulled form of their scene ABI v2 vertex shader**: `TranslateOptions::pullVertices` (`src/shader/hlsl.cpp`), variant slot `SceneVsPull`. Its entry takes only `SV_VertexID`, `SV_InstanceID` and the ref stream, and builds the translator's own input struct `RvkVsIn` by reading every declared input register from the draw's **VertexFetch block** (`abi.h`, 128 bytes: per stream the reference pair of its data in the mesh arena + the stream offset and the stride; per register v0..v15 offset / stream / format; the D3D9 base vertex) through `rvkPullAttr` (`shaders/include/rvk_pull.hlsli`). The translated program after that is the same text as the input-assembler form (renamed `rvkVsMain`), so its clip position is bit-identical whenever the pulled inputs are (the lesson of shader-permutations.md 12); the ubershader's vertex port takes the pulled translated part (`RVK_UBER_PULL` beside `RVK_UBER_TRPOS`, `shader::uberVertexHlsl(..., pull)`). Device side: `computePullFetch` (prepareDraw, from the draw's layout: refused for user-pointer / per-instance streams, a buffer without an arena address, an unaligned stride / offset, a format without a conversion), the pulled pipeline beside the v2 one (same pixel shader and state, the ref stream as the only vertex input), `CachedDraw::pull` / `scenePipelinePull`, a fetch table parallel to the persistent record slots (`DrawRecordTable::ensureFetch`, created only while pulling is on; the MISS checks and the rolling scrub compare it with the records and blocks: `DrawRecordTable::holds`), and `ownedFlush` buckets a pulled item by **pipeline x dynamic state x index binding x view slot** (no vertex input in the key) with `vertexOffset = 0` in the CPU-written and the GPU-compacted arguments (`owned_compact.comp.hlsl` job flag; the shader adds the base vertex: D3D12's `SV_VertexID` ignores the command's base vertex, Vulkan's `VertexIndex` includes it). GPU safety: an item is pulled only when the CPU twin of its slot holds a valid block whose every read stream has a reference and a stride (`pullFetchUsable`), else it is drawn with its vertex input (counted `no fetch block`). The Vulkan mesh arena always has device addresses now (it had them with the RT tier only). rvk.log `native: vertex pull (5 s)` (per view kind: flushes, items / flush, pulled, indirect commands / flush, distinct pipelines and index bindings; refusal counts; also while off = the baseline), `native: stages (5 s)` `vertex_pull=...`, `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1` knows the key.

**The input assembler's conversions, exactly.** A first version computed `c / 255.0` in the shader: the new rhitest case found **every** UNORM8 vertex one ulp off in at least one channel (126 of the 256 codes: DXC / the driver turn the division into a multiply by the rounded reciprocal), SNORM16 / UNORM16 in 1.5 - 9 % of the codes; the input assembler rounds `c / (2^n - 1)` correctly on both backends. `c / N = c / 2^n + c / (2^n N)` (the first product exact, a power of two) is correctly rounded for every code of N = 255, 65535, 32767 (checked exhaustively in IEEE float32, fused or not) and is what `rvk_pull.hlsli` uses; floats are raw copies, half floats `f16tof32`, D3D12's UNORM read of UBYTE4 (the translator scales it back under push flag bit 17 + register) is mirrored by the fetch block's format code.

**Gates.**
* `rvk_rhitest` new case **"vertex pulling = the input assembler, bit for bit (S-4)"** (`tools/rhitest/main.cpp`, `shaders/test/rhi_pull.vert.hlsl`): 65,536 vertices x 13 attributes (FLOAT1..4, D3DCOLOR, UBYTE4, UBYTE4N, SHORT2N/4N, USHORT2N/4N, FLOAT16_2/4: every code of every 8- and 16-bit channel), each read by the pipeline's vertex input and by `rvkPullAttr` from the same buffer: **0 of 65,536 vertices differ in every format on Vulkan and D3D12**, `--validation` 0 messages (Vulkan; D3D12 the case passes, see below). `testIndirectPull` and every other case PASS on both backends.
* `rvk_d3d9test` new case **"owned pull (S-4)"** through the real path (computePullFetch -> CachedDraw -> ownedDerive -> the fetch table -> ownedFlush -> the compaction): eight quads in two vertex layouts (strides 28 / 48) behind a non-trivial matrix, drawn as the game draws them (an input-assembler z pre-pass, then the owned colour draws with ZFUNC EQUAL): **one indirect command for both layouts, every pixel equal** to the input-assembler reference; off = 2 commands, equal; a slot without a valid fetch block = drawn with its vertex input in its own bucket, equal; **negative control** (the position read as FLOAT3, w = 1 + 1 ulp in the data becomes 1): 4,089 bytes differ, so the check sees a last-bit difference. Vulkan and D3D12 `--validation`: PASS, 0 failures, 0 validation errors.
* `rvk_tests` 328 / 0 (settings count 147; `RvkVertexFetch` has an `abi_blocks.cpp` entry). `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded` PASS (90), `dxil-check uber` (census jungle + town) 3,441 / 3,441, new `dxil-check corpus corpus --pull` 149 / 149 vertex shaders (SPIR-V + signed DXIL). `tools/vs_equiv.py` 52 EQUAL (3 unsupported as before), `tools/ubershader_check.py` 259 EQUAL (63 NO-HLSL, 4 unsupported as before).
* Game (private copy `risen-test-vk-s4`, instance 56, lock `rvk-s4`; player defaults + every owned stage + `rt.table_entries` + `lighting.clustered` / `full_lit` + `debug.ubershader`): first run `-Validation` + GPU-assisted at jungle: 0 VUID / error lines. **Black-pixel check** (jungle, `scratch/uberfix/blk.py`): 0.0000 % near-black in every burst frame with pulling on, as off (pub_interior 0.108 - 0.117 % in both arms: its own dark pixels, the same with pulling off and with default settings). **Picture check** (`native.picture_check`, jungle / pub_interior / harbour_docks, freeze_wait 1800, no FSR): every settled window `ok` with pulling on (worst view 0.0246 / 0.0394 / 0.0160 % against the control's 0.0146 / 0.0410 / 0.0147 %); the one `FAILED` window (3.1 % at the arrival at harbour_docks, the window after an UNRELIABLE one) appears identically in the off arm (3.098 %): not this stage. MISS 0, DIFFERING 0 (owned, compaction, cluster cull `FALSE NEGATIVES 0`), bucket key clashes 0 in every run, the same counts in the off arm. D3D12 game run: see below.

**Commands per view (the owned flush, one flush per view and pass; `native: vertex pull (5 s)`, settled windows):**

| scene | view | items / flush | indirect commands off -> on | distinct pipelines | index bindings |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| jungle | main z | 485 | **138 -> 5** | 5 | 1 |
| jungle | main solid | 86 | **66 -> 21** | 21 | 1 |
| jungle | cascades | 111 | **42.3 -> 4** | 4 | 1 |
| savegame hold | main z | 523 | **91 -> 7** | 7 | 1 |
| savegame hold | main solid | 42 | **35.1 -> 17.6** | 17.6 | 1 |
| savegame hold | cascades | 136 | **30.4 -> 4.6** | 4.6 | 1 |
| pub_interior | z / solid / cascades | 88 / 29 / 33 | 30 / 24 / 8 -> 4 / 16 / 1.3 | 4 / 16 / 1.3 | 1 |

The whole mesh arena's indices live in one block here (index bindings 1.0 everywhere), so once the vertex input is out of the key **the command count is exactly the number of distinct pipelines**: the z and cascade passes reach single digits (the gate of 14.4), the solid pass is left with its material permutations (part a). The 5 s totals: buckets = indirect packets 85,600 -> 10,240 per window at jungle (-88 %).

**GPU (XTX, `-PerfFrame` + `RVK_PERF_FRAMES=24`, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy, three runs per arm interleaved, code 9a1b22b (before the fetch-block load change of c4022e5); ms; `scratch/s4/sum.py`):**

| scene | arm | total | main solid | main z | cascades | CPU engine + HAL |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| savegame hold (frame 6000) | off | 3.682 / 3.692 / 3.591 | 0.801 / 0.758 / 0.736 | 0.327 / 0.342 / 0.330 | 0.240 / 0.241 / 0.241 | 9.33 / 9.09 / 8.91 |
| | pull | 3.640 / 3.652 / 3.625 | 0.760 / 0.775 / 0.766 | 0.324 / 0.339 / 0.308 | 0.242 / 0.240 / 0.251 | 8.81 / 8.85 / 8.93 |
| jungle (frame 4200) | off | 5.522 / 5.635 / 5.633 | 1.443 / 1.511 / 1.478 | 0.606 / 0.614 / 0.609 | 0.360 / 0.363 / 0.393 | 10.59 / 10.18 / 10.20 |
| | pull | 5.443 / 5.556 / 5.406 | 1.445 / 1.521 / 1.406 | 0.584 / 0.590 / 0.584 | 0.360 / 0.358 / 0.344 | 10.26 / 10.44 / 10.41 |

Reading: **the jungle main z pass is 0.024 ms faster in every run** (0.610 -> 0.586 mean, -4 %, larger than its run-to-run spread; hold 0.333 -> 0.324, inside the spread), the solid pass and the cascades are inside the noise, the frame total -0.03 (hold) / -0.13 ms (jungle) inside the total's noise; **CPU: no difference** (the flush builds fewer packets, ~ -1 us; the per-item work is unchanged). Pipeline binds per frame 178 -> 176 (hold), 242 -> 239 (jungle). That is what 14.3 predicted for the XTX (~0); the 680M part of the prediction (-0.14 .. -0.7 ms: 1 - 3 us of front-end work per small indirect draw, 163 -> ~30 commands per frame) remains a model: on the XTX the removed ~250 commands per frame (jungle) cost ~0.03 ms. **Costs:** the pulled vertex shaders have the same registers on gfx1035 (RGA 2.9.1, 19 in-game pairs: 81.7 -> 81.8 VGPRs mean, max 125 -> 124, 0 spills) but **2 - 4 x the ISA bytes** (every input's 14-way format switch is compiled; at run time the branches are uniform per draw). **Loads per vertex** (ISA of the in-game modules after commit c4022e5, which loads the fetch block once per vertex instead of four words per input): the ref-stream fetch, 4 - 7 `global_load_dwordx4` + 1 `global_load_dword` of block metadata (the same 128 bytes for every vertex of a draw: cache hits), and **exactly one load per declared input** in its taken branch (`dword` / `x2` / `x3` / `x4` by format; POSITION0 FLOAT3 = one `global_load_dwordx3`); the z vertex shader with one input: 6 vector loads + the ref fetch, the lit ones (5 - 8 inputs): 10 - 15. The input assembler issues one typed load per attribute too; what pulling adds is the metadata (not scalarised: the slot comes from the per-instance ref, a VGPR). Attributes of one interleaved vertex are separate loads of the same cache lines, not one combined `dwordx4`: the layout is per draw (dynamic), and 16-byte alignment is not given (strides 28 / 48 / 64 ...). **Vertex bytes per pass** (`native: vertex pull (5 s)`, pulled items' indices x the bytes their inputs read: an upper bound before the post-transform vertex reuse and before the cluster cull; savegame hold / jungle, per view): main z 1.49 M / 1.60 M indices, **26.4 / 26.8 MB** (17.7 / 16.7 B per index, **of it POSITION0 17.9 / 19.2 MB**); main solid 0.18 M indices, 11.5 MB (64 B per index, position 2.2 MB) at the hold; a cascade 0.31 / 0.21 M indices, 5.9 / 3.7 MB (position 3.7 / 2.6 MB). A separate exact float32 position stream for the depth-only passes would cut the z pass's fetched bytes by ~28 - 32 % (the non-position bytes are the masked draws' texcoords and the skinned weights / indices) and, more, the cache lines touched (the 12 position bytes sit in 28 - 64-byte interleaved vertices); not built (the follow-up's question). one more pipeline per owned v2 pipeline (the pulled one beside it: e.g. 425 vs ~380 variant modules built in a three-spot run; first-sight compile ~13 ms each on a worker, cached); cold pipelines built per run 11 - 13 in both arms (warm caches).

**Part (a), the permutation merge: not built, by measurement.** (1) The solid pass's remaining 16 - 21 commands are its distinct pixel shaders: the ubershader's material permutations, which differ by the engine's graph expressions (`XFX_PS_DIFFUSE` / `NORMAL` / `SPECULAR` ... text: 47 diffuse, 16 normal, 50 specular forms in the census, 14.1.3) far more than by flags; merging them needs L3 (the graph as data: a recipe table / interpreter in the shader), a project of its own with its own EQUAL gates, not a flag change. (2) Module sharing by text (exact by construction: two shaders whose variant text is equal share one module and hence their pipelines and buckets; implemented, on while `native.vertex_pull` is on) found **0** equal texts in the game (425 modules, 0 shared): the z vertex shaders differ in their translated text, not only in their declared inputs. (3) The flag part of L2, measured on gfx1035 (RGA 2.9.1, the census's 159 lit `PS_Default` requests with 0 / 1 / 2 point lights compiled as they are and with `XFX_PNTLIGHTCOUNT` = 3, the register need of a class module whose light loop takes the count from the record; `scratch/s4/lc_rga.ps1`): GI + cluster form (the player defaults' owned lit form) mean 133.4 -> 145.4 VGPRs (+9 %), class max 183 -> 197 (+7.7 %), **58 of 159 members above +10 %**; plain scene v2 form mean 89.3 -> 79.7, max 149 -> 154 (+3 %), 12 of 159 above +10 %. By the 14.4 rule (the class MAX) the light-count merge would pass, but a third of the default form's members would pay 10 - 30 % more registers, and light count is not what splits the owned solid pass (with `lighting.full_lit` every lit draw asks for the 3-light shader already; the remaining split is the graph text), so it would remove few commands. (4) The z / cascade classes are already 4 - 7 commands per view with pulling, so their merge has little left to take (2 - 5 commands). **Recommendation:** leave the material permutations split (S-5's own shaders for trees / grass and a recipe-table material shader are where a real merge would come from); the loading win of rank 7 stays open.

**D3D12.** Game run `-Api d3d12 -Validation` (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`: the debug layer without GPU-based validation) at jungle + pub_interior, pulling on: the debug layer printed only the known DSV-format warning (33 times, the same in the off arm), MISS / DIFFERING 0, commands per flush as on Vulkan (main z 3, solid 5 at pub_interior); jungle near-black 0.0007 % in the first run (8 pixels: a dark ellipse in mid-air in all three burst frames, not a depth hole), 0.0000 % in the repeat and in the off arm: a transient of that run, not reproduced. The S-4 rhitest case and the d3d9test case pass on D3D12 (above).

**Open.** (1) The trees' / grass' / billboards' own flushes are not pulled (they keep their instance streams: S-5). (2) The 680M gain is a model: the user's run decides. (3) `rvk_rhitest --d3d12 --validation` (GPU-based validation) grows past 8 - 12 GB after the texture-ops case and was killed, with this branch AND with main's own build (ba4c917): pre-existing, in the abi probe / parity part; the S-4 case passed in that run, and the D3D12 run without validation is clean. (4) ISA size of the pulled vertex shaders (2 - 4 x): a per-layout specialisation (formats as spec constants / defines when a pipeline only ever sees one layout) would shrink it, at the price of pipelines per layout again.

### 14.13 Bone-history motion: the characters' per-object motion without vertex captures (2026-11-10, branch `worktree-agent-abd753f93bcc71e32` on main 1f79d2d; one Debug setting `native.bone_motion` = off | on | compare, Full, restart, default off, needs `native.owned_actors`; settings count 148)

**Problem (7.41 open 1, 7.44 open 1, 7.45 issue 1).** Per-object motion for FSR / frame generation (`upscale.object_motion=characters`, forced on by any FSR mode) took every skinned solid draw's previous positions from a vertex-shader capture (`cap6`: the world position per vertex slot written to a ring buffer, the previous frame's capture of the same draw read back). A captured draw is composed late, so with object motion (or the RT scene keeping its capture for it, `RtScene::setCharacterMotion`) the actors' main SOLID ops were 0 % owned.

**What was built.**
* **History in the bone table** (`gpuscene/bone_table.*`): with `native.bone_motion` on at start the table is made with twice its capacity; record `kCapacity + slot` holds the slot's image of the PREVIOUS frame (`BoneTable::kHistoryDelta` = 1,024 x 3,216 bytes past the current one). The first serve of a slot in a frame copies the current image into it when the slot was served in the previous frame with the same history tag (`StaticOps::actorHistTag`: HRA, LOD, morph pick: the morph streams the weights refer to) and the root bone (c33..c35 .w) moved less than 500 units (a teleport / respawn / quick-load); else the history is marked invalid. Validity = c231.x = 1 (c231 lies past the last bone row of 66 bones and is never read). A part that does not move writes nothing (prev == cur is remembered). The buffer is zero-filled at creation (`GpuTable::zero`: the 240-frame read-back covers both halves; a never-written current slot would otherwise read back garbage). `BoneTable::prevOf(base)` gives the history base only for a window base the table handed out (GPU safety). rvk.log `native: bone history (5 s)` (copied / already equal / invalid by gap, tag, jump).
* **The bone-motion vertex shader forms** (`d3d9/bone_motion.cpp`): provider slot `VariantSlot::BoneVs` (tag `bmot5`) and its scene forms `SceneVsBone` (v1, the composed draws), `SceneVsBone2` (v2, the persistent owned records) and `SceneVsBonePull` (S-4's pulled form), plus scene forms of the G-buffer motion pixel variants (`SceneGbufferMotion` / `-M`, v1 and v2). The translated program is unchanged; its epilogue (after `precise rvk_pos` is final) runs `rvk_main()` a SECOND time with VS window 2 pointed at the history image (`RVK_BONE_PREV`: `rvkVsWinBm`, `rvk_vsw2Ov` in `rvk_draw.hlsli`; `TranslateOptions::rerunnable` emits `rvk_resetRegs()`, every temp / address / output register back to 0 first: D3D9 shaders read components they never wrote) and outputs the previous world position as `rvk_prevWorld` (location 15) exactly as `cap6` does (the precise clip position through `invViewProj`). The record's `prevVs` (`DrawRecord::prevVs`, `toScenePacket` / `ownedBuild`) carries the history image's base; 0 = no motion of its own (the camera's), and the shader never dereferences a zero pair (the hoisted-load rule). The first run's clip position is not touched: the z-equal colour pass holds (black-pixel check below).
* **Who decides.** `Device::bonePredict` (prepareDraw, before the observers): a skinned main-view solid draw with the G-buffer motion target, scene issue on and VS window 2 a bone image with history, whose bone-motion modules exist. Then `Device::boneMotionDraw()` is true for the observers: `ObjectMotion` requests no capture (`N skinned draws without a capture (bone-history motion)` in `enh: object motion:`), the RT scene skips its solid-pass capture when the skinning pass has the character (`RtScene::observeSkinned`); `sceneDecide` gives the draw the bone forms (v1, v2, pulled; `bonePipes_`), the retained draw remembers it (`CachedDraw::boneMotion`), the owned paths tell the observers the same (`boneOfCached`), `deriveScene` refuses a bone record without the motion target (variant site 8). rvk.log `native: bone motion (5 s)`, `native: stages` `bone_motion=`, `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1` knows the key.
* **Compare mode** (`native.bone_motion=compare`, Vulkan): the captures stay the motion source; the capture vertex shader of a draw whose window 2 is a bone image with history (capture header bit 30, `Device::boneCompareBit`) also runs the bone path and accumulates into a Readback buffer (frame param 6): max difference of the two previous positions on the previous screen (px) and in world units, vertices compared / above 0.01 px, plus the second run on THIS frame's image against the first run (`native: bone motion compare (5 s)`; capture tag `cap6c8`).

**RT scene and the bone table (the user's question).** With the skinning pass on (`debug.rt_skin`, default) the RT scene already needs no solid-pass capture for a character that has a skinning job of the frame (7.45); it only kept it as object motion's previous-position source. With bone-history motion that reason is gone: `rt: scene: characters` shows `solid-pass draws 4773 with / 27 without a job, 4773 without a capture request` (before: `4766 with / 42 without, 0 without a capture request`). The characters WITHOUT a job (27 - 57 draws per 5 s: draws `skinEligible` refuses, e.g. a part whose layout the skin pass cannot read, and the first frame of a part) keep the capture: their geometry for the TLAS comes from it. Feeding the skin pass from the bone table instead of the draws' bone constants would not remove these (they fail on the vertex layout / morph streams, not the palette) and would not change what the skinning pass computes (the owned draws' observers already see the table's CPU twin in the register file, 7.41); not built.

**Results** (private copy `risen-test-vk-bh`, instance 57, lock `rvk-bonehist`; Full + player defaults (RT scene on) + the owned chain incl. `owned_actors` / `owned_shadows` + `upscale.mode=performance`; logs `rvk-work/scratch/bonehist/`):
* **Owned share of the actors' main solid ops** (rvkcompare `hero_idle` + `pub_idle`, `show_actors=1`, clock running): bone motion off: 0 % (no `main solid` entry: every solid character draw captured); on: **100 %** (3,360 of 3,360 ops per 5 s; main z 100 %), DIFFERING 0, actor images compared on check frames 675 - 900 per 5 s, 0 differing.
* **Compare vs the capture path** (compare mode, `rt.allow=false` and RT on, same spots): max difference of the previous position on the previous screen **0.23 px (pub) / 0.39 px (hero)**; in world units up to 4.84 at the hero (x = -38,559). The same shader run twice on THIS frame's image differs from itself by the same 4.84 units (0.22 at the pub): the difference is the clip-to-world reconstruction's rounding along the view ray (a 1-ulp difference of the clip position at Risen's coordinates becomes units in the world, almost nothing on screen), which the capture path has as well; it is not the bone history. With the world operand of the view-projection instead of the reconstruction the rerun agrees to 1 ulp (0.0039 units at x = 38,559), but that operand is not the world space the motion pixel shader compares with (motion appeared on still characters): rejected. `rvk_d3d9test` "bone motion (14.13)" (identity camera: no rounding) measures **0.000000 px / 0.000000 units** over 64 vertices, the negative control (the capture chain pointed at the frame before the previous one) 0.257 units.
* **Pictures.** `gbuffer.debug=motion` at hero_idle / pub_idle (8 consecutive frames, FSR performance): the bone path's motion stays inside the characters' silhouettes (every other pixel exactly the background), magnitude as the capture path's (hero: mean deviation 5.6 - 6.0 vs 5.7, max 18 - 21 vs 19), plausible per body part; no speckles / unwritten areas. Note: in the capture arm only the FIRST frame of each screenshot burst carries character motion, frames 2 - 8 show none (in both RT on and off runs; pre-existing, not investigated); the bone path carries it on every frame. Final FSR pictures (8 frames, shot-reviewer): no ghosting / trails / stair-stepping on the hero or the pub NPCs in either arm, no artefact unique to the bone path (an earlier build that used the view-projection operand showed motion on still parts: rejected, above). Black-pixel check (`blk.py`, all owned stages + RT + table lighting): jungle 0.0000 % with the bone path (0.0001 - 0.0002 % off), pub_idle 0.074 - 0.076 % in both arms (its own dark pixels). Picture check (`native.picture_check`, pub_interior + pub_idle + hero_idle, no FSR, `gbuffer.debug=motion` for the motion target): 6 settled windows `ok`, 0 `FAILED` (the idle spots are `UNRELIABLE`: the scene moves).
* **Soaks** (bone path on, DIFFERING 0 / MISS 0, actor images 0 differing in every run): GPU-assisted validation (`-Validation` + `GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED`, hero_idle + pub_idle, final build and the two before it): PASS, 0 errors (only AMD's FSR `rw_*` storage-format warnings); `rvk_rtwalk` walk (quick-load) PASS; all owned stages + `rt.table_entries` + table lighting at jungle / pub_idle PASS; D3D12 (`-Api d3d12 -Validation`, `RVK_NO_GBV=1`): PASS, main solid 100 % owned, only the known 33 DSV-format warnings.
* **CPU / GPU** (savegame hold 40 s, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, shared copy, FSR performance + RT scene on, rounds interleaved off / on, engine + HAL CPU and GPU ms per frame, first window dropped): off CPU 14.09 / 9.47 / 9.67, GPU 2.95 / 2.82 / 3.06; on CPU 11.90 / 10.21 / 9.59, GPU 2.63 / 3.10 / 3.08. Inside the noise (the first off round is an outlier: another agent's build); the second skinning run per character vertex (~ 19 k skinned vertices per view at the savegame, 7.41) is not resolvable on the XTX. At the hold the RT scene's solid-pass capture requests drop from all (`7516 with a job, 0 without a capture request`) to none (`6950 ... 6950 without a capture request`).
* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 328 / 0 (settings count 148); `rvk_d3d9test --validation` PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan and D3D12 (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`), new case "bone motion (14.13)" (the history: new slot, prev = last frame's image, still part writes nothing, tag change / gap / root jump invalid, `prevOf` guards; the compare part through the device's own capture path, Vulkan); `rvk_rhitest --validation` PASS on both backends (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`); `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded` 90 / 90, `uber` 3,441 / 3,441, `dxil-check corpus corpus --scene --bone` / `--scene2 --bone` / `--bone-pull` 149 / 149 vertex shaders, `--capture-bone` 149 / 149, `corpus corpus --scene2 --bone` / `--bone-pull` / `--capture-bone` 0 failures; `vs_equiv.py` 52 EQUAL (3 unsupported), `ubershader_check.py` 259 EQUAL (63 NO-HLSL, 4 unsupported), as before (the default translation text is unchanged: `TranslateOptions::defines` / `rerunnable` / `$pos` only act in the bone forms).
* **Ubershader.** The bone forms are always the translated program (a provider-based scene form is never built from the ubershader port, `Device::variantWorker`); with `debug.ubershader` the owned skinned SOLID draws use them while the z pass keeps the port, whose clip position is the translated one by construction (`RVK_UBER_TRPOS`, shader-permutations.md 12). Porting the second run into `ge_default.vert.hlsl` would need the port to take its bone window as a parameter; not built.

**Open.** (1) The compare's residue (0.2 - 0.4 px max) is the clip-to-world reconstruction's noise (equal to the same shader's rerun on the same image); an exact comparison would need both paths to keep the world position before the view-projection in the SAME space the motion pixel shader uses (the operand of `c0..c3` is not it). (2) The capture arm's motion only on the first frame of a screenshot burst (pre-existing). (3) Rigid moving draws (`upscale.object_motion=all`) still use captures: the same scheme needs a previous world row in the object table. (4) Characters without a skinning job (27 - 72 solid draws per 5 s) keep the RT scene's capture and stay composed. (5) Compare mode is Vulkan only (the Readback buffer is reached by address).

### 14.14 S-5 result: grass distance thinning is built; pulled tree / grass flushes, packed vertices, half wind and a far-field grass pixel shader are measured and not built (2026-11-10, branch `worktree-agent-a307de4ff041e56bb` on main 6d7ffe0 + the tooling merge 171e9e0, code 038bf5b; one Debug setting `native.own_foliage`, Full, default off; settings count 149)

**Measurement first (jungle, XTX, player defaults + every owned stage + `debug.ubershader`, in-run A/B with `scripts/perf-ab.ps1`, 3 pairs of 240-frame windows, stage states checked per window).** The grass's whole GPU cost was measured by scaling every grass instance to 0 (`-Environment 'RVK_S5_GRASS_OFF=1'`, `native.own_foliage` as the toggle): main solid 1.080 -> 0.922 ms (**-0.159**), main z 0.488 -> 0.393 (**-0.095**), GPU frame -0.49 (A windows noisy, sd 0.53); the cascades draw no grass (7.39). So **all of the grass is ~0.25 ms of the jungle's z + solid on the XTX**; the trees are the larger part of 14.3's "instanced geometry". Everything S-5 can win on the grass is a share of those 0.25 ms (x ~10 - 15 on the 680M model of 14.3).

**What was built: grass distance thinning tied to `lod.view`** (`sc::grassThinParams` / `sc::grassThin`, `src/native/static_compose.*`; applied in `StaticOps::composeVegetation` (the CPU-bytes path and the F7-1 retained buffers, whose reuse key now holds the thinning state) and in the F7-2 owned pass where an op's converted instances are copied into the frame's block). Beyond `start = end x clamp(0.35 x lod.view, 0.2, 0.9)` of the grid's view range (`end` = grid +0x54: the same object for a context's z op and solid op) the share of blades kept falls linearly to 35 % at `end`; a blade's hash (its position bits) decides, and it shrinks to scale 0 over a band of 0.12 of that share instead of vanishing (no pop). Only the instance's xy scale (bytes 0x20 / 0x24) moves: **the wind math is untouched** (position, rotation, wind flag, bend as before; a blade at scale 0 collapses to its root, degenerate triangles). **Exact by construction across the z-equal pair** (shader-permutations.md 12): in Full every grass draw (composed, composed late, owned, check frames) reads OUR converted instance bytes, and the decision is a pure CPU function of the instance, the camera snapped to 1 m (100 units) and the grid's range, so a blade's z pre-pass and colour pass get the same bytes; no GPU float path was added. Off while `native.scene_ops_compare` compares the engine's bytes. rvk.log `native: own foliage (5 s)` (streams thinned, instances, shrunk / removed), `native: stages (5 s)` `own_foliage=active(N grass instances thinned)`, `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1` knows the key; the run-time toggle works (perf-ab: every window matched its arm). Unit test `native_grass_thinning` (near blades untouched, far share 0.2 - 0.55, gradual shrink, bit-identical re-thinning, the snap cell, lod.view moves the start).

* **Share thinned** (rvkcompare jungle): `lod.view` 2 (player default) 34 % of the instances shrunk or removed, 27 % removed; `lod.view` 1 56 % / 47 %; savegame walk (lod 2) 24 % / 19 %.
* **GPU (perf-ab, own_foliage off -> on):** lod.view 2: jungle GPU frame -0.083 ms (z-score -0.7), solid 1.139 -> 1.105 (**-0.034**, -3.0 %), z 0.496 -> 0.474 (**-0.023**, -4.5 %); swamp_camp +0.006 (noise). lod.view 1: jungle GPU -0.094, solid -0.039 (-3.8 %), z -0.034 (-7.3 %); swamp_camp GPU -0.094, solid -0.027, z -0.013.
