That is ~25 - 30 % of the grass's 0.25 ms at the jungle (680M model: -0.3 .. -0.9 ms there). **CPU:** inside the noise in every pair.
* **Pictures.** Jungle / swamp_camp bursts (8 frames, 1 apart, frozen game time) off vs on: the run-to-run difference (mean 2.0 / 0.7 at lod 2, 2.2 / 1.5 at lod 1) lies in the tree canopies and the depth of field (the same-state noise of separate runs, 7.42: 2.4 at the jungle), not in visible grass gaps; neighbour frames inside each burst 0.01 - 0.03 in both arms (no flicker). Savegame walk burst (`rvk_rtwalk`, clock running, 10 frames): neighbour differences 0.38 - 0.54 with thinning vs 0.42 - 0.59 without, luma constant: no pop, the grass sways as before. Nets `net8_n/e/s/w`: the nets are identical between the arms (the run-to-run differences are the spots' sky / sun state), near-black shares as in the off arm (each spot has its own dark pixels, 0.02 - 0.41 % in both). **Black pixels** (`blk.py`): jungle and swamp_camp 0.0000 % in every frame of both arms at both lod.view values, and in the first `-Validation` run. **Picture check** (`native.picture_check`, jungle / pub_interior / harbour_docks, no FSR): every settled window `ok` (worst view 0.0253 / 0.0394 / 0.0160 % against the control's 0.0144 - 0.0410 %), **FAILED 0** of 25 windows (the rest UNRELIABLE: arrivals).
* **Owned checks** with thinning on (all runs): DIFFERING 0 (owned static, trees, actors, vegetation plan / template / registers / converted / count), MISS 0, F7-1 streams reused 100 % on a still camera; 0 `[error]` lines; the first run with `-Validation` (Vulkan, jungle): 0 validation messages. No new GPU-address code (the bytes travel the existing instance-stream path).

**Measured and not built.**
1. **Vertex pulling of the trees' / grass' own flushes.** New census line `native: foliage flush calls (5 s)` (`Device::treeFlushStats.kind*`): a tree flush has 131 items (indirect draws) in **72 indirect calls**; a pulled form with sorted items would need **4.6** calls (distinct vertex shader x pixel shader x dynamic state x index binding); grass: 28 items in 18 calls -> 3.5. At the jungle (87 fps, 2.8 tree + 1.6 grass flushes per frame) that is ~210 calls per frame; S-4 (14.12) measured 133 calls removed = -0.024 ms on the XTX, so the ceiling here is ~0.04 ms. Pulling merges only API calls: the **indirect draws stay 131 / 28 per flush** (every template run is its own mesh / index range), and those are what the 680M front-end model of 14.3 charges. It would need an instance position on both backends (D3D12 `SV_InstanceID` excludes the start instance, Vulkan's includes it: the ref stream would have to carry it), a pulled form with per-instance streams, per-flush fetch blocks (the flushes' records live in the ring, not in the persistent slots S-4's fetch table indexes) and the grass block's address per flush. Not built for <= 0.04 ms; the census line stays for the 680M capture.
2. **16-byte pulled vertices** need a second, packed copy of the tree / grass meshes; a quantized position breaks the z-equal pair with every composed form of the same geometry until none is left: that is 14.4b's load-time layout.
3. **Half-precision wind.** The clip position of every ubershader vertex shader is the TRANSLATED text's (`RVK_UBER_TRPOS`, 14.9, shader-permutations.md 12), so the port's own wind only feeds varyings; a half wind would add work beside the float wind the position needs. Changing the position's wind would mean changing every translated form of every pass (z, colour, cascades, composed late) together, for vertex shaders that are not the limiter (~72 VGPRs) on geometry worth ~0.25 ms in total. Not built: the wind and the sway are bit-identical to before.
4. **Far-field grass pixel shader.** The grass's whole solid cost is 0.16 ms at the jungle; thinning already removes a third of the far blades. A far variant needs a second pipeline per grass bucket and the batches split by distance; ceiling < 0.05 ms on the XTX. Not built.

**Captures still requested by tree / grass / billboard draws in Full with `native.own_foliage` on** (user goal: no vertex captures on the owned path): **one source only, the animated SpeedTrees of the RT scene**: `RtScene::observeTree` (capture variant `cap4`, main view solid pass, instanced batches included) while `animated_` holds, i.e. while `shadows.rt` with its wind option (default on) or GI foliage (`gi.cpp` `setAnimated`, 40 / 80 m) wants swaying trees; then `ownedTreesOk()` is false and the trees (main view + cascades) stay composed. **Grass: none** (the RT scene refuses the vegetation vertex class, `rt_scene.cpp` "vertex shader class"; GI / material RT tally registers only, `ownedVegOk`; object motion observes static / instanced / skinned only). **Tree billboards: none** (no RT scene kind, no object-motion kind). A capture-free tree source can re-run the SpeedTree wind (`mulWindMatrix` with the view block's c14..c29, the rows' c32..c35, the leaf angles) in a compute pass over the owned trees' plan (rows ring + templates, 7.25 / 7.46); thinning does not touch trees.

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 331 / 0 (after the main merge); `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors (the process then crashes at exit after "1 leaked VkBuffer": identical with main's own build of 2026-11-10 22:32, pre-existing), D3D12 `--validation` PASS 0 / 0; `rvk_rhitest --validation` Vulkan PASS, D3D12 PASS (also `--d3d12 --validation`, the new default without GBV); `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded` 90 / 90, `dxil-check uber` (census jungle + town) 3,441 / 3,441 (no shader text changed). RGA: no new shader module exists (the thinning is CPU-side): nothing to compare. `rvk_rare` billboards not run: S-5 changes nothing on the billboard path.

**Open.** (1) The 680M capture decides the pulled tree / grass flushes (the census line gives the call counts). (2) The thinning constants (start 0.35 x lod.view, far share 35 %, band 0.12) are art constants judged on XTX pictures; the user's look at the far grass is the last word. (3) No D3D12 game run with thinning (CPU-only change, backend-independent).

### 14.15 X-1: no vertex captures on the owned path for actors, rigid objects and RT characters; the capture census (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a5c78a21002ed4cee` on main 6d7ffe0 + the merges up to main of 2026-11-11; one Debug setting `native.rigid_motion`, Full, restart, default off; settings count 150 with S-5's `native.own_foliage`)

**The census** (rvk.log `native: captures (5 s)`, every 5 s in every mode; `Device::requestVertexCapture(buffer, instances, CaptureBy, CaptureKind)`, `captureCensusFrame` in `d3d9/bone_motion.cpp`): every vertex-capture request per requester (`motion skinned`, `motion rigid` = enh/object_motion; `rt solid without a skin job`, `rt solid for motion`, `rt cascade`, `rt skin verify`, `rt tree` = enh/rt_scene; `other`) x draw kind (`static`, `instanced mesh`, `skinned`, `morph` = skinned with POSITION1..3 streams, `speedtree`, `vegetation`, `billboard`, `other`) x the path that showed the observers the draw (`composed`, `retained` = a packet-cache replay that the capture refused, `owned` = an owned / scene-draw replay that the capture forced back to composed); `avoided N` = requests a requester left out because a capture-free source serves the draw (bone / object history); the line ends with the moving rigid captures' VS window 2 kinds and `VS window 2 per requester: uploaded / object record / bone image`. The line format is the interface of later stages (F4-5 uses it): `<requester> x <kind>: <n> (<site> <n>, ...) avoided <n>`. Parser: `rvk-work/scratch/nocap/census.py <rvk.log> <shot dir>` (per rvkcompare spot window).

**Census, everything on** (Full, player defaults + every owned stage + `rt.table_entries` + table lighting + `full_lit` + `debug.ubershader` + `native.vertex_pull` + `native.bone_motion=on` + FSR performance + frame generation + GI + AO + RT shadows near + `upscale.object_motion=all`; requests per frame):

| scene | before (main's code) | after (`native.rigid_motion=true`) |
|---|---|---|
| jungle | **139.1**: motion rigid static 53.8 (composed), instanced mesh 30.1 (retained), rt tree 53.7, rt solid for motion skinned 1.05 / morph 0.50 | **48.2**: rt tree 48.2 (the only requester); avoided: rigid static 86, instanced 49, skinned 9, morph 6 |
| harbour_docks | **28.7**: rigid static 17.4, instanced 11.3 | **1.7**: rt tree only |
| hero_idle | **0.13**: rt cascade morph 0.04, rt solid without a skin job morph 0.08 | **0** |
| pub_idle | **5.54**: rigid static 5.42 (owned / retained), rt solid without a skin job morph 0.12 | **0** |
| walk (`rvk_rtwalk`, quick-load) | **51.8**: rigid static 20.1, instanced 16.2, rt tree 15.4, characters 0.2 | **14.9**: rt tree only |

(before = `RVK_NOCAP_OLD=1` for the walk, main's build for the spots: the same thing, see below.) What remains is the SpeedTrees' RT capture (`RtScene::observeTree`): the tree side, F4-5 (compute wind into the RT tree BLAS) is being built by another agent.

**What the census found, and what was done** (each behind `native.rigid_motion` or `native.bone_motion`, or a plain bug fix):
* **(d) Most rigid "moving" captures were false.** `ObjectMotion` hashed c31..c37 of the register file to tell a moving rigid draw. In Full the composed and owned draws' register file holds only the registers the draw's composition wrote (lean mode), the rest is another draw's: a still object looked "moving" whenever a neighbour in the list changed. Now the world rows c33..c35 only (`q.objectRows` for an owned draw). Jungle 84 captures per frame were false; the owned static share at jungle rose 66 -> 73 %, harbour 52 -> 59 %. `RVK_NOCAP_OLD=1` restores the old hash (and items c / pending below) for A/B.
* **(b) Rigid objects that really move: the object table's history** (`native.rigid_motion`, restart, needs `native.bone_motion` not off, acts while `upscale.object_motion=all`). `ObjectTable::setHistory` doubles the table (record `kCapacity + slot`, `kHistoryDelta` = 32,768 x 96 bytes); at the flush a slot whose c31..c35 changed this frame gets its last flush's record as history (valid = live, same generation, translation jump < 500 units: info.w = 1), a slot that changed before and not now gets its record marked invalid (info.w = 0: the camera's motion, as a record that never moved); `prevOf(base)` only for bases the table handed out (GPU safety); rvk.log `native: object history (5 s)`. The static meshes' main solid draws with an object-record window 2 take the bone-motion forms (`bonePredict`, `sceneDecide`): the translated program's second run reads window 2 = the history record; the validity word follows the shader's addressing (`RVK_BM_VALID`: a palette-indexing shader reads the bone image's c231.x, any other the object record's c36.w; a shader whose addressing does not match its window never takes the forms). **Instanced-mesh ops** (held equipment: one placement per draw, served from the object table, no scene form for the kind) take the bone-motion PUSH form (`VariantSlot::BoneVs` drawn directly, `pc.prevVs` = the history base, the motion pixel variant), and their packet-cache replays are refused while rigid motion is on (the push form has no retained packet; before, a moving one asked for a capture and was refused anyway). rvk.log `native: rigid motion (5 s)` (static solid draws predicted, no object window / no history / forms pending; instanced-mesh push form predicted / drawn).
* **(c) RT characters without a skinning job = the heads' morph targets.** The census found one layout only: stride 72 + POSITION1..3 F3 in streams 1..3 (+ NORMAL1..3 D3DCOLOR) with active weights (c31.xyz != 0). `rt_skin.comp.hlsl` adds the targets in the vertex shader's order (`p = mad(d_k, w_k, p)`, k = 1..3) before the bone blend: `ReplaceQuery::morphBuffer / morphOffset / morphStride` (streams 1..3), `RtScene::skinEligible` -> `SkinLayout`, a 3-uint4 morph block per job behind the job table (the job keeps 4 uint4: the block's address in word 3 .yz, the target mask in word 2 .y; `rvk_rhitest`'s own rt_skin case is unchanged and passes). A plain skinned layout ignores c31 (it holds other data there). rvk.log `rt: skin: ... (N draws with morph targets so far)`; `rt: scene: characters` shows `0 not eligible`, `solid-pass draws N with / 0 without a job`. `debug.rt_skin_verify` (the cascades' vertex shader capture vs the compute pass): max error 0.008 - 0.012 units over 250 - 360 k vertices per second, with the morph heads included (rt-shadows.md 10's figure without them: 0.006 - 0.012). `rvk_vktest --skin` has a morph job (targets 1 and 3, own strides / offsets, a zero weight): max error 3e-5.
* **Pending forms need no capture.** While a draw's bone-motion forms compile (`bonePredict`: forms pending), its observers are told it is covered (`Device::boneMotionCovered`): the draw keeps its plain form that frame (the camera's motion), no capture as a fallback.
* **(a) The ubershader's bone form.** With `debug.ubershader`, the bone-motion vertex forms of a request the port covers are the port (`RVK_UBER_BONE_PREV`, + `RVK_UBER_BONE_OBJ` for a rigid shader; `RVK_UBER_BONE_TR=1` keeps them translated): the module's translated part (`RVK_UBER_TRPOS`) keeps the clip position, bit for bit, and now also hands the port its clip position's world point (a one-line epilogue instead of the second translated run: `uberModule` builds the translated part without `rerunnable`); the port runs its own `Transform()` (skinning: `GetWorldMatrix` reads the bones through VS window 2, which `rvk_vsw2Ov` redirects) on this frame's image and on the history image in one loop body, and outputs `prevWorld = trWorld + (w_prev - w_cur)`: a part that did not move gives exactly 0 displacement. Skinned vertex shaders owned as uber: 100 % (`native: owned shaders`), translated builds after an ubershader failure 0 on Vulkan and D3D12.
* **Still vertices** (all bone / object forms): a vertex whose second run gives the same clip position (translated) or the same world position (port) keeps `prevWorld.w = 0`, so the motion pixel shader takes the camera's motion from its own reconstruction exactly as for geometry without a motion source (before, the clip-to-world rounding of the reconstruction, 14.13's 0.2 - 0.4 px, reached still parts too).

**Verification.**
* `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS, 0 validation errors; new case "rigid motion (14.15 b)": the history on the CPU twin (new record invalid, still = invalid, moved = last frame's record valid, a jump of 900 units invalid, `prevOf` guards) and, on Vulkan, the compare mode's capture vertex shader on a rigid shader (object record validity word + the object table's delta) against the capture chain: **0.000000 px / 0.000000 units** over 64 vertices, negative control (the capture of two frames ago) 0.257 units. (The case drains the previous case's compare read-backs first: they arrive kFramesInFlight + 2 frames late.) The bone motion case's skinned test shader now indexes its palette relatively, as the game's do.
* `rvk_rhitest --validation` PASS on both backends (incl. its rt_skin case, which uses the unchanged 4-uint4 job layout); `rvk_vktest --skin --validation` PASS; `rvk_tests` 331 / 0.
* `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded` 90 / 90, `uber` 3,441 / 3,441, `corpus corpus --scene2 --bone`, `--scene --bone`, `--bone-pull`, `--capture-bone` 149 / 149 each; `vs_equiv.py` 52 EQUAL (3 unsupported), `ubershader_check.py` 259 EQUAL (63 NO-HLSL, 4 unsupported): unchanged (the default text is unchanged).
* Compare mode (`native.bone_motion=compare` + rigid, hero_idle + pub_idle, FSR + RT on): max 0.34 - 0.39 px / 4.84 units, the second run on this frame's image vs the first 4.84 units: as 14.13 (the reconstruction's own rounding).
* GPU-assisted validation (`-Validation` + `GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED`, everything on + rigid, hero_idle / harbour_docks; the push form drawn 5,681 times per 5 s): no error but AMD FSR's known `rw_output` warnings.
* Picture check (`native.picture_check`, jungle / pub_interior / harbour_docks, no FSR / FG, freeze_wait 1800): every settled window `ok` (worst view 0.034 - 0.085 %), 0 `FAILED`; MISS / DIFFERING 0 in every run. Near-black (`blk.py`): jungle 0.0000 %, harbour 0.0000 %; pub_idle 0.069 - 0.071 % in both arms; hero_idle 0.25 vs 0.19 %: the extra pixels are on the hero's dark trousers (his pose differs between runs), not holes.
* D3D12 (`-Api d3d12 -Validation`, `RVK_NO_GBV=1`, everything on + rigid): the known DSV-format warnings only; bone forms (v2, pulled) and the push form drawn; the census 0 at hero_idle / pub_idle.
* Motion pictures (`gbuffer.debug=motion`, 8 frames): the port's bone form + rigid (mot_on) and the capture path (mot_old) agree in frame b0 (the capture path shows character motion only in b0, 14.13's open 2); the bone path carries it on every frame; motion stays inside the silhouettes.
* **RT characters, the last two cases** (found by the walk's census after (c)): a cascade's character draw while the skinning pipeline is still being built (the first second of a session: 5 requests in a 3.5 min walk) and a solid-pass part without a job of this frame (its first frame: 1) took a capture. In Full with `native.bone_motion=on` neither does now (`avoided` in the census): the character enters the TLAS with the first skinning job / one frame later.
* **Motion pictures, port vs translated bone forms** (`gbuffer.debug=motion`, hero_idle, 4 frames, frame generation off; share of moving pixels / mean deviation): port bone form + rigid 0.66 - 0.70 % / 4.6 - 4.7; everything translated (`debug.ubershader=false`) 0.54 - 0.70 % / 4.5 - 4.6; translated bone forms with the port's z pass (`RVK_UBER_BONE_TR=1`) 1.2 - 1.5 % / 5.6 - 5.8 (with or without pulling; 1.2 - 2.6 % without rigid). The port's form agrees with the all-translated reference; the mixed arm carries the clip-to-world rounding of the translated reconstruction on near-still parts. (Before the still-vertex rule the mixed arm showed whole-body motion on the idle hero, 6 % of the pixels, deviation 8 - 14, with frame generation on.)
* **FSR pictures** (8 frames, shot-reviewer + mean temporal luma change): no ghosting / trails on characters or held items in either arm. The temporal change on the hero is 0.56 (new) vs 0.42 (the capture path, which carries the characters' motion in burst frame 0 only) and 0.62 with `native.rigid_motion` off: the rigid forms do not add instability; the difference against the capture path is the hero's motion now being present in every frame. pub_idle 0.16 vs 0.09 (895 vs 191 changing pixels, edges of static beams / barrels): a single run per arm, the rigid-off and repeat arms of that spot did not finish (the frame generation hang below); not separated from run-to-run noise.
* **Frame generation hang (not this stage).** About half the runs with `framegen.mode=fsr3` stopped presenting after 1 - 4 minutes: the present thread waits inside AMD's frame generation present for a GPU fence (`FgSwapchain::present` -> `amd_fidelityfx_framegeneration_vk` -> amdvlk WaitForSingleObject), the render thread in `Presenter::waitDone` (`FrameGeneration::recordSwapchain` -> `frameGenPresentsQueued`); no device loss is reported. It happens with the old capture path too (`native.bone_motion=off` + `RVK_NOCAP_OLD=1`: 2 of 2 runs hung) and never with frame generation off (0 of 4); four games (two with frame generation) shared the GPU at the time. `-Environment 'RVK_HANG_WATCHDOG=1'` logs the stacks without `-Perf`. Left to the frame generation stage.
* Walk (`rvk_rtwalk`, quick-load, 3.5 min) after: **rt tree 15.8 per frame, every other requester 0** (rigid static avoided 29.9, instanced mesh 36.0, skinned 15.1, morph 5.9 per frame); the final build with frame generation off: rt tree 14.9 per frame, every other requester 0 over 15,874 frames.

**Owned share** (main view's static ops, per spot window; final build, frame generation off): before -> after jungle 66 -> 79 %, harbour_docks 52 -> 71 %, hero_idle 69 -> 70 %, pub_idle 71 -> 70 %; the actors' main z / solid ops 100 % owned, the cascades 99.9 - 100 %; MISS / DIFFERING 0 in every run. The false rigid captures had forced their draws composed; the instanced-mesh ops (held equipment) are now composed every frame by design (push form, no retained packet).

**The trees (F4-5, another agent).** The only requester left is `rt tree x speedtree` (`RtScene::observeTree`: the main view's solid-pass SpeedTree draws within the RT animation range, refit from the previous frame's capture). Notes handed over: the capture variant's text IS the vertex shader, so a compute kernel built from the translator's unchanged `rvk_main()` (inputs from S-4's `rvkPullAttr`, windows from the owned tree records, the instance index = TEXCOORD4) reproduces the wind deformation bit for bit and can be checked against a kept capture on check frames with a 0-bit tolerance; owned trees can then act with the RT scene on.

**Open.** (1) Trees: F4-5. (2) The pub_idle FSR edge-shimmer comparison needs a clean A/B (same build, rigid off vs on, repeated) once frame generation runs reliably. (3) The translated bone forms with the port's z pass (`RVK_UBER_BONE_TR=1`) show more motion on near-still parts than either pure arm: harmless while the port form is the default under `debug.ubershader`, but the translated reconstruction (14.13 open 1) stays as it was. (4) No `rvk_d3d9test` case draws the bone-motion PUSH form (it needs the G-buffer motion target, which the harness lacks); it ran under GPU-assisted validation in the game (5,681 draws per 5 s) and on D3D12.

### 14.16 S-7c result: the rest of the post chain on our own pixel shaders; the ToneMap fold measured and left opt-in (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a86048c1bfb2499e6`; no new setting: `native.own_post` = off | on | compare as before, Debug, Full, default off)

**Built: every post unit the game was seen to draw now has its own shader in Full + `native.own_post=on`.** The units still on translated shaders after 14.8b (ToneMap, DoFDown, DoF on the screen, the HDRI-only graph's HDRI screen pass, the overlay effects) are drawn with our own HLSL **pixel shaders** (`shaders/post/post_*.frag.hlsl`, shared code in `post_units.hlsli` / `post_dof.hlsli` / `post_overlay.hlsli`). These are ports of the `rvk_fxtool dump --listing` disassembly of post_hdr.fx / post_dof.fx / overlay_color.fx, written instruction for instruction (`_pp` is full precision, as in the translator). No compute kernel was needed: each unit keeps its composed draw (`native::EffectOps`), so its target, which may be the back buffer, stays a render target. The unit also keeps its quad, the engine's translated vertex shader (an `add o1, c0, v1` per tap), its blend / clip states, its constants and its textures. Only the pixel shader changes. `OwnPost::unitDraw` (`src/native/own_post_units.cpp`) builds a `d3d9::Shader` that holds our module and **a copy of the engine pixel shader's reflection**, so the device uploads the same register windows and binds the same stages. `issue()` / `issueLean()` draw the patched copy. In the lean path the composed state is restored right after the draw, so the O3 "after" prediction is unchanged. The own-draw compare checks the patched draw (0 differing).

Units covered: **ToneMap, HDRI, DoFDown, DoF, TOverlayColor, OverlayRadialColor, OverlayRadialBlur (OverlayHit's hit effect), OverlayImage, OverlayMovieStrips / OverlayMovieStripsAbs**. A port is used only when the engine pixel shader's sampler set and outputs match its listing, and only once its module is built (in the background when the mode is switched on; until then the unit keeps the engine's shader). Together with S-7a / S-7b (luminance 8 units, bloom 4, DoF middle 4), all 22 units of the DoF graph + overlay run our shaders, and so do all units of the HDRI graph. rvk.log `native: own post units (5 s): <unit> N ours / M engine (why)`: the engine count was 0 in every run below.

**Fusion (ToneMap folded into DoFDown + DoF): built, measured, opt-in (`RVK_OWNPOST_FUSE=1`).** `post_dofdown_tm` / `post_dof_tm` tone-map the HDR scene themselves:
* The fused DoFDown reads 16 point texels per output pixel: each bilinear tap of the 8-bit ToneMap image at a texel corner becomes the mean of its 4 tone-mapped texels.
* The fused DoF reads the centre texel plus 4 corner means.
* The ToneMap draw's inputs go into the free stages s5 / s6 and registers c16..c20. The ToneMap unit is then held and not drawn.
* Fallbacks: a consumer that cannot fold it gets it drawn late with our port (`drawn late`). Any other unit that samples the held image also gets it drawn first. An unread ToneMap is dropped at the post chain's end.
* Self-checks: ToneMap must be 1:1 on its target with point / clamp sampling, and the consumers' VS offsets must lie on the ToneMap grid.

In compare mode the fold is max 1 step against the engine (no pixel more than 1 step off). **On the RX 7900 XTX it is slower** (GPU table below), so it is off by default. Debug env (A/B aids): `RVK_OWNPOST_UNITS=0` (every single unit stays the engine's), `RVK_OWNPOST_GROUPS=<mask>` (1 luminance, 2 bloom, 4 DoF middle; default 7).

**Compare mode extended.** In compare mode each single unit is drawn a second time after the engine's draw: with our shader, into a scratch render target of the same size and format. Blended or clipped units (overlays, the strips) start from a copy of the target. The scratch is read back against the engine's target (`native: own post (5 s): image '<unit>'`: max step, mean relative difference, share of pixels more than 1 step off). The readback slot is now 48 MiB. Full-resolution images are compared every 4th frame (CPU cost). X8R8G8B8 targets are compared on RGB only. Compare also works on D3D12.

**Numbers (own_post=compare, 1600x900):**

| unit | Vulkan (harbour_docks; pub_interior + jungle with `RVK_DOF_TEST=1`) | D3D12 (harbour_docks) |
|---|---|---|
| tonemap | max 1 step, mean rel 1e-8 (float rounding ties), 0 % > 1 step | **0 steps** |
| dofdown | **0 steps** | **0 steps** |
| dof (screen) | max 1 step, mean rel 6e-9 | **0 steps** |
| hdri (`DefaultEffect="HDRI"`, harbour_docks) | max 1 step, mean rel 5e-8 | - |
| overlay strips | **0 steps** | **0 steps** |
| overlay radial blur (`rvk_rare -Param steps=hit`) | **0 steps** (12 frames compared) | - |
| dofdown (fused) / dof (fused) | max 1 step, mean rel 0.0015 - 0.0023 / 0.0024 - 0.0046, 0 % > 1 step | - |

TOverlayColor / OverlayRadialColor / OverlayImage were not met in any run (OverlayBlackScreen / WhiteScreen etc., triggers in engine-frame/README.md 16). They are ported and pass DXIL, but are not verified in the game.

**Pictures** (`water-compare -Mode full`, off vs on, fused build; control = off vs off against the same left run; `tools/sbs_diff.py` whole-image luma change / mean |d|):

| spot | on vs off | control |
|---|---|---|
| beach_sun | +1.0 % / 2.87 | +0.5 % / 2.92 |
| harbour_docks | -1.5 % / 0.82 | +0.1 % / 0.29 |
| jungle | -0.7 % / 2.38 | 0.0 % / 2.32 |
| monastery_hall | -1.7 % / 1.21 | -1.2 % / 1.18 |
| pub_interior | -1.6 % / 0.90 | -0.5 % / 0.67 |
| sea_horizon | -0.2 % / 0.51 | -0.1 % / 0.39 |
| sun_disc | 0.0 % / 0.22 | 0.0 % / 0.04 |

The harbour_docks shift (about 0.6 of a level, uniform) **does not come from S-7c**:
* `RVK_OWNPOST_UNITS=0` (only the S-7a / S-7b kernels) gives the same -1.5 % / 0.82. The unfused build gives -1.5 % too.
* Single-group runs scatter between -2.3 % and 0.0 % at harbour_docks and between +1.7 % and +3.6 % at pub_interior. Separate sessions therefore differ more than the one control pair suggests (eye adaptation / particles at these spots).

The same-frame compare numbers above are the proof that the ports are EQUAL. Still open: whether S-7a / S-7b drift at harbour_docks. A picture A/B inside one session would settle it.

`shot-reviewer` (crops under `rvk-work/scratch/s7c/review`):
* Unchanged within the control: sun disc, flare, haze and god rays (the sun_disc / beach_sun crops), hue everywhere, the DoF blur of far backgrounds, the letterbox bars.
* No 1-pixel shift (best alignment (0, 0) at every spot), no seams.
* A smooth -1 % around pub_interior's candle glow, not visible side by side.

**GPU per pass** (perf-ab `native.own_post` off / on, 3 + 3 windows of 240 frames in one session per scene, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off; run in the private copy because the shared copy's game exits at start):

| pass / metric | fused, hold | fused, harbour_docks | unfused (default), hold | unfused (default), harbour_docks |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 400x225 pass (DoFDown + DoF middle) | **+0.031 ms** (z 9.6) | **+0.020 ms** (z 10.3) | unchanged | unchanged |
| 1600x900 post pass | +0.031 ms (ToneMap gone, DoF fused) | +0.015 ms | **-0.011 ms** (z -2.9) | **-0.027 ms** (z -2.7) |
| GPU frame | +0.011 ms | +0.018 ms | -0.019 ms (z -2.3) | -0.058 ms (z -1.5) |

* The unfused arms include S-7a / S-7b's kernels. The share of our pixel shaders alone cannot be resolved: they do the engine's ALU work.
* CPU: in the noise.
* Plainly: **the fusion brings nothing on the XTX**. It is slower, because 16 FP16 texels + 16 tone maps per DoFDown pixel cost more than the ToneMap pass they save. On a bandwidth-bound iGPU its 17 MB less traffic might weigh more [I, unmeasured on the 680M], hence opt-in rather than removed.

**Gates.**
* `rvk_tests` 330 / 0. `dxil-check embedded`: all 11 new pixel shaders PASS.
* `rvk_rhitest --validation`: PASS (both backends, abi probe 55 / 55). No new case: the ports only run through the D3D9 draw path, and the compare mode proves them against the translated shaders in the game on both backends.
* `rvk_d3d9test --validation`: Vulkan PASS (0 failures / 0 validation errors), D3D12 PASS. The Vulkan run then exits with 0xC0000005 after one leaked VkBuffer at `vkDestroyDevice`. **Pre-existing**: main's build does the same.
* Game runs (private copy `risen-test-vk-s7c`, instance 62, lock `rvk-s7c`):
  * Compare + `-Validation` + GPU-assisted: 0 messages.
  * On + `native.scene_ops_compare` + `native.lean_check=10` + GPU-assisted, fused build: 0 messages. Effect draw compares 0 mismatches; own draws checked 6,284, differing 0; lean check after / store / fields 0 differing. The fold ran every frame: 6,284 ToneMaps folded, 0 drawn late, 0 outside draws while held.
  * D3D12 compare and On, `-Validation` (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`): only the baseline DSV-format warning.
  * FSR quality + frame generation + `-SyncValidation`: only AMD's own `rw_*` format warnings. The units are ours under FSR.

**Left on engine shaders: nothing the game was seen to draw.**
* Not ported, because never met: post_radialBlur (RadialBlur graph), post_wobble, post_edgesmooth.
* Not shaders: Indicator and OverlayHit's CopyCurrentRTToImage are copy filters; they stay the engine's HAL copy.
* Unchanged: the GUI sprite batches (effect_ops_gui) and the units' vertex shaders (translated, one add per tap).

(Superseded by 14.18: the units' vertex shaders, RadialBlur / Wobble / EdgeSmooth and every other effect draw of the native frame are on our shaders since S-7d.)

### 14.17 F4-5 result: the RT scene's animated SpeedTrees by compute, no vertex capture; trees owned with ray tracing on (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-ad6c249fd4eb8b4fe` on main a01cfb5; one Debug setting `native.tree_wind` = off | on | compare, Full, run time, default off; settings count 150)

**Problem (15.1 item 3).** With any consumer of swaying trees (`shadows.rt` with its wind, GI foliage: `RtScene::setAnimated`) the RT scene took the animated SpeedTrees from a vertex-shader capture of the composed main-view solid draw (`observeTree`, `cap4`); an owned run has no draw to capture, so `RtScene::ownedTreesOk()` was false, `Device::ownedTreeObserversOk` refused and the trees (main view + cascades) stayed composed: 0 % owned.

**What was built.** (1) `shaders/rt/rt_tree_wind.comp.hlsl`: the position part of the SpeedTree vertex shader (`ge_default.vert.hlsl` Transform under `XFX_SPEEDTREE`: the wind-matrix blend `lerp(p, p * Wind[BLENDINDICES0.x], BLENDWEIGHT0.x)`, the leaf-mesh offset `MeshMatrix(NORMAL1, NORMAL2) * RotX(rock) * BLENDWEIGHT2.xyz`, the leaf-card corner through RotZ / RotY (rock / rustle = c32.xy x leaf angles c26..c29) and the leaf angle matrix c23..c25, then the instance's world rows) per tree instance over the draw's own vertex buffer (by address; attributes decoded by D3DDECLTYPE as the input assembler does), into a buffer of the capture variant's layout (16-byte header, world position w = 1 + TEXCOORD0 per vertex slot). One job = one instance: vertex slot 0's address, 20 float4 of constants (c14..c29 + the instance's c32..c35, copied at the observation), the attribute words, the class (`enh/tree_wind.h`: `layoutOf` = op 0 / 1 wind only, op > 1 leaf mesh when NORMAL1 / 2 are declared else leaf cards; the CPU reference `treewind::world`). (2) `RtScene::observeTree`: while `native.tree_wind` is on and the pipeline is built, an observed instance (a composed draw or an owned run shown through `Device::ownedTreeObserve`) gets a job and its `DynDraw` points at the job's output; no capture is requested. The jobs of a frame run in ONE dispatch at AfterOpaque (pass `rt.tree_wind`, order -110: the frame the capture would have run in, before a scene built at AfterOpaque), the outputs are marked captured (`markVertexCaptured`), so the next frame's refit (`buildDynamic`, previous frame's captures) and the material vertex reads take them unchanged: **same timing and same frame's wind as the capture**. The wind registers come from the draw's VS view window when an owned window serves it (`Device::ownedWindows()`: window 1 = c12..c30 from the view table), else the register file (checked: they never differed). Composed tree draws are packet-cache replays once they ask for no capture: their instance stream cannot be read back there, nor in an owned run: the identity ramp is assumed for SpeedTree batches (every tree stream is one; `tree batches with the identity instance stream assumed`, `readable but not 0, 1, 2, ...` stayed 0). (3) `ownedTreesOk()` = `!animated_ || treeWindActive()`. (4) `compare`: composed draws also take the capture, with header bit 31 (the operand the shader multiplies with its view-projection = its exact world position), and the compute pass compares slot by slot (max error per class, counts over 0.1 / 1 unit). rvk.log `rt: tree wind (5 s)`, `rt: tree wind: declaration [...] -> class` once per declaration, `native: stages (5 s) ... tree_wind=active(N tree instances by compute, M of them from owned runs)`; `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1` knows `native.tree_wind`.

**Exactness (compare mode, Vulkan).** Against the vertex shader's exact world operand: **max error 0.0011 units** at the jungle spot (9.6 M vertices compared per 5 s: branches / fronds 0.0010, leaf cards 0.0011; leaf meshes none in range), **0.0024 units** on the savegame walk (1.9 M per 5 s), 0 over 0.1, 0 over 1 = float noise. Found on the way: the capture's own main-view path (clip position x inverse view-projection) is off by up to **0.70 units** against that operand at the jungle (64 % of the vertices over 0.1): the compute is more exact than the capture it replaces. Declarations seen (one per class): stride 60 (op 0 / 1, BLENDINDICES UBYTE4, BLENDWEIGHT0 FLOAT1), 88 (leaf cards), 96 (leaf mesh); 0 refused.

**Owned share, captures, RT scene** (everything on as 15.1 without frame generation, `upscale.mode=performance`, `shadows.rt=near`; savegame `Risen_Save_0005` quick-load walk 330 s, lod 2x):

| run | main views owned | cascade views owned | tree ops composed late | tree captures (run) | compute jobs (owned runs) | animated trees in the TLAS (mean / max) | volatile ranges | TLAS instances (mean) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| walk, `tree_wind=off` | 0 of 4,524 (0 %) | 0 of 6,786 | - | 116,363 | 0 | 15.1 / 26 | 11 | 1,334 |
| walk, `on` (GPU-assisted validation) | 6,577 of 8,238 (**80 %**) | 9,873 of 12,360 (80 %) | 33 of 1.01 M | **19** (before the pipeline was built) | 211,640 (168,739) | 25.3 / 27 | 11 | 1,337 |

80 % = every owned frame (1 check + 1 warm-up frame of 10 compose by design, `native.owned_check=10`); the jungle spot gives 79 - 80 % too. The first build missed the composed frames' replays (no stream read-back: no job, so the next frame's TLAS lost the animated trees after every composed frame: mean 17.5 animated trees); fixed by the identity rule above. Owned checks: DIFFERING 0 / MISS 0 in every run; 0 `[error]` lines.

**Pictures.** `native.picture_check` (jungle, pub_interior, harbour_docks, no FSR, `tree_wind=on`): 11 settled windows `ok` (worst view 0.076 % against the control's 0.056 %, depth 0 differing pixels), **FAILED 0** (17 UNRELIABLE = arrivals, 1 NO DATA). Black pixels (`blk.py`): 1 - 8 pixels (0.0001 - 0.0007 %) in every burst frame of both arms. Wind bursts (`rvk_rtwalk mode=sun rate=0 hour=14`, 10 frames 3 apart, off vs on, separate runs; shot-reviewer): after the first frame's eye-adaptation step both bursts move smoothly (consecutive-frame mean difference 0.8 - 1.4 off, 0.8 - 1.3 on, no single-frame spike, growing steadily against frame 0), same canopy / ground shadow character, no holes or streaks; one pair of runs at the savegame differed in lighting (one run flat-lit: run-to-run weather), not judged. The `shadows.debug=rt_term` bursts came out as the lit scene (the burst capture does not show that debug view), so the RT term was not judged alone.

**Backends.** D3D12 jungle (`tree_wind=on`, `RVK_NO_GBV=1` debug layer run in compare mode earlier: only the 25 old PSO DSV-format warnings): 32 - 35 k tree instances per 5 s from owned runs, 80 % owned. **Found: on D3D12 the tree capture writes nothing** (compare mode: 0 vertices compared, every slot "not written"; the capture's slot / instance arithmetic assumes Vulkan's VertexIndex / InstanceIndex, see the SV_VertexID gotcha): animated trees on D3D12 were never right before; the compute path is the first that gives them their wind there. Open: in compare mode on D3D12 the owned runs produced no jobs (not chased: `on` works; there the new counters `owned-run instances without a job` show only "beyond the range").

**GPU / CPU.** Pass `rt.tree_wind` 0.003 - 0.005 ms GPU at the jungle (XTX, 240-frame perf windows). The CPU A/B (`scripts/perf-ab.ps1 -Key native.tree_wind -A off -B on`, hold + jungle, 3 pairs, stage states matched every window) was **unusable: four other agents' games ran (engine + HAL 22 - 43 ms per frame against ~8 normally)**; not repeated (no number claimed). Expected from 15.2: the trees' composition share (~2.5 ms at the savegame) minus the owned path (7.25b: ~0.5 ms per owned view).

**GPU safety.** CPU guards: null vertex / output / constants allocations refuse the job (the capture or the rest pose instead), slots < 2^20, job count <= 1,024 per dispatch (the rest counted `dropped`), the attribute words 4-byte aligned, register reads bounded (c32 + 4 i + 3 < c232). `rvk_rhitest` "SpeedTree wind compute (rt_tree_wind, F4-5)": the production shader on three jobs of the three classes (declarations through `layoutOf`, UBYTE4 / FLOAT16_2 inputs, buffers at non-zero offsets) against `treewind::world`, plus the compare accumulator (unwritten / exact / moved slots: counts and max error): PASS on Vulkan and D3D12 `--validation`; the ABI probe reads `TreeWindPush` (56 blocks). First game runs Vulkan jungle with `-Validation` + GPU-assisted validation (owned runs included) and the GPU-assisted walk: 0 errors (only AMD FSR's known format-mismatch warnings).

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 333 / 0 (new `tree_wind_layout_classes_and_refusals`, `tree_wind_reference_matches_the_shader_math`); `rvk_rhitest --validation` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS; `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS (0 failures, 0 validation errors); `dxil-check embedded` 102 / 102, `dxil-check uber` (census jungle + town) 3,441 / 3,441.

**Open.** (1) The CPU / fps gain needs a quiet machine (perf-ab command above). (2) D3D12: compare mode's owned-run gap; the tree capture itself is broken there (moot once `tree_wind` is the default). (3) `rt.debug=depth` probes on trees were not rerun (the TLAS tree set: the sightings hash equal in composed and owned frames, animated-tree counts as with captures).


### 14.18 S-7d result: the shadow mask chain, the SSAO chain, the PSSM pass, the terrain blur, the rare image effects and every unit's vertex shader on our own shaders; S-7c's open items closed (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-ac5123ec361d80d15`; no new setting: `native.own_post` = off | on | compare as before, its scope extended; settings count unchanged)

**What was left after S-7c** (15.1 census): the shadow mask units (UpscaleSSAO, one SSM / DSSM quad per cascade, VSSM), the SSAO chain (SSAO, BlurSSAO x 2, Blur), the PSSM point-light pass, the terrain map's Blur7H / V, every post unit's vertex shader (translated) and the effects never met in a run (RadialBlur, Wobble, EdgeSmooth; TOverlayColor / OverlayRadialColor / OverlayImage unverified). Every one of them runs our own shaders now in Full + `native.own_post=on`.

**Built.**
* **The effect ports** (`shaders/post/fx/*.{vert,frag}.hlsl`, 67 files: 30 vertex + 37 pixel shaders): every vertex and pixel shader of the effects the native frame composes, de-duplicated by bytecode: post_SSM.fx (DSSM, VSSM, SSM, SSM_Low, PSSM p0 / p1 / p2, PSSM_Low p2, SSAO, SSAO_Low, BlurSSAO, UpscaleSSAO, Blur), the terrain blur fx (Blur7H / V / Blur7: one pixel shader, two vertex shaders), post_hdr / post_dof / post_bloom / post_streaks (all techniques: the S-7a / b kernels' fallback draws and the unused ones), overlay_color (vertex shaders; the pixel shaders are S-7c's hand ports), post_radialBlur (4 techniques), post_wobble, post_edgesmooth (2). Every captured variant of a family has the same bytecode (10 post_SSM, 22 post_hdr, 12 post_dof, 8 overlay, 4 wobble, 4 bloom, 2 each of the others: checked), so one port per shader serves them all.
* **How they are written**: `tools/post_port.py` (the generator; new `rvk_fxtool extract <fx> <dir>` writes an effect's shaders as bytecode, `rvk_shadertool export` translates them) restructures the translator's instruction-for-instruction HLSL into one entry function with local registers and the shared D3D9 arithmetic helpers of `post_port.hlsli` (rcp / rsq clamped to FLT_MAX, nrm(0) = 0, pow(x, 0) = 1, the draw block's alpha test / texkill). The D3D9 listing itself is not copied (game-derived); each file names its effect, the techniques that use it and the engine's constant names. They are generated, not hand-edited: hand transliteration was tried first and is error-prone (post_SSM's SSAO_Low reflects a kernel vector through one swizzle in the dot product and another in the multiply-add; only the translator's semantics get such lines right). The hand ports of S-7c stay as they are.
* **Dispatch**: table `src/native/post_port_table.inl` (FNV-1a 64 of the engine shader's tokens -> port), `OwnPost::fxPort` (`src/native/own_post_fx.cpp`: the modules built by 4 workers when the mode is switched on, ~67 DXC compiles from the disk cache; our `d3d9::Shader` carries the engine shader's reflection, semantics and sampler mask, every translated variant "not applicable"). `EffectOps::drawComposed` (`src/native/effect_ops_fx.cpp`) is now the one place every composed effect draw goes through (issue, issueLean, issueOne, the group flush, the PSSM pass): On = drawn with the ports of its vertex and pixel shader, the composed state's shaders set back after the draw (the O3 "after" prediction unchanged; the own-draw compare of check frames compares against the patched composition). The GUI sprite batcher is not touched (F13). Units: shadow mask, SSAO chain, PSSM, terrain blur, post (all kinds); rvk.log `native: own fx shaders (5 s): <kind> N draws: vs ours / engine, ps ours / engine (first engine reason)`; the stage line has `own_post=active(on: N effect draws / kernel runs on our shaders)`.
* **The enhanced shadow mask** (`shadows.enabled`) keeps working: its replacer recognises the cascade / variance draws by the pixel shader's semantics, which the ports carry; it replaces those draws, everything else of the chain (UpscaleSSAO, SSAO chain, PSSM, terrain blur) runs the ports (run `on_av`: `enh: shadows: mask pass with 3 cascades`, 0 engine shaders).
* **Compare mode extended** (`native.own_post=compare`): every composed unit with a ported shader is drawn a second time with the ports into a scratch copy of its target BEFORE the engine's draws, so the stencil and depth they test are the ones the engine's draw sees: single draws test the stencil without writing it (write mask 0, no depth writes); the PSSM sequence (stencil INCR / DECR per cell, the colour pass zeroes what it drew) runs with its stencil writes only when VSSM ran before it (the whole mask's stencil is then 0, and the sequence leaves it 0) and no pass writes depth. After the unit the images are compared (`native: own post (5 s):   image '<port>.frag'`); the read-back now also reads float16 / float32 images (max rel per value; the terrain map's G32R32F, the R32F levels), full-resolution compares rotate (one in eight per frame, the 48 MiB read-back slot), the scratch cache holds 24 sizes / formats (8 thrashed: new targets every frame). The S-7c units' compare uses the vertex shader port too.
* **Rare effects in a test run**: overlay debug commands `post effect <name>` (`eCImageFilterAdmin::ActivateEffect`, the scene effect until replaced), `post default` (ActivateDefaultEffect), `post overlay <name> [duration [fade-in [fade-out]]]` (StartOverlayEffect; duration <= 0 = until stopped), `post overlay stop [fade]`; `src/engine/image_effects.*`, the admin getter 0x95750 checked at its call site as engine/exposure does (RE: admin +0x90 scene effect, +0x98 overlay, +0xa0 fade-in / +0xa4 fade-out / +0xa8 duration / +0xac elapsed / +0xb0 blend; Process advances the overlay, nothing switches the scene effect back by itself). `mods/rvk_rare` step `imagefx` freezes the game time `fxhold` seconds so the commands anchor to it (`freeze+N: post effect RadialBlur`, ...; recipe in the step's comment). `shot <name>` = a screenshot at a command (in-session A/B pictures).

**Numbers.**
* Offline, GPU (`python tools/post_port.py --corpus <corpus> --work <scratch> --check`: rvk_shadereq, 64 trials of random constants / varyings / 8x8 textures per pixel shader, the vertex shaders through `--vs-test` with the clip position and every varying): **77 of 77 EQUAL** (the 67 generated ports and the 10 S-7c hand ports, among them TOverlayColor / OverlayRadialColor / OverlayImage), worst relative difference 0 except two PSSM vertex shaders at 1.6e-7 (fma contraction of the position).
* In the game, compare mode, Vulkan (`cmp2`: pub_interior, harbour_docks, jungle, beach_sun, `-Validation`; `pssm_cmp`: rvk_pointshadow; `fx_cmp` / `ov_cmp` / `hit_cmp`: rvk_rare imagefx with the commands; all PASS):

| unit (port) | compared images | max steps | note |
|---|---|---|---|
| UpscaleSSAO (ssm_upscalessao) | 651 | **0** | 1600x900 |
| SSM x 3 per frame (ssm_ssm) | 1,962 | **0** | stencil-tested quads, alpha only |
| VSSM (ssm_vssm) | 655 | **0** | |
| SSAO (ssm_ssao) | 5,239 | **0** | 800x450 |
| BlurSSAO x 2 (ssm_blurssao) | 10,478 | **0** | |
| Blur (ssm_blur) | 653 | **0** | 1600x900 |
| PSSM colour pass (ssm_pssm_p2, + p0 / p1 null shaders) | 60 | **0** | rvk_pointshadow, 3 passes x cells |
| terrain blur Blur7H / V (blur7_blur7h) | 18 | **0** | only when the terrain map is re-rendered |
| RadialBlur (radial_radialblur) | 63 | **0** | `post effect RadialBlur` |
| Wobble (wobble_twobble) | 62 | **0** | |
| EdgeSmooth (edge_edgesmooth) | 34 | **0** | |
| TOverlayColor (OverlayBlackScreen / WhiteScreen) | 72 | **0** | S-7c hand port |
| OverlayRadialBlur (OverlayHit) | 88 | **0** | S-7c hand port |
| ToneMap / DoF with the vertex shader ports | 655 / 654 | 1 / 1 | as S-7c (float ties) |
| the luminance / bloom / DoF-middle units' draws (fallback form) | 5,239 each | **0** | hdr_*, bloom_*, dof_*, streak_streak |

  D3D12 (`dx_cmp`, `dx_pssm`): every port 0 steps except **SSAO: max 17 / 22 steps in a single texel now and then** (max rel 4.7e-8 / 6.1e-8, < 0.0001 % of the pixels; dx_cmp / dx_pssm): a point-sampled depth tap whose computed uv lies on a texel edge lands on the other texel, because DXC schedules the port's and the translated text's identical arithmetic differently for DXIL (on Vulkan both give the same SPIR-V result: 0 steps); the same class as S-7b's bright-pass texel flips. ToneMap / DoF with the vertex shader ports 0 steps; PSSM colour pass 0 (28 compares); the terrain blur's first two compares after the start differed in every pixel: on D3D12 a draw whose PSO is still building is skipped, so the scratch kept its copy (compares now start 120 presents after an image's first one: `cmpFirst_`; the later ones were 0). Validation: only the baseline DSV-format warning.
* On + `native.scene_ops_compare` + `native.lean_check=10` + GPU-assisted validation (`on_av`, player defaults incl. the enhanced mask): 0 validation messages, own draws checked 2,633+, differing 0; lean check after 0 / 7,795, store 0 / 7,795, fields 0 / 16,068 differing; `native: own fx shaders`: shadow mask, SSAO chain, terrain blur, post: vs / ps engine **0**. D3D12 On (`dx_on`, `-Validation`, `RVK_NO_GBV=1`): 0 messages beyond the baseline DSV-format warning; own draws differing 0; lean check after 0 / 9,056, store 0 / 9,056, fields 0 / 18,630; `native: own fx shaders`: engine 0 in every kind.
* First run with the new code (`cmp_av`, compare, `-Validation` + GPU-assisted): 0 messages.

**S-7c open item (a), harbour_docks -1.5 %: settled, not S-7a / b, not S-7c.** One session, frozen spot, `native.own_post` toggled at run time (`set native.own_post on|off`), 3 cycles x 4 shots per state (`shot`, 40 presents apart, 500 presents after each toggle): mean picture on vs off **+0.003 %** luma (12 vs 12 shots), mean |d| 0.300; control (the off shots' halves) -0.125 %, mean |d| 0.344. The same with the bloom kernels left out (`RVK_OWNPOST_GROUPS=5`): -0.024 % vs control -0.119 %. A first session with single shots: on vs off -0.16 .. -0.22 %, off vs off -0.15 .. +0.09 %. The 1.5 % of 14.16 was the difference between two sessions (eye adaptation state at the freeze, splash particles), as the single-group scatter there suggested. Per-kernel compare numbers at harbour_docks are the table above (`cmp2`): luminance rel 4e-5 (S-7a), bloom max 22 steps in < 0.02 % of the pixels mean, DoF middle max 2 / 3 steps (S-7b's known rounding ties), all within 14.8 / 14.8b.

**Pictures.** `shadow-compare.ps1 -Mode full` (shadow_inland_a / _b / _top, beach_sun; private copy): (a) the engine's mask chain off vs on: luma +0.30 / -1.18 / -1.30 / -0.68 %, mean |d| 2.80 / 5.49 / 3.01 / 2.92; control (off vs off, two sessions) +1.19 / +7.38 / -1.08 / -0.38 %, 2.80 / 10.27 / 3.27 / 2.73; (b) the enhanced mask on, the rest off vs on: +1.44 / -2.51 / -0.10 / -0.43 %, 3.89 / 10.40 / 2.30 / 4.95. `shot-reviewer` (x8 difference maps, 3 x 4 grids, crops of ground shadows, contact shadows, foliage, beach; `rvk-work/scratch/s7d/review`): no difference the control does not also show: no seam, band, stipple, half-pixel shift, SSAO change or missing / extra shadow; the excess at shadow_inland_top (b) is palm fronds at another sway phase. (`shadow-compare.ps1` declared no `-Api` and failed every run at the ValidateSet: fixed.)

**Fusion.** **Not built; it does not pay on the RX 7900 XTX, plainly.** perf-ab `native.own_post` off / on (3 + 3 windows of 240 frames per scene, one session each, private copy, `shadows.enabled=false` so the engine's mask chain runs): harbour_docks GPU frame +0.007 ms (z +0.7), jungle -0.17 ms (z -0.8, noise: sd 0.24); every pass within its noise (the 1600x900 mask pass with its 9 draws 0.145 / 0.142 ms, the 800x450 SSAO pass (3 draws) 0.038 / 0.039 ms, the 1024x1024 terrain map 0.020 / 0.020). In a run-time toggled session the S-7a / b kernels are refused (their persistent images were created before the setting: `no storage usage`), so these arms measured exactly the ports against the translated shaders: **the ports cost the same**. What a fusion could save: the whole engine mask chain is ~0.18 ms on the XTX at 1600x900 (mask pass 0.145 + SSAO 0.038); merging the 3 SSM quads + VSSM into one pass that picks the cascade per pixel (the stencil selection done in the shader) and the SSAO chain into one compute dispatch removes 4 - 6 pass / draw boundaries, ~3 - 6 us each on the XTX (14.8) = **0.01 - 0.03 ms**, inside the noise above; on the 680M the pass-count model of 14.8 gives ~0.03 - 0.10 ms [I, unmeasured]. And the enhanced mask (`shadows.enabled`, the player default) already draws all cascades + the variance term in ONE procedural pass, so a fused engine mask would only serve players who turn the enhanced shadows off. The ports keep the engine's exact passes (the EQUAL proof per unit); a fusion is a picture-preserving rewrite with its own compare and is left for the iGPU measurements (14.5).

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 331 / 0; `dxil-check embedded`: post 84 / 84 (every new port PASS); `rvk_rhitest --validation` (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`) PASS both backends (abi probe 55 / 55); `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan PASS (0 failures, 0 validation errors), `--backend d3d12` PASS. No new rhitest / d3d9test case: the ports add no GPU-address code (they read the ABI's register windows and bindless slots like the translated shaders) and the equality is proven per shader by rvk_shadereq and per unit in the game on both backends. Picture check: `native.picture_check=true` with the owned chain (`-All`) + `native.own_post=on`, jungle + pub_interior, `freeze_wait` 1800 (`pic`): **ok** in every settled window, 2 `UNRELIABLE (the scene moves)` windows at the spot change, never FAILED; DIFFERING / MISS 0 in every line; stage line `own_post=active(on: ...)`.

**Left on engine shaders, of the effect draws the native frame composes: none.** Not met in a run but ported and EQUAL offline: DSSM (no terrain map: dungeons), SSM_Low / SSAO_Low / PSSM_Low (the mask's HighQuality flag off: lower shadow / SSAO settings, not tested to keep the copy's options as they are), RadialBlurMask / Blood / Fast and EdgeSmoothDebug (no effect uses them: the RadialBlur / EdgeSmooth effect data name technique 0), ExpToneMap / ExpHDRI / ColorOutput / BrightPass / DebugAlphaChanel (no effect uses them), OverlayRadialColor / OverlayImage (no effect of the 13 uses them: S-7c hand ports, EQUAL offline). Not shaders: the 1/4 depth copy, Indicator and OverlayHit's CopyCurrentRTToImage (HAL copies). Other stages, not S-7d: the GUI sprite batches (F13), the effect shaders of particles / strips / rain / sky / decals (F11 / F12), the material shaders (ubershader). A port whose module is not built yet (the first seconds after the mode is switched on) draws the engine's shader for those frames (`native: own fx shaders` names it); that is the F15-1 pattern (pending module), not a lasting fallback.

### 14.19 F2f result: instance lists and hardware batches owned; F9 (blended terrain layers) not built (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a1b9ff84e76920b11` on main 0b73029; one Debug setting `native.owned_instances`, Full, run time, default off, needs `native.owned_persistent`; settings count 152)

**What was built.** With `native.owned_instances` the main view's render-instance ops (render-op node 0xffffff, solid list: `eCMeshResource2::ExecuteRenderInstances`) are owned like the other static mesh ops (`src/native/static_ops_inst_owned.cpp`, `src/gpuscene/row_table.*`):
* **Hardware batches are composed per instance** (`composeSolid`, main view, Static group): the engine's own fallback path for the same resource (`< 2 instances / lightmapped / not capable`): the non-instanced shader version of the material, one draw per instance with the instance's own illumination, switch and flags, c32 .w = 1 and c33..c35 = the instance's world rows. So every render-instance op is an instance list and needs no instance stream, no `c32 + 4 i` row block and no batch split (`sc::planBatches`) in the owned path. Picture: within the in-run picture check (below); GPU: below. (The plan of 15.4 row 6, per-batch instance-row images with an instanced indirect command, would need an instance-rate stream 3 that is position dependent in the persistent flush, i.e. a per-frame ramp + repeated refs as the trees' flush has: not needed once the batches are lists.)
* **The stamp holds the instance list** (`instanceStamp`, mixed into the geometry stamp): count, and per instance (in the order the composition draws: the engine's qsort with its comparator on a copy while `res +0xa4` is set, deterministic) the matrix, switch, `+0x44`, illumination pointer, flags and the illumination's pass-structure inputs (light list, sun bit, no-sun, static / dynamic light counts), read every frame (no engine event exists for the list: `depFast` / `depNoteCheckFrame` / `depRegister` are skipped for these records, the full stamp is the validity). The list header / bytes are left out of the composition's read log (they are rewritten every frame; the first build re-made 3,000 - 5,000 records per 5 s through the read-log audit until they were).
* **Each draw's VS window 2 is an immutable image in the instance row table** (`gpuscene::RowTable`, 16,384 images x 96 bytes, the object record's layout c31..c35 + info, window base = image - 31 x 16, kTablePad in front): written once from the composed registers when the record is built (`instanceRows`), released (deferred by kFramesInFlight + 1 presents) with the draw record (`orec::DrawRec::row`, RAII `RowHandle`), uploaded at the present with the other tables, GPU read-back every 240 frames (`native: instance rows (5 s)`, DIFFERING 0 in every run). The composed draws of a valid record are served from the same images (`instanceServeComposed`), so the check frames compare like with like; a draw whose shader reads c31 gets no image (the op is not retained). Kind `Object`, so the device counts it with the object windows and F5-2's cluster cull reads its rows (`RowTable::worldRowsAt` for the CPU check).
* **Records**: `recExclusion` takes the instance op when its list has 1 - 512 instances, none with overlay flags and all with a light list (`instanceExclusion`); `recLiveWhy` checks each draw's own illumination slot (`DrawRec::illumPtr`, a new `DrawDesc::illumPtr`); the owned frames run the slot's render-instance sort (`instanceSideEffects`: `SortRenderInstances` + `res +0xa4 = 0`) besides BindLightmaps / touches.
* **Scene forms + motion**: `Device::setSceneInstances` gives the non-instanced `draws::Kind::InstancedMesh` draws the scene forms of static meshes (`sceneDecide`'s `staticLike`), incl. the scene bone forms of a rigid draw with an object-record history; `bonePredict` no longer picks the bone-motion PUSH form for an issued instance draw while the stage is on (14.15 b's "instanced-mesh ops are composed every frame while rigid motion is on" is resolved: their window 2 is a row image, a still one has no object motion, a moving one changes its stamp and is composed that frame as before). With `native.vertex_pull` they are pulled like the other owned static draws.
* rvk.log `native: owned instances (5 s)` (stamps read, records built / live, owned ops / draws, batches composed per instance, composed draws served from their row image, sorts, stamp CPU), `native: instance rows (5 s)`; `native: stages (5 s)` `owned_instances=...`, `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1` knows the key.

**Owned share of the main view's static ops** (`native: owned static (5 s)`, settled spot windows, rvkcompare freeze_wait 1800; settings: Full, `-PlayerDefaults`, the F1 / F2c chain (`material_table`, `illum_table`, `lighting_images`, `owned_windows`, `owned_objects`, `owned_records`, `scene_draws`, `owned_static`, `owned_persistent`) + `rt.table_entries`, `lighting.clustered`, `lighting.full_lit`, `owned_shadows / trees / billboards / vegetation / vegetation_ops / actors`, `clusters` + `cluster_cull` + `cluster_cull_shadows`, `vertex_pull`, `debug.ubershader`, `bone_motion=on`, `rigid_motion`, `tree_wind=on`, `native.picture_check`; 0 views gated off in every window):

| spot | off (same build) | on | left to the engine path (on) |
|---|---|---|---|
| jungle | 78.7 % (hardware batch 8.1 %, instance list 7.0 %, blended 6.1 %) | **92.1 %** | blended / no z write 7.9 %, 1 texture refusal per view (7.43 finding 4) |
| harbour_docks | 67.4 % (batch 8.1 %, list 11.0 %, blended 13.4 %) | **86.6 %** | blended 13.4 %, 1 texture refusal per view |
| pub_interior | 63.8 % (batch 6.4 %, list 25.5 %, blended 4.3 %) | **95.7 %** | blended 4.3 %, 1 texture refusal per view |

(of all main views incl. the 2-in-10 check / warm-up frames; `ops` count a hardware batch op once in the off arm, its per-instance op once in the on arm.) Hardware batches and instance lists: 0 left in every settled window; what stays is F9's class. Jungle settled: 27 k instance ops / 67 k draws owned per 5 s, 231 records / 587 row images live; stamp CPU 10 ms per 5 s (~0.03 ms per view at jungle, 0.006 ms at pub_interior).

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 333 / 0 (settings count 152). `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan and `--backend d3d12`: PASS, 0 failures / 0 validation errors, new case **"instance rows (F2f)"** (two images served as VS window 2 through the device's window override with junk in the register file = the register-file reference bit for bit; a c31-composed image refused; a released slot has no window and is not handed out before the deferral). `rvk_rhitest --validation` Vulkan + `--d3d12 --validation`: PASS. `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded` PASS, `dxil-check uber` (census jungle + town) 337 / 337 in every family (no shader changed). Game runs (private copy `risen-test-vk-f2f`, lock `rvk-f2f`; logs `rvk-work/scratch/f2f/`): first run `-Validation` + `GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED` at jungle with the stage on: 0 VUID / error lines; **picture check** (jungle, harbour_docks, pub_interior): 12 windows `ok`, 16 `UNRELIABLE` (arrival / settling), **0 FAILED**; DIFFERING / MISS / EVENT MISS 0, row-table read-backs DIFFERING 0; **near-black** (`uberfix/blk.py`): jungle / harbour 0.0000 % in all burst frames in both arms, pub_interior 0.118 % on vs 0.115 % off (its own dark pixels, 14.12); **capture census** `native: captures (5 s)`: 0 requests in every settled window (nothing new); D3D12 `-Api d3d12 -Validation` (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`) jungle + harbour_docks with `-GracefulExit`: only the known DSV-format warning (33), 0 error lines, owned 86.6 %, `owned_instances=active`, exit code 0, no dump. Picture off vs on (separate runs): jungle differs in 7.6 % of the pixels (> 8 / 255), located in the SpeedTree foliage and the GI-lit ground patch (wind phase / temporal state between two sessions; the props drawn by the instance ops show no visible change), harbour_docks 0.21 % (its own burst noise 0.42 %), pub_interior 1.5 %; the in-run picture check (owned vs composed of the same session) is the gate.

**GPU** (`scripts/perf-ab.ps1 -Key native.owned_instances`, settings above without the picture check, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, private copy, one session per scene, 3 A/B pairs, 240-frame windows, stage states matched every window; `rvk-work/scratch/f2f/perfab`): **savegame hold: GPU frame 3.69 -> 4.32 ms (+0.62, z 4.0), almost all of it `water.rt` 1.05 -> 1.75 ms (+0.69, z 13, every B window 1.69 - 1.78 against every A window 1.01 - 1.11)**; the main colour / z / cascade passes unchanged (-0.01 .. -0.02); CPU engine + HAL 9.73 -> 10.49 ms (+0.76, z 2.0, private copy, other agents' load). Jungle: GPU 5.16 -> 5.95 ms (A noisy, sd 0.99), `water.rt` +0.30, the scene passes +0.04 .. +0.15. The TLAS instance / triangle counts and the RT scene's observe counts are the same in both arms (`rt: scene: instances` 1,302 / 1.16 M triangles), so the extra water RT time is not more geometry; not isolated. Candidate (unverified): the screen-reuse test of the water reflections reads the G-buffer motion / depth that the instance draws now write through their owned (scene ABI v2) forms instead of the composed ones. **This GPU cost is a gate the stage does not pass yet: it stays Debug / default off.**

**F9 (blended terrain layers) not built.** 4 - 13 % of the main view's static ops (jungle 55, harbour_docks 23 per view) stay composed. What owning them needs, beyond this stage: (1) a scene form for a non-batchable draw: `sceneDecide` gives scene pipelines only to `packet_.batchable` draws (no alpha blend), because `flushBatch` and the owned flush group packets by pipeline, which reorders draws; (2) an ordered owned path: the layers blend over the base terrain at equal depth, so they must be drawn after it and in list order: either at the op's own place (the F4-1 billboards' `submitOwned` path; it refuses whenever an observer `observes()` the kind and phase, i.e. with the RT scene on, so it would need the bulk observers of the persistent path) or one ordered run (consecutive items with one bucket key per indirect call, no grouping) at the end of the solid list (a hook there does not exist yet: the solid pass has `DoBeginRenderSolid` only); (3) the picture gate in Full: terrain crops jungle / beach / town. Recommended as its own stage (F9) together with F14 (the alpha / refracted lists need the same ordered machinery).

**Open.** (0) The `water.rt` cost above (+0.3 .. +0.7 ms on the XTX) must be explained and removed before the stage can be a default. (1) F9 above. (2) Moving instances (held equipment while the actor moves) remake their record every frame they move (composed that frame), as before this stage; an object-table window per instance whose matrix is its entity's would keep them owned, but the instance record carries no entity pointer (not resolved). (3) The run-to-run picture difference at jungle was not separated from a same-build control pair (two off runs).

#### 14.19.1 The `water.rt` cost of F2f explained: the hardware batches' cut-outs (2026-11-11)

**Cause (confirmed).** Not the G-buffer / screen reuse, not more TLAS geometry: the RT scene's **cut-out records**. `rt: scene: materials ... records: cut-out` = 179 (off) vs 290 (on) at the savegame hold, same instance / triangle counts. A temporary per-material census (patch kept in `rvk-work/scratch/wrt/wrtdiag.patch`) named the 111 extra cut-out instances: `Nat_Veg_Herbs_01` 70, `Nat_Veg_Ferns_01` 20, `Nat_Vegetation_Ivy_01` 16, `Nat_Veg_Herbs_02` 6, all hardware-batched instances (`kRtRecordInstanced`). In the off arm the batch draw (instanced shader version) resolves its RT material by the bytecode fallback (`uvSet` 0x310 = `kRtUvHeuristic`, name empty, `masked` false), so these masked vegetation instances enter the TLAS **opaque**; on, the per-instance draws (the non-instanced shader version) resolve the material graph (`masked`, Masked blend, opacity texture) and the instances become alpha-tested (`FORCE_NO_OPAQUE`, mask `kRtMaskAlphaTested`): every reflection ray that meets one runs the candidate loop (record, triangle, uv, ray-cone LOD, opacity sample). Proof by tweak: `rt.reflection_tweaks=1` (reflection rays treat cut-outs as solid) removes the delta (hold off 0.67 / on 0.74 ms, z 0.7); `=2` (no shadow rays) keeps it (0.84 / 1.64). **The on arm's picture is the correct one**: in-session toggle at swamp_pond (`water.debug=reflection`, frozen spot, off / on / off) shows the off arm reflecting the herb clumps as solid dark quads and the on arm as cut-out strands (off1 vs off2 control 0.05 % of the pixels > 8/255, off vs on 11 %; final picture 2.7 %, murky water). The off arm's batch material (heuristic, never masked) is a pre-existing RT-scene defect in every mode with hardware batches (Wrapper included); fixing it would make both arms pay the cut-out cost and change the default picture (decision left open).

**Fix (exact, both arms).** `rvkRtTraceClosestOpaqueFirst` / `rvkRtVisibilityOpaqueFirst` (`shaders/include/rvk_rt_trace.hlsli`), used by `water_rt.comp` for the reflection ray and the hit's shadow ray: one query without the cut-outs (mask without `RVK_RT_MASK_ALPHA_TESTED`; nothing to test), then a query of the cut-outs only (mask = alpha-tested = exactly the non-opaque instances) bounded by the opaque hit (closest) or skipped when an opaque surface already blocks (visibility). Same candidate set, same alpha test, same closest hit (up to exact-t ties): cut-outs behind the opaque hit are never tested. Other consumers keep the one-query functions (unoccluded rays would pay two traversals).

**Numbers** (`perf-ab.ps1 -Key native.owned_instances`, the F2f settings, private copy, 240-frame windows; `water.rt` ms): before (14.19) hold 1.05 -> 1.75, jungle 1.01 -> 1.31. With the split (RVK_SHADER_DIR session, 2 pairs, tight windows): hold **0.57 -> 0.82** (delta +0.25, z 31; without shadow rays +0.24 -> the rest is cut-outs in front of the opaque hits / along sky rays, i.e. the real alpha tests); embedded build, 3 pairs (noisy session, sd 0.16 - 0.30): hold 0.70 -> 1.05, jungle 0.79 -> 0.88 (+0.09, z 0.4). The on arm now costs what the off arm cost before (hold 1.05, jungle 1.01); the off arm is 0.3 - 0.4 ms cheaper itself. The residual on/off delta is the price of the correct cut-outs, not a defect of the instance path. Gates: `rvk_tests` 333 / 0, `dxil-check embedded` 169 / 169, `rvk_rhitest --validation` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS, `rvk_d3d9test --validation` + `--backend d3d12` PASS.

**Seen on the way (not caused by this change):** a GPU hang + `VK_ERROR_DEVICE_LOST` in the OFF arm with main's shaders (`RVK_SHADER_DIR` = the old files) at rvkcompare `lake_jungle` arrival, all F2f settings (`rvk.log`: "the GPU has not finished frame value 4593 after 12 s"); no `VK_EXT_device_fault` report was logged. Cross-session pictures at the spots differ by SpeedTree wind phase and splash particles (harbour_docks) far more than by the arms: judge the arms only inside one session (`rvk-work/scratch/wrt/toggle.ps1`: `shot <name>` + `set` at `freeze+N`).

**Follow-up (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a6f1842b52b25cd19` on main 27ea91b): the batch material fixed; the opaque-first traces proven; two platform defects found on the way** (details: rt-reflections.md 3.3.1).
* **Correction: the batch defect was Full-only, not "every mode".** In Wrapper the engine runs the batch's `ExecuteShaderConstants`, so `eCShaderBase::ms_pActiveShader` is the material's shader and the RT material already came from the graph (rvk.log `rt: scene: materials ... bytecode s9 0` at swamp_pond, Wrapper; Wrapper sessions with the pre-fix behaviour and with the fix show the same herb strands). In Full the lean path skips the slot code, the active shader is stale, and the batch draws fell back to the bytecode heuristic (`bytecode s9` 694 - 781 per 5 s window at swamp_pond).
* **Fix** (`enh/rt_scene.cpp` `RtScene::batchMaterial`): a hardware batch is drawn by `eCMeshResource2::ExecuteRenderInstances(sub)` without `eCSubMesh::Render`, so its draw tag has no material; the RT scene now takes the op's sub-mesh (`tag.opDesc` node 0xffffff, sub-mesh = desc & 0xff, `eCRenderContextMesh +0x28` -> `eCMeshResource2 +0x58 / +0x60`, sub-mesh `+0x38`, the draw's index range inside the sub-mesh's `+0x28 / +0x2c`, SEH-guarded reads) as the material the per-instance path tags (`learnEngineMaterials`, every pass). rvk.log `rt: scene: materials ... batch sub-mesh materials N`. A/B: `rt.reflection_tweaks` bit 64 = the pre-fix behaviour (run time).
* **Proof** (Full, F2f chain + all stages, swamp_pond, one session, `water.debug=reflection`): batched (owned_instances off, the fix) vs per-instance (on) vs batched: reflection 0.001 % / 0.000 % of the pixels > 8/255 against a 0.000 % control (was 11 % before the fix), RT sun shadow term (`shadows.rt=near`, `shadows.debug=rt_term`) 0.000 %, final picture 0.003 % (control 0.003 %); the same cut-out record count in both arms (325). In-session pre-fix (64) vs fix: reflection 7.1 % / 6.7 % (control 0.021 %): the dark quads come back with 64; RT shadow term 0.02 % (control 0.001 %: the herbs' near shadows are small at this spot).
* **Cost** (`perf-ab.ps1 -Key rt.reflection_tweaks -A 64 -B 0 -Mode full -PlayerDefaults -Setting 'shadows.rt=near','rt.reflections=medium'`, 3 pairs of 240-frame windows, shared copy): `water.rt` hold 0.51 -> 0.75 ms (+0.24, z 4.2), jungle 0.32 -> 0.45 (+0.13, z 10.7), swamp_pond 0.68 -> 1.08 (+0.40, z 4.6); `shadows.rt trace` hold -0.15 (z -0.8, noise), jungle +0.01, swamp_pond +0.36 (z 1.4, A noisy). This is the price of the correct cut-outs with the opaque-first traces already in place (the RT sun shadows trace one query: rvkRtVisibilityOpaqueFirst there is a candidate).
* **The opaque-first traces are exact** after two fixes (rhitest `opaque-first traces` + the in-game compare `rt.reflection_tweaks` 32: 0 differing rays at swamp_pond and harbour_docks; before the fixes 31 - 32 rays per frame differed at swamp_pond): (1) on Vulkan a texture size query of a lane-varying bindless index returns the first lane's size (the alpha test's ray-cone LOD; the two forms run a candidate in waves of other lanes, so they picked other mips near the alpha threshold): the records now carry their textures' sizes (`uvSet` bits 16-31, `rvkRtTexArea`); (2) on D3D12 a cut-out's committed t is wrong with TMin > 0 when the loop read `CandidateTriangleRayT` (826 of 826 rays): `rvkRay` starts D3D12 rays at tMin.

### 14.20 F15-1 result: no composed-late fallback from pipelines / variants / textures after the first seconds (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a89e9a26c41d601a9` on main 0b73029; no new setting, default behaviour in Full, `-Environment 'RVK_F15_BASELINE=1'` = the old behaviour for A/B; settings count unchanged)

**Measured first (new counters, every mode where `native.owned_static` acts).** `native: owned static (5 s)` / `owned shadows (5 s)` now split the composed-late ops by ROOT reason (`root reasons: ...`: the refusal site's own reason instead of "a draw refused"), and while any owned op is composed late rvk.log holds one `native: composed late (1 s) t+N s: main X ops in V views (x / view), cascades ...; <reasons>; variant queue Q (built B, failed F run total)` line per second (t = seconds since the first owned view of the session: the warm-up profile). Reasons: `forms pending at the record's composition` (the record was made while its scene forms / pipelines were still being built: `CachedDraw::v.scenePending`, deriveScene code 4 site 1 / code 16), `variant module arrived since` (sites 3 / 5), `variant decision moved`, `texture not sampleable`, `observer capture`, `record table full`, `shader module changed`, `material stamp / poll`, `view windows`, ... (`kLateNames`, `static_ops_owned.cpp`). Scratch tools: `rvk-work/scratch/f151/late.py <log> [bucket s]` (the 1 s lines bucketed), `fstat.py` (the new `perf: frame intervals (ms): n p50 p95 p99 max` line of `-Perf`).

**What was pending, and why** (everything on as 15.1 + `native.rigid_motion`, `native.tree_wind=on`, `native.own_foliage`, Full, `-PlayerDefaults`, `RVK_TABLE_NOTRIP=1`, frame generation fsr3 on Vulkan; owned static 70 - 80 % of the ops in every settled window, `gated off` 0 after the first window; private copy `risen-test-vk-f151`):
1. **Cold caches** (fresh copy, no SPIR-V / HLSL / pipeline cache): 545,508 ops composed late over 242 s of spots, **all `forms pending`** (100 - 290 ops per main view for ~70 s, cascades the same once they arrive). Cause: `Device::sceneDecide` requested a draw's forms one after the other, each step only when the next composition found the previous one done: v1 modules -> v1 pipeline -> v2 modules -> v2 pipeline -> pulled module -> pulled pipeline, six round trips through the variant queue (3 threads, FIFO) and the pipeline workers.
2. **Warm caches** (SPIR-V on disk): `forms pending` for the first ~30 s (4,448 ops) and at every new spot / area, because **the scene forms' pipelines were never remembered across runs**: `pipelines.keys` held only base-module pipelines (`moduleHashes_`), the scene forms are variant modules and their pipelines are built by `prewarmOncePipeline`, which never recorded a key.
3. **Steady state, every frame**: `texture not sampleable` 0.8 ops per main view (7,309 in a 190 s spots run, 12,101 in a 370 s walk): an 800 x 450 render target (the render-scale image at `upscale.mode=performance`) one static material samples is still in its colour-attachment layout when the owned pass reaches it; the record's derivation refused (code 7), the op was composed, and the composition's `prepareTextures` made it sampleable. That is the "texture 1 per view" of 15.1 / 7.43 finding 4.
4. Not readiness (stay, by design): `material stamp / poll` (a material's structure version moved: streaming at a spot arrival; 15 - 50 ops per run), and on the first seconds of a session single events of `observer capture` / `variant decision moved` (n3: 437 ops before t+30 s).

**What was built.** (a) `sceneDecide` requests every form at a draw's first sight (`variant()` of the v2 vertex / pixel and the pulled vertex form next to the v1 ones) and, while the v1 pipeline is still building, also the v2 / pulled pipelines whose modules exist (`earlyPipelines`, counted `early v2 / pulled pipeline requests` in `native: scene draws (5 s)`); the decision itself is unchanged (only builds start earlier). (b) **Variant pipeline keys**: `PipelineKey` carries the variant slot of each stage (`vsSlot` / `psSlot`, `kBaseModule`, `kNoModule` = depth-only, magic `RVKPKEY2`: old files are ignored once), `variantHashes_` maps a variant module to (shader, slot), `recordBackgroundKey` remembers every background-built scene pipeline; at a shader's creation the known keys with variant modules request those variants (only while the variant's provider is registered) and the pipeline is built as soon as both modules exist (`prewarmKey`, `prewarmVariantKeys` from the variant worker). rvk.log `d3d9: N pipelines known from earlier runs (M of them with variant modules ...)`, `native: pipeline keys (run total): ...` (keys loaded / variant modules requested / waits / pipelines prewarmed / recorded). (c) **The texture case owned**: before an owned op is derived, a texture of its records that is not sampleable (and is not a bound target) is made ready by `Device::ownedPrepTexture`, exactly the step `prepareTextures` does for a composed draw (end the scope, `prepareForSampling` on the frame list, or the upload list for a pending upload), after the items queued so far are flushed: the same barrier at the same place in the command stream as the late composition made, without composing the op (counted `textures made ready instead N` in `native: owned static (5 s)`). No draw is ever left out or delayed: a draw whose forms are still pending is composed as before (it was drawn, only not owned).

**Composed-late census, before / after** (ops composed late in the whole run; `readiness` = pending forms + module arrived + texture):

| run | baseline (`RVK_F15_BASELINE=1` / pre-change build) | F15-1 | last second with a readiness reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| spots, cold caches (b1 / n1) | 545,508 + 4,224 texture | 137,654 pending + 133 arrived, 0 texture | t+242 / t+206 (DXC-bound: the variant queue drains ~150 s) |
| spots, warm SPIR-V, no variant keys yet (n2) | - | 498 pending (t+14 .. 29, 8 at a new spot t+84) | t+84 |
| spots, warm + variant keys (b2 / n3) | 4,448 pending + 7,309 texture = 11,782 | 88 pending + 26 arrived + 109 decision + 328 observer (all before t+30 except 21 at the spot change t+80) | t+175 / t+83 |
