| savegame walk 370 s, lod 2x (wb / wn) | 2,712 pending + 12,101 texture = 14,828 | 56 pending + 32 arrived (t+18 .. 28) + 50 material | **t+347 / t+28** |

Per main view in the walk's settled windows: baseline 0.8 composed-late ops every frame, F15-1 **0** from t+29 s to the end (the only later lines: `material stamp / poll` at a streaming burst, t+330 .. 359, 35 ops). Trees / grass have their own counters (`native: owned trees [main view]`: `special lighting` / `no record` / `no template` = their stage's exclusions, ~2 % of the taken tree ops at jungle, not readiness; grass `late` 13 in the walk): not in F15-1's scope.

**Hitches / loading.** The variant-key prewarm runs on the variant (3) and pipeline-prewarm threads during the load (n3: 391 of 690 keys with variant modules, 319 variant modules requested, 669 variant-key + 232 base pipelines prewarmed). Frame times (`-Perf`, walk, 29 / 40 world windows): F15-1 p50 20.7 ms, p99 median 40.7 (worst window 176), baseline p50 14.4, p99 29.8 (worst 144); loads (`tools/loadtimes.py`): first 11.9 s / quick loads 5.2 s against 6.9 / 3.6. **Not a valid comparison**: the machine ran other agents' games (engine + HAL 24 - 38 ms per frame in both runs, ~8 normally; base fps 18 vs 55 in the windows sampled); repeat the walk A/B on a quiet machine (Open 1).

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 333 / 0; `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS (0 validation errors); `rvk_rhitest --validation` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS; every game run PASS with `-GracefulExit` clean (exit 0, no dumps), MISS / DIFFERING 0, no `[error]` line (8 Vulkan runs, 4 D3D12 runs). **D3D12** (PSO path; jungle + harbour_docks + pub_idle, no FSR / frame generation): first run with the new key format 655 ops late (524 pending, 123 arrived, last t+87), second run (332 variant-key + 227 base PSOs prewarmed from 621 keys) 240 (190 `variant decision moved` in the first seconds, 8 pending, last t+58), owned static 71 % (all owned frames); jungle A/B on D3D12 (warm): baseline 4,053 ops (550 pending, 3,486 texture, until the end) against 256 (214 `variant module arrived since` + 8 pending before t+39, 34 material); the D3D12 runs end with the known engine exit crash (`SharedBase.dll+0x7caa9`, every render mode, not ours), Vulkan exits 0. **Picture check** (`native.picture_check`, jungle / pub_interior / harbour_docks, freeze_wait 1800, no FSR / frame generation): 21 windows `ok`, **FAILED 0** (16 UNRELIABLE = arrivals). That run was the copy's first with `upscale.mode=off` (other render targets: 240 new keys) and composed 3,299 ops late for pending forms until t+119: a new configuration's first session behaves like the warm-SPIR-V case (n2). Pictures: near-black pixels (`blk.py`) equal between the arms (harbour_docks 0 / 0, jungle 6 / 0, pub_idle 739 / 709, monastery_hall 45 k / 45 k = the hall's dark shadow; hero_idle 1.7 k - 5.9 k varies run to run with the hero's animation, the pictures look alike). D3D12 / picture check: see below.

**Exit race found and fixed on the way**: a shader worker finishing during `Device::destroy()` (between `stopVariantWorker` and `stopShaderWorkers`) could reach `variant()` through the key prewarm and restart the stopped variant threads; `destroying_` stops `variant()` / `prewarmKey` from then on.

**`native: owned shaders (5 s)`** gets a `none` column (UberWhy::NoPixel): S-6's depth-only forms are no longer filed under `failed` (n3, one window: StaticMesh owned 125,624 = uber 22,892, **none 86,516**, failed 16,216; Skinned owned 4,782 = uber 1,704, none 2,738, failed 340). The remaining `failed` was not analysed (Open 2).

**Open.** (1) Walk frame-time / load-time A/B on a quiet machine (`scratch/f151/run.ps1 -Scenario walk -Perf` with and without `RVK_F15_BASELINE=1`, `fstat.py`, `loadtimes.py`). (2) The `failed` share of owned static items (13 %): a scene form built translated (likely the rigid / bone-motion pixel forms, 14.13 / 14.15), unverified. (3) Cold first session: DXC-bound (the variant queue drains for ~150 s at jungle); more variant threads / a priority for the owned forms were not tried. (4) `material stamp / poll` composes an op whose material changed (a real data change): owning it means rebuilding the record without a composition (F10 territory).

### 14.21 The all-on soak owned nothing: the mesh part table's observer gate; the read-log leak behind "MISS (audit)" (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a70c47a6e65ecebeb` on main 0b73029; no new setting)

**Symptom.** The soak of main with the full all-on list (`rvk-work/scratch/soak/allon.txt`, Full + `-PlayerDefaults`) owned nothing: every main / cascade view `gated off`, every owned stage `inactive(armed, nothing owned in the window)`, no reason logged (the "not this view" line has a budget of 4 and spent it on the start-up gates: object table, lighting images pending).

**Gate (measured).** `native.mesh_parts` (F0's diagnostic part + layout tables) registers the `MeshPartTable` as a `DrawObserver` at its first composed static draw (only for the buffer notifications; `observes()` is always false). It never declared `registersRead()`, so it kept the default `Registers::All`, and `Device::ownedObserversOk` (F2c-3, 7.16) refuses ownership while any such observer is registered. **Not a regression of a merge**: the code is unchanged since F0 / F2c-3 (2026-11-06). The soak list was the first all-on list that contained `native.mesh_parts`. The F2f / F15-1 lists that owned did not have it. `RVK_TABLE_NOTRIP` is unrelated: it only keeps the RT scene's `rt.table_entries` on, and that trip did not fire in any soak run. Proof run: `RVK_MESHPARTS_GATE=1` (the old declaration, kept as an A/B switch) logs `native: owned static: main views gated: observer 'mesh part table' declares no register reads (Registers::All)`.

**Fix.** `MeshPartTable::registersRead()` = `Registers::None`. Every observer now has `observerName()` (rt scene, gi, material rt, object motion, mesh part table).

**Gate logging (new).** `ownedBegin` names the first unmet condition (`StaticOps::OwnedGate`: records, scene draws, windows, objects off / not ready, lighting images off / pending, material table, table lighting, observer, not verified). It is logged once per change per view kind (`native: owned static: main views gated: <reason> (after N views in the previous state)` / `... gate open (after N gated views)`) and counted per 5 s window: `N gated off: <reason> <count>, ...` in the `owned static (5 s)` and `owned shadows (5 s)` lines. The trees' and vegetation's own readiness log `native: owned trees: gated: <reason>` / `native: owned vegetation draws: gated: <reason>` and `gate open`. `Device::ownedObserversOk / ownedTreeObserversOk / ownedVegObserversOk(std::string* why)` name the observer and the reason. Stage line (`native: stages (5 s)`): `inactive(armed, gated: <reason> <count>)` and `inactive(setting on, code off: needs <keys>)`. The keys come from the setting's `Spec::needs` (`Registry::unmetNeedKeys`) plus the settings `applyStaticOps` arms the stage with. `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1`'s regex takes the new forms (one nested paren level).

**MISS (audit) (measured, fixed).** `RVK_AUDIT_SITES=1` (new, diagnostics) stores each logged read's call chain. The audit line then ends `read at d3d9.dll + <reader> < <caller> < ...` (`llvm-symbolizer --obj=d3d9.dll 0x180000000+<offset>`) plus the stamp's illumination and the record's anchors. Every disagreement (Vulkan and D3D12) was a read of ANOTHER op's composition: actor illuminations (`composeActorImpl -> materialDrawsImpl`: the light list pointer, `illum +0xd`) and the shader admin's special-pass type (`specialPassDrawImpl`: `admin +0x314`, 0 / 3 / 6). They sat in static-mesh and actor records whose own stamps held. Cause: `recEnter` reset `Store::readLog` only when the record was being built at entry. An op excluded after its composition (`recComposed`'s `exclude`) left its reads behind. A record rebuilt for a post-composition stamp (view / materials / draw count, `R.building` set in `recComposed`, composed WITHOUT the log on) then took the leftover reads as its own and lacked its own. So these were not misses of the owned path: the records were audited against foreign memory. A record rebuilt that way also carried no audit coverage of its own. Fix: `recEnter` always starts a fresh log (`Store::logging` = this op's composition was logged; a record takes reads only then). `bbEnter` does the same for the billboard records. After it: 234,675 + 68,126 audits in a spots run, 0 disagreements (the admin-only class measured 0, so no exemption was added).

**Numbers (all-on list + `-PlayerDefaults`, `-Perf`; private copy `risen-test-vk-gate`).**

| run | static (main) | cascades | actors | trees (main) | MISS / DIFFERING |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Vulkan walk (`rvk_rtwalk`) | 76 - 78 % | 99 - 100 % | z / solid / cascades 100 % | 31 - 34 k ops taken per window (~99 %) | 0 / 0 |
| Vulkan spots (jungle, harbour_docks, hero_idle, pub_idle) | 69 - 75 % | 80 - 96 % | 100 % | (no trees at the hero / pub spots) | 0 / 0 (audit 0 after the leak fix) |
| D3D12 spots | 68 - 71 % | 99 - 100 % | 100 % | as Vulkan | 0 / 0 |
| Vulkan picture check (jungle, pub_interior, harbour_docks; FSR + frame generation dropped, `freeze_wait=1800`) | 70 - 71 % | 100 % | 100 % | | `ok` in all 10 settled windows (worst view 0.04 - 0.09 %), `FAILED 0`; the other 18 windows UNRELIABLE (spot changes / the moving camera) |

Gates: `rvk_tests` 333 / 0; `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS (0 failures, 0 validation errors); `rvk_rhitest` Vulkan + D3D12 PASS.

### 14.22 F9 + F14 result: ordered owned draws - the blended terrain layers, the solid list's no-z-write draws and the alpha / refracted lists drawn from records at their op's place (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a74a0948549b108e8` on main 0f8f85b + 27ea91b; one Debug setting `native.owned_ordered`, Full, run time, default off, needs `native.owned_persistent`; settings count 153)

**What was built** (`src/native/static_ops_ordered.cpp`; the device's half in `d3d9/draw.cpp`, `scene_draws.cpp`, `owned_persist.cpp`):
* **Scene forms for order-dependent draws.** `Device::setSceneOrdered`: a main-view static mesh / refracted / F2f instance-list draw of the solid, alpha or refracted pass that is NOT batchable (blend, no z write) gets its v1 + v2 scene forms like a batchable one (`orderedNow_` in prepareDraw, `CachedDraw::ordered`); the pipeline carries the blend (dynamic state, part of the bucket key). A composed ordered scene draw is recorded alone AFTER the pending batch: the batch is flushed BEFORE `toScenePacket` puts the draw's record into `sceneRecs_` (a flush empties that list; flushing after it was the first device loss, below). `replayDraw` / `replayOwned` / `ownedDerive` accept `c.ordered` where they required `batchable`.
* **Records.** With the setting on, `recEnter` records the Mesh group's alpha / refracted ops too, `recExclusion` keeps the blended solid ops (no `Blended` pre-check), lets alpha / refracted ops have fade < 1 (the fade is in the stamp and the object window), a sort id and a depth-bias material; `recComposed` marks the record `ordered` (every alpha / refracted op; a solid op when a draw blends or writes no depth); an alpha / refracted draw without a lighting entry is kept (unlit: its derivation refuses if the shader reads PS window 3). `OpRecord::ordered` records are never compacted (`ownedSelect`: `ordered (drawn from records at their place)`); their side effects are the composition's BindLightmaps calls (`recLightmaps_`) + the touches. A depth-bias record (`OpRecord::dbvp`: the composed c0..c3 = the depth-bias view-projection) reads its list's dbvp view slot.
* **Drawing at the op's place.** On an owned frame the op stays in the engine's list and its slot is entered at the engine's position: `StaticOps::orderedPlace` (opEnterImpl, before the composition) checks the record like `ownedSelect` (`orderedValid`: event fast path / full stamp, live slot, scene draws), the view stamp, derives / reuses the persistent DrawRecords (`ownedDrawOpPersistent`, unchanged) and appends them to a **pending run**; the slot is skipped, the side effects run. A run = consecutive ordered ops of one list segment and view slot: it is queued as ONE ordered `ownedFlush(..., ordered = true)` (a bucket is a run of consecutive items with one key, never merged with a later one; packets not batchable, recorded after the pending batch) when anything else is recorded: `Device::setOrderedHook` + `setOrderedPending` - the hook runs at the start of `submitInterned`, `flushBatch` (every draw, clear, scope end, copy: the refraction copy sees the solid list's layers), `ownedFlush` and `ownedTreeFlush` (their scratch must not nest), and at `viewEnd` / a new list segment. The engine's own sort result is the order: nothing is re-sorted (F14's per-frame back-to-front order is the list as Execute runs it).
* **View slots**: the solid list's ops read slot 1 (the solid pass's own registers; a second `set` with other content refuses), the refracted / alpha ops slots 16 / 17 and their depth-bias twins 2 (`kViewSlotDbvp0`) / 18 / 19 (c0..c3 = dbvp), set once per list segment and view. The env epoch of an ordered segment has its own index per list (`Device::ownedEnvEpochAt`, `kOwnedEnvOrdered`): it keeps the last four (hash, epoch) pairs, so the device state the composed ops in between leave behind (sampler rows the derivation does not own) does not re-derive the records every frame. `Persist` holds the slot and env index (the MISS check uses them).
* **Observers** see an ordered draw per draw (`Device::setOwnedBulkSuspended`: the F2c-4b bulk contract is plain opaque static draws); a draw an observer wants a capture of refuses and the op is composed. The register file is saved only when an observer loads a draw's windows into it (`Device::ownedRegsArm` / `ownedRegsRestore`), the shaders / tag per op.
* **Water's draw replacer**: a replaced draw (the enhanced water, `water.enabled`) is not retainable (`replayAllowed`), so its op is never recorded and stays composed where the replacer acts; with the engine's water the refracted ops are ordinary ordered records. Particles, strips, blended billboard sets and the skinned actors' alpha ops are other groups (F11 / F6) and unchanged.
* rvk.log `native: owned ordered (5 s)` (per list: looked at / owned / draws / composed with the reason, not recorded by reason, records built; runs, ordered flushes = buckets, lost draws; CPU at the ops split into stamps / setup / draws / side effects + run flushes), `native: stages (5 s)` `owned_ordered=...`, `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1` knows the key; diagnostics `native: owned ordered: ... without a scene form`, `... not retained`, `... not recorded (draw level)`.

**Two GPU page faults on the way (device loss under GPU-assisted validation, reported by the orchestrator; both fixed and covered by d3d9test):** (1) av1: the composed scene form of a blended draw flushed the pending batch AFTER `toScenePacket` had pushed its record, so `p.scene` indexed an emptied `sceneRecs_` (garbage window bases); fixed by flushing first (`submitPacket`, `replayDraw`, `replayOwned`); case "the composed scene forms ... opaque + blended interleaved". (2) p1: the alpha list's fading herbs bind stream 3 at instance rate (`0x80000001`, stream 0 not instanced); an indirect owned draw's `firstInstance` is its record index, so the instance-rate attribute was read at that index, out of its one-element buffer; fixed: such a draw gets no ordered scene form (`orderedNow_` requires no `STREAMSOURCE_INSTANCEDATA` stream) and `ownedFlush` refuses an ordered item with a per-instance binding (CPU guard); case "a draw with an instance-rate stream" + the forged-item guard. After both: GPU-assisted validation runs av3 (jungle + pub_interior) and av4 (jungle): 0 VUID / error lines, no device loss.

### 14.23 F6-2 result: the actors' last exclusions owned (focus highlight, fade / alpha, switch materials, morph picks); the alpha list's herbs owned; a device loss of the ordered path found and closed (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a8f448b75194da031` on main 5225240; no new setting: extends `native.owned_actors` (+ `native.owned_ordered` for the order-dependent draws), `-Environment 'RVK_F62_OFF=1'` = F6-1's exclusions, `RVK_F62_INSTRATE_OLD=1` = the herbs' instance-rate binding; settings count unchanged, 153)

**Census first** (new rvk.log line `native: actor census (5 s)`: every actor op per view class (main / cascades / other) x list (z / solid / alpha / refracted), owned or composed with its reason: `orec::Excl`, `check / warm-up / gated view`, `record refused at selection`, `no record yet`, `alpha list (not recorded)`, `view not recorded`; + the records not built after the composition, ordered actor ops drawn at their place, morph pick sets; parser `rvk-work/scratch/f62/cen.py` gives the share of all ops and of the owned frames' ops). All-on list (`rvk-work/scratch/soak/allon.txt` + `owned_instances` + `owned_ordered`, Full, `-PlayerDefaults`, FSR performance + frame generation), private copy `risen-test-vk-f62`, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f62/`:

| run | main z | main solid | cascades z | alpha (actors) | what stayed composed (owned frames) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| main's code (m1: jungle, harbour_docks, hero_idle, pub_idle, `show_actors=1`) | 80 % (100 % of the owned frames) | **0 %** | 64 - 66 % (82 %) | - | solid: every op `no record yet` (below: a counting bug under FSR); cascades: 18 % no record |
| + the counting fix only (m4fsr, `focus=1`) | 63 - 78 % | 36 - 72 % (46 - 89 %) | 67 - 80 % | - | overlay flags (the focus NPC: 582 ops / 5 s at jungle, in z, solid and cascades), switch materials 11 - 12 % of the solid ops everywhere |
| **F6-2 (s3: hero_idle, pub_idle, harbour_docks, jungle, `show_actors=1`, `focus=1`)** | **79.4 - 80.5 % = 99.3 - 100 % of the owned frames** | **78.9 - 80.5 % = 98.7 - 100 %** | **78.2 - 80.9 % = 98.7 - 100 %** | rare: 60 of 66 ordered (r1av) | the first 10 - 20 s of the first spot (pipelines / bone forms still compiling: `record refused at selection`), 8 - 12 ops `no record yet` at a spot change |
| `RVK_F62_OFF=1` + `RVK_F62_INSTRATE_OLD=1` arm (s0off, same spots, same binary) | 78.6 % of the owned frames at the focus spots, else 100 % | **46.4 % at the focus spots, 87.5 - 89.3 % elsewhere** | 82.9 % at the focus spots, else 100 % | - | overlay flags 1,350 - 1,446 ops / 5 s (z + solid + cascades), switch materials 11 - 12 % of the solid ops |

In every settled window of s3 the actors' share is 80.0 % of all ops = 100 % of the owned frames (`native.owned_check=10`: 1 check + 1 warm-up frame in 10 are composed by design); the focus NPC's ops are ordered records drawn at their place: 1,356 - 1,440 of 1,356 - 1,440 per 5 s at pub_idle / hero_idle with FSR + frame generation.

**What was owned, and how** (`src/native/static_ops_actors_owned.cpp` unless noted):
* **The FSR counting bug (main solid 0 %).** A composed draw whose packet-cache replay is refused (an observer capture, 2,400 per 5 s with FSR) is set up a second time on the fresh path; `actorServeComposed` counted both calls, so `actorCur_.draws != drawCount_` and `recEnd` built no record (`draws not offered the bone table`). Now per draw index (`ActorCur::drawMask / offeredMask`): the last call decides.
* **Overlay flags (the highlighted focus NPC).** The z pass is the special pass without the flags: z and cascade ops are no longer excluded (`entityFlags` is in the stamp: a change rebuilds the record). The solid pass's `GetPasses(flags)` adds the highlight passes (blended): with `native.owned_ordered` the op's record is ordered (`recComposed`), and `orderedPlace` now takes the Actors group's solid / alpha ops (`StaticOps::actorOrderedNow`, tag `Source::Skinned`; an actor record never takes the event fast path in `orderedValid`: it has no event dependencies). `Device::prepareDraw`'s `orderedNow_` accepts `Kind::Skinned` (while `sceneActors_`).
* **Fade < 1 and the alpha list's actor ops.** The fade is c32.w of the bone image (served per frame) plus the pass states (`passStates`: blended below 1): the stamp and the bone key hold the fade's CLASS (`fadeClass`: 1 or "fading"), not its bits (a fading actor would rebuild its record and take a new bone slot every frame). Fade < 1 is no exclusion; the alpha list's actor ops (`RenderAlpha`) are recorded (`recEnter`: `actorOrderedNow` for List::Alpha, lit like the solid ops) and drawn as ordered records at their place (view slot 17).
* **Switch materials (NPC variants: 11 - 12 % of the solid ops at every spot).** `eCShaderBase::ExecutePass`'s SetSwitch writes registers / a sampler slot per entity variant into the shader's pools, so the material table's record of the shader (read from the engine object) holds whichever variant was written last: such registers were flagged history dependent (`sw` bits: windows not served) and the composition excluded (`Excl::Switch`). Now the material table has **switch variant records** (`MaterialTable::lookupOrCreateVariant`, key = (shader, hash of the pass's switch writes)): the polled pools + the variant's writes on top (`overlay_`, applied in every `refresh` / `pollOwned`), exact for that value; `passDraw`'s `applySwitch(..., variant)` collects the writes (`passSw_`), `checkMaterial` gives the draw the variant slot (the per-frame memo keys on the hash), `noteSwitchWrite(..., variant)` still marks the base record's registers (its windows stay unserved) but does not count `switchNotes` (so `Excl::Switch` no longer fires for a variant draw). The check frames compare every composed register with the variant record (`native: material table (5 s)`: registers 771,030 compared, DIFFERING 0, `switch variants: 16 slots made, 7,329 draws` per 5 s at the pub). Idle variant slots are freed like base ones (`varKey_`; a base slot's map entry is only erased by its own slot). On while `native.owned_actors` is on (`switchVariantsOn`); static meshes with switch materials (the alpha list's ~1 / view) are recorded too.
* **Morph pick changes** (2 per blink; 8 - 264 record rebuilds per 5 s at hero_idle with the clock running). The pick left the geometry hash: for an actor the stamp's `lightmaps` field (an actor has no lightmaps) holds the pick's hash; a record rebuilt for a new pick keeps the previous pick's draws (`OpRecord::pickSets`, at most 4, LRU; `actorPickKeep` in `recEnd`), and a pick seen before swaps its draws in (`actorPickSwap` in `ownedSelect`, `orderedValid` and `recEnter`) instead of a composition. Census: `morph picks: N swapped in from earlier picks, M new (composed), K kept for later` (s1 hero_idle: 23 swapped, 332 new in the first window; the blinks of a frozen spot repeat few picks).
* **Captures of the focus NPC's highlight passes** (found under FSR: `rt solid for motion x skinned / morph` 900 + 900 per 5 s at pub_idle, the same before F6-2: m4fsr 363 + 363 at jungle): a skinned main-solid draw that writes no G-buffer motion (the additive highlight pass, a blended fading actor: `gbufferEligible` false) needs no previous positions, but the RT scene / object motion asked a capture for it. `Device::noMotionOf(CachedDraw)` / `noMotionNow` (`d3d9/bone_motion.cpp`) make `boneMotionCovered()` true for such draws (object motion writes motion only through the G-buffer's motion pixel variant: nothing is lost). After: **0 capture requests** in every settled window of s3 except `motion rigid x instanced mesh` (held equipment, 14.15's push form by design, not an actor class).

**Task 2: the alpha list's herbs** (`Nat_Veg_Herbs_*`, ~10 ops / view at jungle; also `Special_Fog_Ground_01`, `Nat_Obj_SpiderWebs_*`). They bind stream 3 with `D3DSTREAMSOURCE_INSTANCEDATA | 1` while stream 0 has no `INDEXEDDATA`: the draw is not instanced, the attribute reads element 0 of a one-element buffer. `Device::computeVertexInput` now binds such a stream **per vertex with stride 0** (the same element for every vertex: the same values; an indirect draw's firstInstance = its record index no longer moves it) while the ordered path is on (`Device::instRateAsVertex`; the pulled form's fetch stride is 0 too); the draw then gets its ordered scene forms (the `orderedNow_` test reads the draw's BINDINGS, not the device's stream slots) and its VS windows 1 / 2 (`Device::streamsInstanced`: an instance-data stream bound per vertex is not instanced for the windows, `setOwnedFor` / `recEnd`). The flush guard (an ordered item with a per-instance binding is refused) is unchanged. **Found on the way: main's alpha list was 0 % owned in the all-on run with FSR** (m1: `not a scene draw` 7,000 - 9,100 per 5 s): the old test looked at the device's stream slots, and the engine leaves stream 3 bound at instance rate after the herbs, so every later alpha draw counted as instance-rate. Alpha list after: **jungle 95.8 %, harbour_docks 83.7 - 84.7 %** of the looked-at ops (the rest: no record = the enhanced water's replaced draws / water-tagged), `native: owned static (5 s)` 98.4 - 100 % of the main view's static ops.

**The device loss (GPU-assisted validation run a1av, 08:41:47; fixed, regression case added).** With the actors' ordered records a pending ordered run met a packet-cache REPLAY of a batchable composed scene draw: `replayDraw` pushed the draw's record into `sceneRecs_` (`toScenePacket`), then `submitInterned` queued the pending run first (`orderedFlushPending` -> `ownedFlush` -> `flushBatch`), which recorded the waiting batch and emptied `sceneRecs_`; the replayed packet then joined the batch with an index into the emptied list. At the next flush `recordOrdered`'s fast path (`sceneRecs_.empty()`) recorded the batch's scene packets as PLAIN packets: their scene pipelines read the record / ref stream that was never bound = **a GPU page fault at address 0** (`vulkan: device fault: address 0x0 read invalid`), preceded by 807 `scene run dropped: packet without a record` guard lines. Fixes (`d3d9/scene_draws.cpp`, `d3d9/draw.cpp`): (1) `toScenePacket` queues a pending ordered run BEFORE the draw's record joins the list; (2) the fast path refuses any packet with a scene index (`scene packet dropped: its record list was emptied`); (3) `DrawPacket::sceneGen` = the record list's generation (moved by every clear), checked per packet in `sceneFlushRun`. `rvk_d3d9test` sub-case of "owned persistent (F2c-4a)": an opaque replayed scene draw waits in the batch, a run becomes pending, a second replay follows: the picture equals the normal draws and no scene run is dropped. **Its negative control (the old order, run once without validation) reproduced the device loss in the test process** (a TDR on the shared GPU); the A/B switch that made it possible was removed again. After the fixes: GPU-assisted validation a2av (pub_idle + jungle, `focus=1`), w1av (walk), r1av (`rvk_rare`): 0 errors, 0 VUID, no device loss.

**A DIFFERING of the fading herbs (reported by the F12 agent: `owned static turned off: 4 draws DIFFERING`, `[Alpha #81] ... fade 0.988 ... ### 14.24 F12 + F13 result: the sky dome (main view + sky cube faces) and the GUI sprite batches on our own shaders (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a60def423973d188d` on main 5225240; two Debug settings `native.own_sky` / `native.own_gui` = off | on | compare, Full, run time, default off; settings count 155)

**What the four classes draw with today (before this stage).** Of roadmap rows 13 / 14 only two classes still ran translated engine shaders: the **sky dome** (`eCShaderSkyDome`, the shader pool's `ge_skydome_2_0.fx` VS / PS, corpus `vs20_98629e7bbba95f12` / `ps20_38851f343d71da1e`; the main view's sky op and every face of the sky environment cube) and the **GUI sprite batches** (`eCSprite::Flush`'s effect: inline HLSL, technique `Main`, corpus `fx_d6ce5091aa988c66`, `g_mWorldViewProj` + one sampler). The **stars / sun / moon billboards** and the **decals** are `eCShaderDefault` materials, i.e. `ge_default_phong.fx` requests (`debug.material_sources` census: every pool file of the frame is ge_default_phong / ge_default_leafs / ge_particle_2_0 / ge_skydome_2_0): with `debug.ubershader` they already take the HLSL ubershader (S-1a / S-1b; `Device::prepareDraw` gives every draw whose pixel shader has a material source the plain ubershader module or a variant built from it) [I: from the code path; the per-kind `uber` counters only cover scene draws].

**Built.**
* **The ports** (`shaders/post/fx/sky_dome.{vert,frag}.hlsl`, `gui_main.{vert,frag}.hlsl`): generated by `tools/post_port.py` like S-7d's (the gui effect as a new family; the two pool shaders as `SINGLES` from the corpus), same table (`src/native/post_port_table.inl`, 71 rows), same `OwnPost::fxPort` (a `d3d9::Shader` with the engine shader's reflection). Two generator changes for these families only (the S-7d ports are byte-identical, checked): (1) **the vertex shaders keep the translator's shape** (registers as static globals, the program in its own function): DXC propagates the entry's `precise` position back into the program through locals (the dot products became `NoContraction`) but not through a global, so the one-function form gave the sprite VS's clip position **1 ulp** off the translated shader (rel 1.14e-7; S-7d's PSSM VS have the same 1.6e-7) - GUI edges must not move, now 0; (2) **dense stage interfaces** (`rvk_fill<n>` members 0..max): D3D12 links VS / PS by register in declaration order and the sky PS reads VARY0, 1, 3..7, 16 of the VS's 0..7, 16 - the first D3D12 run failed the PSO (`Vertex Shader - Pixel Shader linkage error`, 12 messages; the translated text has the same fillers there, `varyingFillers`).
* **Sky** (`StaticOps::skyOwnBefore / skyOwnAfter`, `static_ops_sky.cpp`; the dome's `sc::DrawDesc::skyDome`): in `StaticOps::issue`, the dome's draw (main view and cube faces: the same composition) gets the ports' shaders (On) and its own-draw check maps them back to the composed ones. Compare: before the composed draw, the target is copied (`OwnPost::scratchOf`: any face / level, the source's VkFormat), the port draws into the copy (render targets 1..3 unbound - the dome blends -, depth / stencil writes off, viewport + scissor as the device has them), and after the composed draw the two are compared (`image 'sky_dome.frag'`, `image 'sky_dome.frag (cube face)'`: a face is copied out first).
* **GUI** (`EffectOps::issueSprite`, `effect_ops_gui.cpp`): every batch of a taken-over flush (HUD, menus, inventory / map / log / character pages, text, cursor, loading screens `gui sprites (view None)`, videos) is drawn with the ports over the natively generated quads (On), lean or not; the scissor rect (the GUI's clip rects) is the device state at Flush's entry and is put back after any target change (D3D9's SetRenderTarget resets it). Compare: the whole flush is drawn first with the ports into a copy of its target, then as composed, then compared (`image 'gui_main.frag'`).
* **Compare hygiene** (both): a pending run of ordered owned draws (F9 / F14) is queued before the copy (it would otherwise be recorded at the compare draw, into the scratch: the one differing sky compare of 8,836 in the first spots run, 81,920 pixels); a compare whose window saw a draw skipped while its shader / pipeline built (`d3d9 stats skippedCompiling / skippedNoShader` + the new `rhi::GameDevice::pipelineSkips()`, D3D12's background PSOs) is dropped and counted (`compares dropped` in `native: own gui (5 s)`; D3D12 menu run: 16 dropped, the cause of 1 differing compare in 327 before); the scratch takes the source's VkFormat (with `own_post=on` a 128 x 128 A8R8G8B8 render target is RGBA8 for S-7b's storage, the cube is BGRA8: the first cube compares were R / B swapped).
* rvk.log `native: own sky (5 s)` / `native: own gui (5 s)` (draws on our shaders / on the engine's with the first reason), `native: stages (5 s)` `own_sky=...` / `own_gui=...` (`scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1` knows both keys); `applyStaticOps` arms them only with their groups taken over (8 + 32, 65536).

**Numbers.**

Owning them needs persistent per-decal vertex / index storage in the mesh arena re-uploaded on change (content hash per decal), a non-op op-record key (admin + record index + material / image / lightmap / illumination) and `orderedPlace` for the decal slot; for 1 - 4.5 decal draws per view (~0.19 ms CPU of composition at the savegame, 15.2) the gain is small. (2) **The sky / GUI stay composed** (per frame, from the engine's state: the dome's registers, the sprite records): only their shaders are ours. A sprite record buffer pulled by our VS (no CPU quads) was not built: the quad bytes are plain copies of the record fields (`fxc::spriteQuad`), so it would save a memcpy, not change a pixel. ### 14.25 Open points of the all-on soak: the last captures, the D3D12 BLAS stall, idle stages in the A/B check, the owned shaders' `failed` bucket (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-ac2e747801ea13732` on main 27ea91b; no new setting, settings count unchanged)

All runs: private copy `risen-test-vk-res`, lock `rvk-res`, Full + `-PlayerDefaults` + the all-on list (`rvk-work/scratch/soak/allon3.txt` = `allon.txt` + `native.owned_instances`), `-Perf -GracefulExit`; rvkcompare spots jungle, harbour_docks, hero_idle, pub_idle with `show_actors=1` unless named; logs + scripts `rvk-work/scratch/res/` (`run.ps1`, `summ.py`). "Before" = the soak of main (`rvk-work/scratch/soak/r3_*`).

**1. The remaining captures.**
* **(a) `motion rigid x instanced mesh` (4 - 232 per 5 s, composed).** Measured: with `native.owned_instances` every render instance of a list is an instanced-mesh draw of the SAME draw key in ObjectMotion (entity = the render context's, the instances share buffers and range), so its "moving" test compared the first instance of this frame with the first of the last; the engine re-sorts the list (`SortRenderInstances`) and the first instance changes with the camera: false motion, a capture of an aliased key (it would have been another instance's capture anyway). `bonePredict` gave such draws nothing (window 2 = an F2f row image: no object-table history; or uploaded while the op's record is built). Now (Full, `native.rigid_motion`): (i) a rigid draw whose VS window 2 is an instance row image has no motion of its own on every path (`Device::rigidRowWindow`; the image is immutable while its record lives, a moved instance remakes the record); (ii) an instanced-mesh draw with an uploaded window 2 goes through `Device::rigidListPredict`: its rows c33..c35 are kept per draw key for the next frame (sorted hashes of every instance, <= 512); rows seen last frame = still; exactly one placement under the key in both frames that moved < 500 units = the bone-motion forms (scene or push form) with a per-frame ring block of the previous rows (`rigidPrev_` -> the record's `prevVs` in `toScenePacket`, `bonePushPrev_` for the push form; the block holds c31, c32 as composed, the previous c33..c35 and c36 = (0, 0, 0, 1u) = the validity word; refused when the shader reads window 2 past c35); a new key, a changed list or a jump = camera motion only. Never a capture. rvk.log `native: rigid motion (5 s)`: `instanced-mesh draws without an object record: row image (still) N, rows seen last frame N, moved (previous rows block) N, camera motion only: new / list changed / refused`. Spots run: 237 k row-image draws, 303 k list draws seen still, 277 moved (held equipment, boats), 122 list changed.
* **(b) retained `skinned` / `morph` spikes (`rt solid for motion`, 114 - 897 per 5 s).** Measured: a skinned packet retained in a frame its part had no bone history yet (its first frame at a spot) or while its forms were building carried no bone form; every replay then showed the RT scene a draw without motion (`boneOfCached` false) -> capture request -> replay refused -> composed, every frame, until the record went away. Now `replayDraw` asks `bonePredict` for such a skinned main solid replay: predicted = the replay is refused once and marked stale (`replayStale_`: the caller makes the packet again from this composition, now with the forms), so it does not repeat (`replays drawn anew for the bone forms`: 2,000 - 3,000 per 5 s at a spot arrival, then 0). `CachedDraw::boneObs` keeps what the composing draw's observers were told (forms declined / pending / rigid still) and the replay and owned paths tell them the same.
* **Census** (`native: captures (5 s)`, requests per run, `summ.py`):

| run | before (soak of main) | after |
|---|---|---|
| Vulkan spots | 5,888 in 11 of 18 windows (instanced mesh 4 - 232, retained skinned + morph 1,710 - 1,794 per window at hero / pub) | **2** in 1 of 28 windows (`rt tree x speedtree`, the first seconds: tree-wind pipeline not built yet) |
| D3D12 spots | 1,390 in 14 of 23 windows | **2** in 1 of 38 windows (the same tree case) |
| Vulkan walk (`rvk_rtwalk`) | 30 - 518 per window | **2** (tree case) |

MISS / DIFFERING 0 in every run. GPU safety: the previous-rows block is a ring block the shader reads only inside c31..c36 (`windowEnd[2] <= 36` required), CPU-guarded (null ring allocation = camera motion only); three runs under GPU-assisted validation (hero_idle + harbour_docks twice, jungle): no error but AMD FSR's known `rw_output` format warnings and, at `vkDestroyDevice`, the leaked-objects report the soak of main has as well (r3_4); the list path drew its moved form only twice under GPU-AV (it acts at spot arrivals: 277 draws in the plain spots run), so its GPU coverage under validation is thin (the block's bounds are checked on the CPU, above).

**2. D3D12 BLAS stall** (soak run 3: `diag: hang` 8.4 s, `rt: scene: slow pass: 2158 ms CPU (96 BLAS ...)`, also 885 / 529 / 532 ms at other spot changes). Measured (spike profile `-CpuProfile`, this build's symbols): not the build kernels (the old slow-pass text guessed that) but **`CreateCommittedResource` per BLAS**: `D3DAccel::allocate` / `createStorage` (build storage and the compacted copy) -> `amdxc64` -> d3d12core `NtGdiDdDDIWaitForSynchronizationObjectFromCpu`: the AMD driver waits on the CPU for each new allocation; a spot change builds and compacts 100 - 300 BLAS. Vulkan suballocates and never had it. Fix: the D3D12 backend suballocates BLAS storage from 64 MiB buffers in the RAYTRACING_ACCELERATION_STRUCTURE state (first-fit, coalescing free list; the span returns through the retire queue = after the frames that may read it; every AS barrier of the backend is a null UAV barrier; larger than a chunk = a buffer of its own; the first chunk is created with the device); TLAS storage stays committed. rvk.log `perf: d3d12 blas arena: N chunks of 64 MiB, X MiB used; run totals: placed / freed / in a buffer of their own`; `-Environment 'RVK_AS_ARENA=0'` = the old behaviour. The slow-pass line now names its parts (upkeep, compactions, BLAS builds, characters / trees, instances + TLAS).

| D3D12 spots run | largest RT passes (ms CPU) | `diag: hang` |
|---|---|---|
| soak of main (r3_3) | 2,158 / 885 / 532 / 529 | 1 (8.4 s) |
| `RVK_AS_ARENA=0`, run 1 / 2 | 722 / 308 / 97; 260 / 107 / 83 | 0 |
| arena, 8 runs (dxon1/2, dx4, dx6 - 9, dx5) | 63; 57; 103; 89; 71; -; 66; **2,711** (dx5) | 0 caused by the RT pass |

The spike profiles of the arena runs no longer show a BLAS creation among the driver waits (what remains: texture / buffer creations of the engine's own loads). One of eight arena runs (dx5, before the slow-pass line named its parts) still had one 2.7 s RT pass at a spot change; three later `-CpuProfile` runs with the new breakdown did not repeat it: **not proven gone**, the next occurrence names its part. The other `diag: hang` lines of these runs are loading screens at the spot teleports (frame intervals of 11 - 22 s with the engine thread in its resource loads, `loading frames not shown`) and one at the graceful exit, not the RT pass. A prewarm build at device creation (the task's first idea) was not needed: the first RT pass of a session is 57 - 89 ms in the arena runs (1,268 ms once without the arena, dx1).

**3. Assert-ArmStages at frozen spots.** `scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1`: `bone_motion` and `tree_wind` are "idle-capable" (their input can be absent from a whole run: nothing animates at a frozen rvkcompare spot / no animated tree in the RT range). An arm that claims them on now also passes when no window says them active, provided every window from the first armed one on says exactly `inactive(armed, nothing owned in the window)` (the windows before it may say `setting on, code off`: `tree_wind` waits for its pipeline). `setting on, code off` after arming, `gated`, `setting off`, a stage that never arms: still failures; every other stage unchanged (all-idle `owned_trees` still fails). `Assert-WindowStages` accepts the same idle state for these two. Checked with 7 synthetic logs (`rvk-work/scratch/res/stagetest.ps1`: idle after code-off, gated, code-off after armed, never armed, trees idle, claimed off, active in some) and the three soak logs: all as expected.

**4. The owned shaders' `failed` bucket** (F15-1 open 2; 13 % of the owned static items). Measured with a new one-line-per-shader log (`native: owned shaders: translated scene form of a covered request: <stage> shader <hash> slot <n> (<file> <entry>), uber slots, kind, pass`, the first 32): every case was a variant built from the bytecode alone before the engine's request (`CompileShaderPoolEllement`, `engine/material_source`) was attached to the shader: F15-1's pipeline keys of earlier runs queue a shader's variants as soon as its base module exists (`prewarmPipelines` on the shader workers, a disk-cache hit takes < 1 ms) and the variant was built translated (`uberSlots` bit not set = `failed`). It appeared only with keys from earlier runs (the first D3D12 run of a copy had 0). Fix: (i) the detour parses the request before the compile and leaves it pending on the thread (`d3d9::setPendingMaterialSource`); `Device::createShader` attaches it to the shader it creates (or finds without one) before anything is queued; (ii) a request that still arrives later (the engine created the shader before it asked the pool for it: 77 - 90 shaders per run) resets the variants built without it (`Device::sourceAttached`, the old modules stay alive as in `resetUberVariants`, retained draws are remade by `variant module arrived since`), and a variant whose build was in flight when the request came is built again by the worker (`late engine requests: N variants rebuilt in flight / M shaders' variants reset` in `native: owned shaders (5 s)`). After: `failed` 0 in every window of the Vulkan and D3D12 runs (vertex and pixel, every kind), 0 lines of the new log. Before (same build without the fix, warm caches): StaticMesh pixel failed 513 - 723 / vertex 3,591 - 6,025, Skinned pixel 684 - 852 per 5 s.

**Gates.** `rvk_tests` 333 / 0; `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded` 169 / 169; `rvk_d3d9test --validation` Vulkan PASS and `--backend d3d12 --validation` (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`) PASS, 0 failures / 0 validation errors; `rvk_rhitest --validation` Vulkan and `--d3d12 --validation` PASS; every game run PASS with `-GracefulExit` exit 0, no dumps; MISS / DIFFERING 0 in every Vulkan and D3D12 all-on run; D3D12 `-Validation` (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`) all-on spots: only the known 25 DSV-format warnings (and the debug layer's live-object list at exit, unnamed objects; the arena chunks are released before it).

**Open.** (1) The list path's moved draws assume one placement per key; a list with several instances of which one moves gets camera motion only (`list changed`). (2) No `rvk_d3d9test` case draws the bone forms (the harness has no G-buffer motion target, 14.15 open 4); the list path ran under GPU-assisted validation in the game.

### 14.26 F11 result: particles, precipitation and strips expanded on the GPU from per-particle records; decal records in persistent buffers (2026-11-11, branch `worktree-agent-a0280216cdd562dcb` on main eec55fe; two Debug settings `native.own_particles` / `native.owned_decals` = off | on | compare, Full, run time, default off; settings count 157)

**What was built.**
* **Particle records, quads by the vertex shader** (`native.own_particles`; `src/native/static_ops_fx.cpp` `particleRecords` / `particleToGpu`, `src/d3d9/particle_gpu.*`, `shaders/include/rvk_particle.hlsli`, `Device::particleVs` / `particleDecl`). A particle op is drawn as ONE instanced draw: instance i = particle record i (0x68 bytes: position + half width, the velocity product + half height, sin / cos of the three angles, the age ratio, 1 - frame fraction, both sprite-frame uv rects, the clamped COLOR1 as the engine's D3DCOLOR, the non-zero-angle mask), vertices 0..3 = its corners in the engine's quad index order (read from the emitter's static IB once: 0 1 2 0 2 3). The op's constants (the inverted active camera's rows + position, the orientation mode, the entity matrix and its SharedBase inverse, the fixed axis, flags (entity coordinates, axis in entity space, pinned corners, normal mode), the pin point, Eo, the pivots, `bCVector::GetUp`) are VS c232..c247 (window 3, unread by every particle shader: refused otherwise). The vertex shader is the engine's own translated particle shader (any ge_particle_2_0 variant: unlit, lit LightingStyle 1 / 2 / 3, one or two textures) with its POSITION / NORMAL / COLOR0 / COLOR1 / TEXCOORD0 / 1 inputs replaced by `rvkParticleExpand` (translator hooks: `extraInputs` = the record's TEXCOORD8..15 on stream 1 at instance rate, `prologue` = the expansion; each input is the field at the offset the engine's own declaration gives it, so a variant reading another input is refused). `rvkParticleExpand` is UpdateVertices 0x5d58b0 written in HLSL with SharedBase's exact rules (re-looked-up: `Normalize` = 1 / sqrtss then 3 multiplies, the cross product's operand order, `TransformDirection` / `bCVector *= bCMatrix` row-vector sums incl. the projective path, `SetToRotation` with CRT sinf / cosf - the record carries SharedBase's own sin / cos: `SetToRotation` about (0, 0, 1) gives m0 = cos, m1 = -sin exactly -, `bCByteAlphaColor` = x 255 truncated, the low byte, no clamp), every operation `precise`: the orientation modes 0..4, the three rotations about R / U / L, the pivots, pinned corners, the facing flip and COLOR0's facing bytes, normal modes 0..3, frame blending. What stays on the CPU: the copy + depth sort of the particle array (the sorted array is the emitter's game state: the lean commit sorts it in place with the engine's DepthSortParticles anyway) and the per-particle scalars (half sizes, sprite tiles, COLOR1, three SharedBase calls per rotated particle). Instanced emitters reuse the master's records (the lean cache holds records + constants).
* **Precipitation** (the late list's eCSkyObject billboards, 0x30-byte records re-done from the drops as before): one instance per drop, `rvkSkyBillboardExpand` (sc::skyBillboardVertices' corner / uv rule) on the material's translated vertex shader; no constants.
* **Strips** (eCStrip_PS): one 0x18-byte record per vertex (position, the accumulated u / v, the segment's alpha factor, the side), the colour lerp A -> B (bCFloatAlphaColor Sub / Multiply / Add, alpha too) and SharedBase's byte rule in `rvkStripExpand` (A / B in c232 / c233); the CPU vertices are still built as the fallback (strips are rare: rvk_rare's titan strip, weapon trails).
* **Light streaks**: nothing to own - the engine draws none (MaxShaderVersion 0, README 16; their geometry is the engine's static buffers anyway).
* **Decal records in persistent buffers** (`native.owned_decals`; `static_ops_nonop.cpp` `composeDecals` / `closeRecord`): RenderDecals' queue, sort, records (material / image / lightmap / light list / outdoor factor) and lock cycles are composed as before, but each record is keyed by what its bytes are made from (every decal's pointer, its vertex / index array pointers and counts, alpha, the dynamic flag and, for a dynamic decal, its entity's world matrix); a known key draws from that record's static VB / IB (vertex 0, record-relative indices) without reading or transforming a vertex; a new / changed key builds the bytes once into a fresh buffer pair (the old pair released at the frame boundary); a rolling content check rebuilds every 30th frame per record and compares (MISS), 2,048 entries, unused 300 frames = evicted. Compare = the per-frame bytes are drawn and checked against the persistent copy every frame.
* **Compare mode** (`own_particles=compare`): the composed CPU-vertex draw is drawn as before; before it, the GPU form is drawn into a scratch copy of the target (`OwnPost::scratchOf` / `compareUnit`, images `particles`, `precipitation`, `strips`), and the C++ twin of the expansion (`d3d9::particle::expand` / `expandStrip`) is compared byte for byte with the engine-rule (SharedBase) vertices.
* rvk.log `native: own particles (5 s)` (ops / quads / record bytes from records, ops on CPU quads with the first reason, the records' or vertices' CPU time, twin check, image compares, precipitation, strips), `native: own particles (5 s): <mode>: N particle draws` (OwnPost scene counts), `native: owned decals (5 s)` (passes + CPU, records drawn from persistent buffers / new / rebuilt, content checks, MISS, entries, evicted, bytes uploaded); `native: stages (5 s)` `own_particles=...` / `owned_decals=...` (`scripts/lib/stage-state.ps1`: both keys, both idle-capable). The particle ops are not composed on the scene ops' compare frames (they compare the CPU vertex bytes).

**Not built / what "owned" means here.** The draws are still issued at the op's place through the composition's DrawDesc (states, samplers, the particle shader pool's registers, the passes): the per-draw state composition is ~1 us per op; making it a persistent DrawRecord (F2c / F14 machinery) needs scene forms of ge_particle and is not built. The particle pixel shaders and the vertex shaders' transform / fog / lighting part stay the engine's translated programs (no hand port of ge_particle_2_0.fx: the corpus holds 2 unlit VS + 3 PS of it; the lit variants appear only in rvk_rare); the geometry part is ours.

**Bugs found on the way.** (1) `StaticOps::newDraw` did not reset `DrawDesc::particleGpu`: after `own_particles` was switched off at run time, a recycled descriptor kept the flag and the CPU vertices were not attached - the torches' flames vanished (toggle run tog2: the off-after-on arm differed in 3.5 % of the pixels, every other arm within the noise); fixed (reset in newDraw), tog3 equal. (2) The compare draw bound the precipitation's 0x30-byte record stream with the particle stride 0x68 (5 % of the pixels off in `precipitation`, an in-buffer over-read); fixed, then max rel 0.

**Numbers.**

| check | result |
|---|---|
| `rvk_d3d9test` "own particles (F11)", Vulkan `--validation` + GPU-assisted, D3D12 `--validation` | 5 ops (orientation modes 0..4, entity coordinates with a rotated entity, axis in entity space, rotations about R / U / L, pins, normal modes) x 4 pictures + 9 rain billboards + an 8-segment strip: **0 pixels differ** from the C++ twin's vertices through the input assembler, 0 validation errors |
| C++ twin vs the engine rule (SharedBase), compare runs | `rvk_spells` 1,134,068 vertices byte-exact (orientations 0 / 1 / 2 / 3), `rvk_rare` 708,256 byte-exact (lit fog emitters, instanced fire emitters, the strip), harbour_night / harbour_docks / beach_night_storm all byte-exact; 0 differ |
| image compares (GPU form vs the composed draw, same target content) | `particles` thousands per run, max 0 steps, worst value rel <= 9.8e-4 in single pixels (GPU sqrt / divide ulps at a corner), 0.0000 % of the pixels off; `precipitation` max rel 0; `strips` max rel 0 |
| decals compare + On content checks | 61,000+ checks (av3 3,574, av4 56,310, rare 1,063, ...), **MISS 0** in every window |
| toggle pictures, one session (harbour_night, all-on, frozen: off / on / off / on, 2 shots each) | same-state pairs mean abs 0.10 - 0.11, cross-state pairs 0.13 - 0.16 = the same as off vs off 300 frames apart (0.14): no visible change |
| all-on spots (jungle, harbour_docks, pub_interior, beach_night_storm, frame generation on) | PASS, MISS / DIFFERING 0, capture census 0 requests in settled windows (2 in the first window: the known tree-wind case), `own_particles=active`, `owned_decals=active`, no error line, graceful exit 0 |
| D3D12 `-Validation` (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`), all-on without frame generation, harbour_night / beach_night_storm / harbour_docks | PASS, graceful exit 0, only the known DSV-format warning (25), `own_particles=active` (up to 9,769 ops / 178,583 quads per 5 s from records), precipitation from records (~3,000 drops per op, 600 ops per 5 s), `owned_decals=active`, MISS / DIFFERING 0 |
| GPU-assisted validation game runs (av1 compare, av2 On + rain, av3 / av4 compare incl. decals, av5 On incl. decals) | 0 error / VUID lines, no device loss |

**CPU** (rvk.log timers of the particle work per op, harbour_night all-on, one session, toggled): engine-rule vertices + sort (off) 4.5 - 5.1 us per op, records + sort (on) 0.86 us per op (78 - 80 ms vs 16.6 ms per 5 s for ~19,500 ops: the per-op saving ~4 us, i.e. 0.1 - 0.2 ms per frame at 30 - 40 ops per view). Decal passes 45 - 50 us (on, every record a hit) vs 60 - 65 us (off) at 1 - 2 records; the queue walk, sort and per-record pass composition stay.

**perf-ab** (`scripts/perf-ab.ps1 -Key native.own_particles -A off -B on -Scenes hold,jungle -Pairs 3`, all-on without frame generation + `owned_instances` / `owned_ordered` / `own_sky` / `own_gui`, player defaults, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, 240-frame windows, every window's stage state matched its arm; `rvk-work/scratch/f11/perfab_particles`): **savegame hold** GPU frame 8.63 -> 8.78 ms (+0.15, z +0.7), CPU engine + HAL 17.65 -> 18.05 ms (+0.39, z +1.2); **jungle** GPU 10.26 -> 10.28 (z 0.0), CPU 18.95 -> 18.26 ms (-0.69, z -0.6): neither resolvable (the machine's other load: CPU frames of 17 - 19 ms against ~8 ms on an idle machine); the expected CPU effect (~0.1 - 0.2 ms) is below the windows' spread. The alpha pass itself (`800x450, 1 colour (97)`) +0.009 / +0.013 ms (z +1.5 / +1.4): the expansion in the vertex shader. GPU otherwise unchanged.

**Left.** (1) The particle draw's per-op state composition (passes, registers, samplers: ~1 us per op) and the engine's translated ge_particle vertex / pixel programs (a hand port like the ubershader's for ge_default is the next step; the GPU-expansion hook is shader-agnostic). (2) The CPU depth sort stays by design (game state). (3) The decal records are keyed by pointers + counts + alpha + the dynamic matrix: an in-place rewrite of a static decal's vertex array would be caught only by the rolling check (every 30th frame per record; never seen: MISS 0). (4) Decal / particle draws are not persistent DrawRecords / ordered owned records (F2c / F14 machinery): they need scene forms of ge_particle / the decal materials' records keyed outside an op list; at 1 - 4.5 decal draws and 6 - 40 particle ops per view the measured gain does not justify it yet.

## 15. Road to a fully owned frame (2026-11-10, census + roadmap; measurement and planning only, no feature code)

User end state: **Full makes no engine-composed calls, has no fallbacks and no vertex captures**: every draw of the frame is collected, skinned, culled and drawn by us
with our own shaders, and the engine's per-frame CPU collection is taken over too (F10, parked in 7.31b / 7.30 as "CPU collection takeover"). This section is the
measured census of what is still missing (15.1 - 15.3) and the staged road to that end state (15.4 - 15.8). Three other agents are building S-5 (tree / grass /
billboard shaders), the no-capture work (actors, rigid object motion, RT characters) and S-7c (post chain) while this was measured: their rows are marked "in progress".

Setup: branch `worktree-agent-aa657641e5ec4cb9c` on main a3f8ef6 (no code change), private copy `risen-test-vk-plan`, instance 61, lock `rvk-plan`; logs, harness and parsers
in `rvk-work/scratch/plan/` (`run.ps1` = the settings below, `table.py` = the census rows, `census.py`, `shaders.py`, `at.py` / `win.py` = window dumps). **Everything on**:
Full + player defaults + the F1 / F2c chain + `owned_static / persistent / shadows / trees / billboards / vegetation / vegetation_ops / actors`, `clusters` + `cluster_cull` +
`cluster_cull_shadows`, `bone_motion=on`, `vertex_pull`, `depth_z=on`, `own_post=on`, `debug.ubershader`, `rt.table_entries`, `lighting.clustered` + `full_lit`,
`upscale.mode=performance`, `upscale.object_motion=all`, `gi.enabled`, `ao.enabled`, `shadows.rt=near`, `framegen.mode=fsr3` (runs c1 / c2 / c3 / c5; the later runs
without frame generation, see 15.1 item 6). Runs: c1 spots (jungle, harbour_docks, monastery_hall, hero_idle, pub_idle with `show_actors=1`, freeze_wait 900), c2 the same
with `native.coverage` (it blocks the packet cache, so its owned stages are inactive: it measures only engine vs ours), c4 control (harbour_docks + pub_idle, `depth_z=off`, no
frame generation), c5 `rvk_spells` (night, spells), c6 `rvk_rare` (every rare kind incl. the crystal lights' point-light atlas), c7 `rvk_rtwalk` (quick-load walk, lod 2x),
c8 savegame hold `-CpuProfile -Perf` (`RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, private copy: absolute ms carry other agents' load). All runs PASS except the two frame generation
stalls of item 6; DIFFERING / MISS 0 in every run, no device loss, no error lines.

### 15.1 Census: who draws what (per main view, 5 s windows; `owned` = persistent records / templates drawn by indirect flushes, the rest is composed by us)

Three levels exist today: (1) **engine's own draws** (the engine's code issues the draw): coverage run c2 = **8 draws of 5,978,608 in the whole run (0.0001 %)**: the
first sky cube face (buffers not created yet: 4), particles "unreadable" (2) and "permutation not compiled" (2). So the frame has no engine-issued draw left after the
first world frames; lean frames skip every slot's engine code (`native: lean (5 s)`), copies in the post chain left to the engine 0. (2) **composed by us** (natively composed
from the engine's op lists every frame, drawn through `prepareDraw` / the packet cache: CPU per op ~4.5 us, 7.29). (3) **owned** (persistent DrawRecords / templates, no

`none` column. (3) The first run in a fresh copy (cold SPIR-V / HLSL caches) composed the first ~90 s with translated decisions until the ubershader modules arrived
(decided `failed` 6 - 25 k per window); they were remade later, no fallback stays.

the GUI module's own logic, the message loop.

### 15.3 What "no composed calls, no fallbacks, no captures" means, concretely

* **No composed calls**: no op of the frame is composed from the engine's registers / pools per frame; every draw is a persistent record (or a GPU-generated one) whose inputs
  arrive as events / table rows. Measured residue: everything in 15.1's "stays composed" list + the 2-in-10 check / warm-up frames (verification by composition must move to a
  Debug compare mode, as `native.picture_check` / `scene_draws_check`: default `native.owned_check=0` in the end state).
* **No fallbacks** (in Full; Wrapper keeps the translated path, 15.8): no "composed late" (pending pipelines / variants: pipelines are prebuilt or the draw waits like the
  D3D12 path's "a draw whose PSO is pending is skipped", never a translated stand-in), no `left to the engine path` class, no "permutation not compiled" composition, no per-
  draw translated shader (bone-motion forms, capture forms, fx_2_0 effect shaders), no stage that turns itself off at run time except on a GPU error (DIFFERING / MISS become
  test gates, not run-time switches).
* **No captures**: no vertex-shader capture variant anywhere (object motion, RT trees / characters, `rt_skin_verify` = Debug only).
* **Collection taken over**: the engine's `ExtractFromFrustum` / `Request` / `RequestRenderOps` do not run for the main and shadow views; the engine's lists are empty;
  its side effects (residency, visible stamp, illumination update, LOD prefetch) are done by us from our visible set.

Independent of each other: F2f / F6-2 / F12 / F13 / F15-1 / FG-1 / F11; serial chains: S-5 -> F4-5 -> F3-4 -> F8-1; F2f -> F9 -> F14 -> F8-1; all -> F10 -> L-1 -> F15-2.

### 15.5 F10: what the collection takeover means concretely

The native collection already exists in compare mode (`src/native/f10_collect.*`, `f10_portal.h`, `f10_math.h`, `RVK_F10_COMPARE=1`): portal walk, sector-tree leaf gate,
live flags, dynamic bounds, fade, LOD pick, hierarchy nodes, exact in 63,689 main views (7.31b), at 207 - 252 us per view against the engine's 405 - 660. What is
missing, in stages:
* **F10-1 own visible set drives the owned classes** (main view): for an element whose every context is owned, the engine's `eCFrustumElementItem::Request` is not called;
  we run its side effects ourselves: the visible stamp `item+0xc = db+8` (decals read it), `UpdateIllumination` when its dirty byte is set (a call into the engine, not a
  copy), `UpdateLoD` (the pick is ported and exact; `PrefetchData` when the wanted LOD is not resident = an engine call), `QueueData(res, 1)` / `CacheResource(res)` for
  the mesh and every sub-mesh material (the LRU: engine calls, at most once per resource and frame), the busy counter and `AddRenderInstance` (gone once F2f owns
  instanced meshes). The op lists then only hold the not-yet-owned classes. Gate: the M3 side-effect digest (`diffSideEffects(engine, native).zero()`) over every view of the
  soaks, on check frames the engine's Request runs and its ops are compared with the records (as `owned_check` today). Removes `RequestRenderOps` / `Request` for owned
  elements (~0.7 ms) and the compaction (`compactOwned` 0.34: no op to take out).
* **F10-2 the cull itself**: `ExtractFromFrustum` skipped for the main view: the native portal walk + the **occluders** (`eCOccluder_PS::AddOccluder` 0x675270, not ported:
  7.31b open (a); until then the engine's final beam is read), the frustum beam built natively (`eCBeamTree::Init` 0x67bc40), the sector tree replaced by the scene table +
  the per-entity cull data cached at cache-in (`OnCacheIn` / `OnUpdateBoundary` events; the actors' bounds per frame from the animation, 7.31b item 3). Later a GPU cull
  of the scene table (F5's cluster cull extended to entities) with the visible set read back for the residency side effects (GD3D11's model: 2-frame lag + margin, so the
  touches of F10-1 come from the read-back; the draws themselves need no CPU visible set).
* **F10-3 shadow views**: the cascades' `ExtractShadowMap` + the shadow dbs' collection (caster callbacks, cylinder per cascade), the terrain map's and the atlas cells'.
* **F10-4 the engine's Execute for the main view**: with empty lists the sorts and slot loops are idle; skip `eCFrustumDatabase::Execute`'s list walk, keep its frame
  bookkeeping (`UpdateFrameDependencies`, the camera, `db+8` frame counter).
* **Staying correct with the game logic**: everything the game decides stays the engine's (entity flags, cache-in / out, animation, particle simulation, scripts' hides /
  shows); we only replace the *query* of that state. Inputs without an event (entity flag word: inline writers, the folded getter 0x852a0; dynamic bounds) are read live per
  candidate as in 7.31b (~2 us per view) or per frame for the ~300 dynamic actors; the scrub and the check frames stay as Debug nets. Risk: residency (a resource that
  is not touched is evicted: the LRU soft limit was never reached at the savegame, 7.29, so a missed touch is silent: the side-effect digest is the gate), streaming bursts
  (a load creates ~1,900 resources), and the engine's own use of the visible stamp (decals, maybe AI / scripts' "is visible": not audited; to be searched before F10-1).

### 15.6 Fallbacks and devices without features

The floor (Vulkan 1.3 + push descriptors) is not enough for Full's owned path: it needs `drawIndirectCount` (optional in 1.2 / 1.3), `shaderDrawParameters`, buffer device
addresses (core), descriptor indexing for the bindless set (core), `storageImageExtendedFormats` (own post's bloom blend), and for X-1 / the captures that remain
`vertexPipelineStoresAndAtomics`; half precision (S-2) is optional with a float build of the same HLSL. Recommendation: **Full becomes a capability tier** ("Full requires",
a `Spec::requirements` set on `renderer.mode=full`, greyed with the reason, as the RT tier); on devices without it the player gets Wrapper. **The translated path stays
for Wrapper** (the faithful mode) and as the developer compare tool (`native.*_compare`, `rvk_shadereq`, `vs_equiv.py`), not as a per-draw fallback inside Full. The
D3D12 backend needs the same list (SM 6.6 + binding tier 3 is already its floor; `ExecuteIndirect` with a count buffer is core). Once F15-2 lands, a Full frame either draws
an item from its record or skips it for that frame (pipeline pending at most during a load), and a check failure is a test failure.

### 15.7 The load-time Full data layout (14.4b) in this order

L-1 comes after the last composed draw of a geometry class is gone (rows 1 - 17), because until then the engine's vertex layouts must stay readable by the composed /
input-assembler forms and a second, packed copy would be an unread or duplicate output. With L-1 the render mode becomes a start-up choice (Wrapper <-> Full in-run A/B
moves to two runs); its first step is the exact float32 position stream for the depth-only passes (z + cascades fetch 17.9 - 19.2 MB of positions per frame of
26.4 - 26.8 MB, 14.12), the packing (16-bit cluster-relative positions, octahedral normals) needs the 680M captures to decide.

### 15.8 Recommended next three stages

1. **F4-5 SpeedTrees owned with the RT scene on** (after or together with S-5's wind port): the largest composed block in every outdoor scene with RT on (0 % owned,
   ~2.5 ms CPU of composition + issue at the savegame, 296 ops / 2,929 instances per view at jungle), and it removes the RT scene's tree captures (25.5 k per 5 s).
2. **F2f + F9 (instance lists, hardware batches, blended terrain layers)**: the static residue (16 % of the owned frames' static ops), small CPU, but it is what every later
   stage (F14, F8-1, F10-1) needs: no class may pin the engine's op creation.
3. **FG-1 + F15-1** (the frame generation stall and the composed-late / pending-pipeline fallback): both block the end-state configuration itself: the all-on run with
   frame generation hangs in 2 of 3 runs, and 65 - 97 ops per view at jungle are still composed because a pipeline or variant was not ready.

**Limits.** One run per scene (5 s windows, frozen spots), the actors' exclusion reasons and the grass split under frame generation not resolved, the CPU profile in a private
copy (other agents' load), the sampler hides entries below ~1.5 % (particles, sky, GUI composition are below it), 680M numbers are the 14.3 model, no new counter was added
(the existing stage lines were enough; the `uber ... failed` reading is proven by the control run c4).
